# Sanming Tonghui · Juan Eight
## A Comprehensive Gathering of the Three Fates

**Attributed to Wan Minying of the Ming dynasty.** From the imperially commissioned *Siku Quanshu*.[^01]

*Reading conventions.* The five groups below cover Jia, Yi, Bing, Ding, and Wu days, each through the twelve double-hours. The six days within each group are the six occurrences of that heavenly stem in the sexagenary cycle. Wood, Fire, Earth, and the other capitalized phase and configuration names retain the terminology of the preceding translations. **〈 〉** marks the source’s small-character material. In the recorded cases, **year / month** supplies the two pillars to be joined to the day and hour immediately above; a sexagenary pair within a biographical comment is not an additional natal pillar. Square brackets mark supplied or repaired readings. Historical assertions about health, death, social standing, and conduct are translated, not endorsed as established facts. The endnotes distinguish the different methods and the uncertain readings.[^02]

## 1. Jia Days, Jiazi Hour
### 六甲日甲子時斷
<!-- source: L14-L26 -->
〈The unfavorable months stated below are to be judged together with the hour.〉[^03]

> A Jia day born at Jiazi hour:  
> Within Defeat, Seal-Ribbon comes, with Officer to generate it.  
> A month communicating with Wood qi is no common thing;  
> Reverse these conditions, and name and profit are empty.

On a Jia day at Jiazi hour, although Jia is defeated at Zi, hidden Gui Water supplies generating qi as Seal-Ribbon, and Officer also generates the Seal. If Ji Earth damages Seal, distinction depends on communication with the month’s qi; otherwise there is refinement without substance.[^04]

**Jiazi day.** This is Zi Remotely Inviting Si. Without Geng, Xin, Shen, or You in year and month, without Chou to bind or Wu to clash, one leaves the ancestral household, establishes oneself, and becomes distinguished. If both year and month are Yin, Shen and You luck brings great wealth followed by financial decline. Zi, Hai, Mao, or Wei in year and month gives distinction in western luck; Jiachen month also gives distinction. In You month, judge Direct Officer alone: great distinction. Si, Wu, or Xu month is ordinary. In Wu month Jia dies, and Zi’s clash is particularly adverse. Yimao and Yisi months indicate death under the law.

<!-- source: L27-L69 -->
〈Recorded cases—**Gengzi / Jimao:** Minister Fan Jizu, banished to frontier service. **Wuzi / Jichou:** an envoy. **Wuwu / Bingchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiwei / Yihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiaxu / Dingmao:** first in the provincial examination. **Jisi / Yihai:** a chancellor. **Jiayin / Jiaxu:** a Junior Guardian. **Dingyou / Renzi:** a bureau director. **Gengyin / Guiwei:** a bureau director. **Jiyou / Dingmao:** Chen Dadao, metropolitan graduate in Bingxu, prefect, a man of Chu. **Wuzi / Xinyou:** an assistant prefect.〉[^05]

All the preceding cases take day and hour as their basis and append the persons’ birth years and months. Where official titles differ, the fates belong to an earlier age. The same applies below.

**Jiayin day.** It arches the noble Xin within Chou. Without Geng, Xin, Shen, You, Chou, or Wei in year and month, there is great distinction. A further Jiayin month brings solitude and injury; only the monk’s or Daoist’s life is suitable. Hai or Zi in year and month gives fourth-rank distinction. In Wu month, northeast luck also gives distinction. In You, Chou, Si, and similar months, Officer or Killings is openly present: with Seal in the pillars, all are distinguished. Mao or Wei makes the body too strong, and injury through punishment remains. Yisi month brings punishment; Dinghai month, a violent death amid flourishing.

〈Recorded cases—**Renyin / Jiachen:** a minister. **Yihai / Bingxu:** a marquis. **Xinhai / Xinchou:** a junior provincial administrator. **Jiaxu / Dingchou:** a capital prefect. **Wuzi / Jiayin:** [Fu] Xingjian, first in the palace examination. **Wuzi / Guihai:** a commander-in-chief. **Jisi / Bingzi:** a censor. **Renyin / Jiachen:** a bureau director. **Gengyin / Wuzi:** a Grand Commandant.〉[^06]

**Jiachen day.** If year and month occupy Water positions, Water floods and Wood floats: roots are moved and leaves exchanged. In Shen month, Killings Star meets Seal and gives distinction. In Zi month, Water-and-Wood luck also gives distinction. You month gives Direct Officer and great distinction; Yin, Wu, and Xu months are all auspicious. Yimao month brings injury through punishment; Guisi month, death in Water or Fire; Guihai month, a violent death.

〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Xinhai:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Gengchen / Wuzi:** a provincial administration commissioner; died in Gengwu year. **Gengzi / Renwu:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Renyin / Renyin:** a courtesan.〉

**Jiawu day.** Hour and day clash, raising concern for wife and children. A month communicating with Wood qi gives manifest distinction. Pure Zi and Wu in year and month, or Hai, Wei, and You months, all give distinction. 〈One account says:〉 high standing. 〈Another:〉 solitary, with wealth, pure distinction, and a name. Yisi month ruins the ancestral estate and shortens life; Yihai month gives self-punishment and death by Blade; Guihai month, a violent death amid flourishing.

〈Recorded cases—**Jiwei / Gengwu:** Academician Li Milun. **Bingxu / Jiawu:** Grand Secretary Zhang Siwei. **Guisi / Gengshen:** Minister Xu Zan, admitted to the Grand Secretariat, from the Central Region. **Wuxu / Renxu:** Minister Chen Qu, from Chu. **Jiawu / Dingmao:** Minister Zhang Mengnan, from the Central Region. **Jiyou / Renshen:** the Ming Tianqi emperor. **Dingyou / Renzi:** a provincial administration commissioner.〉[^07]

**Jiashen day.** Jia at Embryo encounters Seal; Seal transforms Killings into distinction. Repeated Mandarin Ducks make heirs difficult. Southeast luck leaves civil or military office inactive. Hai, Mao, Wei, Chen, Shen, and Chou months all give distinction. Yimao month shortens life; Dingsi month gives death with the body mutilated.

〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Bingzi:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Guiyou / Jiazi:** a Hanlin academician. **Bingshen / Gengyin:** a provincial graduate. **Guihai / Yimao:** a commandery prince. **Guihai / Jiwei:** Chancellor Li Gang. **Jiazi / Yihai:** an academician. **Gengshen / Bingxu:** Zhang Woxu, governor-general and minister, lived beyond eighty; from Northern Zhili.〉

**Jiaxu day.** It arches Hai, Heaven’s Gate, and joins the imperial portals: this is Jia’s Birth ground and must not be judged as Separated Angles. Year and month communicating with the Metal qi of Shen, Si, You, and Chou give great distinction. Wuyin in year or month indicates deafness and inability to speak, or injury by wolves and tigers; Ren appearing makes it auspicious. Yimao month gives death through punishment; Yihai month, death at robbers’ hands. The favorable and unfavorable years and months of these six days must be read flexibly and together. 〈The same applies below.〉

〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Dingchou:** Grand Secretary Xie Qian. **Dingchou / Renyin:** Vice Minister Wang Rang; Reader Sun shares the chart. **Wuchen / Yichou:** a junior provincial administrator. **Jiyou / Yihai:** a judicial reviewer. **Wuxu / Jiayin:** Sun Zong, vice minister of the Court of Imperial Sacrifices, from Zhejiang. **Guimao / Guihai:** a military commissioner, from Zhejiang.〉[^08]

〈The configuration of Jia’s remotely invited Lu:  
White jade rises from sunken mud.  
One morning its appointed course arrives,  
And a noble person lifts it up.〉

> Jiazi meets Jiazi, joined in succession:  
> It promises an immortal plucking cassia in the lunar palace.  
> Chou binds, a clash comes, Officer-ghost is broken:  
> Achievement falters and cannot reach completion.

> Jiazi hour meeting Jiazi:  
> Within, Seal-Ribbon and its tally agree.  
> If Gengshen and Xinyou are encountered,  
> With Chou and Wei added in year and month,  
> Arched nobility lies hidden, then shines greatly;  
> Si and Wu clash and break, leaving ordinary fortunes.  
> If punishment, control, Void, and clash are truly absent,  
> One surely rises above the crowd.[^09]

## 2. Jia Days, Yichou Hour
### 六甲日乙丑時斷
<!-- source: L70-L113 -->
〈The unfavorable months for the six Jia days below follow the preceding rule; judge the hour’s prohibitions together with them.〉

> A Jia day born at Yichou hour:  
> Rob Wealth and Yang Blade should not be present.  
> Fire in the pillars, with Xin Metal carried there,  
> Brings restraint and balanced peace, and distinction lasting long.

On a Jia day at Yichou hour, Xin Metal is Officer and Ji Earth Wealth. The hidden Ji in Chou is seized by the visible Yi. Yichou is Metal Spirit. If year, day, and hour assemble a Fire configuration that restrains it, the disposition is pure and harmonious and the person distinguished. Without Fire, there is violence and cruelty; a Water configuration gives wickedness and injury to the household.

**Jiazi day.** Linked Pearls obtain combination: a worthy wife and noble children. Spring birth makes the body strong but scatters wealth. Summer makes Jia decline, but Metal Spirit is restrained, giving distinction. Autumn gives distinction as a close attendant. Chou month is most auspicious; abundant Hai and Zi are ominous. 〈Recorded cases—**Guihai / Guihai:** a commander-in-chief. **Gengchen / Renwu:** a minister. **Bingzi / Xinchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renxu / Jiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Yichou / Dinghai:** rich but without sons. **Jiwei / Guiyou:** Zhu Guozuo, palace-examination first and Grand Secretary, from Zhejiang.〉

**Jiayin day.** Spring brings poverty; autumn distinction; winter wealth. In summer, Fire restrains Metal Spirit: auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Jiyou:** Vice Minister Wang Ji. **Wuzi / Jiayin:** a vice minister. **Yichou / Renwu:** a vice minister.〉

**Jiachen day.** Substantial wealth and resources. Year and month communicating with Fire qi give distinction. 〈One account says:〉 it indicates “blood.” 〈Recorded cases—**Guihai / Guihai:** Minister Hu Baiquan, an eminent official. **Bingchen / Xinchou:** a chief councillor. **Dingsi / Guichou:** Vice Minister Chen Bangzhan, from Jiangxi.〉[^10]

**Jiawu day.** Metal Spirit enters a Fire configuration. A weak body gives poverty and early death. Yin and Xu assembling Fire is one Wood repeatedly encountering Fire positions: inauspicious. A single Yin, a single Xu, or birth in Shen, You, or Hai month gives fourth- or fifth-rank distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Renwu:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Yimao / Jiashen:** a bureau director. **Jiyou / Guiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Dinghai / Renyin:** a provincial graduate. **Wuxu / Renxu:** Minister Zhao Shiqing; another person with the same chart suffered injury through punishment. **Renxu / Gengxu:** Shi Xueqian, an education commissioner, from Shanxi.〉

**Jiashen day.** Illness and ordinary fortunes. Zi-month birth gives distinction in southern luck. Autumn birth transforms Killings; Seal-Ribbon exposed among the heavenly stems gives still greater distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Renshen:** Chief Censor Wang Yie. **Yiyou / Wuzi:** a commander-in-chief. **Yisi / Yiyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingwu / Wuxu:** a great bandit. **Yisi / Dinghai:** Minister Chen Changzuo.〉

**Jiaxu day.** Fields stretch across the landscape, coin-strings rot, and stored grain grows old; yet early injury is not escaped. Yin in year and month gives father and son distinction together. Zi month with western luck brings gold insignia and purple robes. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Yiyou:** Minister Chen Rui. **Bingxu / Renchen:** Wang Zongmu’s wife; her husband and sons number five metropolitan graduates. **Gengyin / Bingxu:** Governor Wang Sanchong, from Jiangxi. **Jiaxu / Jiaxu:** Grand Secretary Yang Yiqing; no sons. **Dingwei / Wushen:** Zhou Jiamo, Minister of Personnel, from Chu.〉[^11]

〈Chou makes the Metal-Lock configuration;  
Without a key, there is no passage.  
Let Yin, Wu, and Xu appear among the pillars:  
Only Fire’s restraint brings the work to completion.〉

> Rob Wealth and Yang Blade are shunned in the hour’s enclosure;  
> Within the palace, Wealth and Officer have locked the gate.  
> Chen and Wei encountering one another are greatly auspicious;  
> Without them, pass mornings and evenings as you please.

> A Jia day meets Yichou hour;  
> Gold and jade lie stored within the Storehouse.  
> Tianyi Noble Person is injured by Rob Wealth;  
> Clouds veil the bright moon and scatter its light.  
> A Fire configuration, southern luck, brings distinction;  
> Metal Spirit receives fitting restraint.  
> Withered Wood and abundant Water give ordinary fortunes;  
> Leaving ancestors and home, one flourishes late.

## 3. Jia Days, Bingyin Hour
### 六甲日丙寅時斷
<!-- source: L114-L183 -->

> A Jia day born at Bingyin hour:  
> The hour holds the day’s Lu and seats Food God.  
> No flourishing Ren appears, no punishment or breaking:  
> Blessing, longevity, health, and peace—a person rich and noble.

On a Jia day at Bingyin hour, Jia Wood is vigorous at Yin. Bing is Food God, the Longevity Star obtaining its ground. Without Ren to seize or Gui to control, a month communicating with Fire-and-Wood qi gives distinction. Officer Star is avoided, as is Shen clashing with Lu.

**Jiazi day.** The day’s Lu occupies the hour: the road into the blue clouds opens. Without Geng or Xin Metal in year and month, there is distinction; communication with Wood-and-Fire qi gives the highest distinction. Wu month with northeast luck gives fifth- or sixth-rank distinction. Shen month gives Returning Lu Meeting Killings, indicating great authority and rank. A Water configuration is ordinary. Year and month repeating day and hour give great distinction. 〈One account says:〉 a life of migration. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiwei / Renshen:** Chancellor Han Qi. **Xinmao / Jihai:** a chief censor. **Gengshen / Renwu:** a junior provincial administrator; rich. **Bingwu / Guisi:** an administration commissioner. **Jiachen / Gengwu:** a ministry secretary. **Dingsi / Wushen:** a censor. **Yiwei / Yiyou:** a prefect. **Jiayin / Dingmao:** Xu, first in the palace examination. **Wuzi / Yimao:** a provincial graduate. **Gengzi / Jimao:** a censor. **Jiazi / Yihai:** Commander Xu Yiming, from Jiangnan. **Gengyin / Dinghai:** Zhang Cheng, a palace official. **Dingchou / Renzi:** Xie Shaofang, provincial graduate and censor, from Fujian. **Yihai / Xinsi:** Supreme Commander Xiong Wencan, put to death under the law.〉

**Jiayin day.** If year and month lack Si, You, Xin, and Chou, this is Returning Lu, reaching the first or second rank. Pure Wood and Fire with Earth give both wealth and distinction and office among the Six Ministers. Bingzi, Hai, Wei, or Mao month gives fourth- or fifth-rank distinction; southwest luck is most auspicious. Some born in You or Chou month are also distinguished: examine the exposed stems and the year. 〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Dingchou:** Chief Censor Wang Dayong. **Dingwei / Renyin:** Minister Nie Bao, a lecturer on learning; no sons. **Jiwei / Jisi:** a military commissioner. **Wuyin / Bingchen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiayin / Bingyin:** a privy councillor. **Guiwei / Jiwei:** Chief Censor Zhang Lu. **Bingyin / Renchen:** Weng Yuxiang of the Ministry of Personnel, from Jiangnan. **Jiachen / Guiyou:** Hu Xuetian, metropolitan graduate.〉[^12]

**Jiachen day.** Dragon and Tiger Arching the Gate, also called Dragon Singing and Tiger Roaring, indicates distinction; alternatively, lifelong proximity to the noble. Wealth is sometimes gained, sometimes lost; achievement and profit pass through Completion and Incompletion. Wei or Yin in year and month gives office among the Six Ministers. Xu month gives surveillance office; You month, third rank; Zi, Hai, and Chou months, fourth or fifth rank. Western luck gives great distinction. Mao or Si in year and month is ordinary. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 transformation into Wood indicates distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Wuzi:** Sun Sui, styled Duke Zhonglie after death; died in the Prince of Ning’s rebellion. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** Grand Secretary Jin Gui. **Jimao / Guiyou:** a vice minister. **Renshen / Jiyou:** a senior provincial administrator. **Xinyou / Dingyou:** a provincial graduate. **Guiyou / Guihai:** a provincial graduate. **Xinsi / Yiwei:** Cao Dazhang, metropolitan first and palace second, with Bingwu and Guichou examinations named; from Jiangnan. A wealthy merchant shared his chart. **Wuzi / Jiayin:** Zhuang Qixian, palace second, with Bingwu and Guichou examinations named; from Fujian.〉[^13]

**Jiawu day.** The body occupies Extinction ground: ordinary fortunes. Communication with a Water month nourishes Wood and is auspicious. A Fire month shortens life; Companion assistance among the heavenly stems prevents harm. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Gengwu:** a renowned general. **Bingxu / Wuxu:** a commander-in-chief; an ominous outcome. **Renxu / Jiachen:** Remonstrance Official Wang Yaori. **Bingwu / Guisi:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jihai / Bingyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renzi / Jiawu:** a vice minister of a court. **Jiazi / Jiaxu:** an assistant prefect. **Gengxu / Wuyin:** Qian Ruogeng, metropolitan graduate and prefect, imprisoned and spared through his son’s examination distinction. **Dingwei / Bingwu:** poor and short-lived. **Yiyou / Bingxu:** blind.〉

**Jiashen day.** Hour and day clash, threatening wife and children. Identical year, month, and day give great distinction. Si or Hai gives second or third rank. Chen and Zi in year and month assemble Water and transform Killings into Seal: auspicious. Wei month, the Wealth Storehouse, is also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Xinhai:** Wen Jingkui, provincial graduate and chief censor. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** Qiao Yu, Minister of Personnel. **Renzi / Renzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuyin / Guihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renchen / Dingwei:** a prefect. **Bingshen / Gengzi:** a privy councillor. **Bingzi / Dingyou:** Huang Chenghao, serving in the two provincial commissions, from Zhejiang. **Jiazi / Yihai:** the Marquis of Anyuan. **Gengzi / Gengchen:** Hanlin scholar Wu Zhongxing, flogged at court for remonstrating in Dingchou; eight sons, from Jiangnan [additional wording is damaged]. **Dingwei / Jiashen:** a prefect. **Jiayin / Dingchou:** Lu Changgeng [place-of-origin wording incomplete]. **Jisi / Bingyin:** Vice Minister Qian Ce, a southerner.〉[^14]

**Jiaxu day.** Earth in year and month gives wealth; Metal gives reversals. With Metal present, birth must be in the Seal months Hai or Zi to give distinction; other months do not. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Fire-and-Wood transformation gives abundant blessing. Abundant blessing. 〈Recorded cases—**Jichou / Bingzi:** a palace official. **Renchen / Wushen:** Yi Dun; Wealth and Fate possess qi, but Chen and Xu injure one another and Yin and Shen clash, so wealth is followed by poverty. **Jiaxu / Dingchou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Dingyou / Xinhai:** Minister Lin Cong. **Renshen / Renzi:** a senior provincial administrator. **Wuwu / Renxu:** rich. **Guiyou / Yichou:** blind in both eyes; violent death. **Jiazi / Bingyin:** blind in one eye; poor. **Jisi / Bingyin:** Wang Xiangheng, governor of Su, from Shandong. **Gengwu / Renwu:** Yuan Zhongdao, in the southern Personnel administration. **Guiwei / Jiazi:** Shen Weixian, serving in the two provincial commissions. **Jiaxu / Xinwei:** Minister Cui Chengxiu; an ominous end.〉[^15]

〈The Day’s Lu Returning to the Hour:  
Only without Officer is it exceptional.  
Let punishment and clash be wholly absent;  
Do not doubt its wealth and distinction.〉

> Jia and Bing invite one another into the Tiger’s country;  
> Blessing Star seats Lu and brings writing to renown.  
> Luck and the Four Pillars meeting without injury,  
> Sooner or later one rises to the surveillance hall.

> Jiazi at Yin hour encounters Bing;  
> Learning, blessing, and Lu take equal rank.  
> Meeting Chen and Xu gives two or three wives;  
> Lu turning toward the Origin gives wealth and distinction.  
> Ding, Wu, Geng, and Shen diminish blessing;  
> Without Officer to cause entanglement, the pattern is rare.  
> Noble from birth, one finds a person to promote one;  
> This fate begins with difficulty and ends with ease.[^16]

## 4. Jia Days, Dingmao Hour
### 六甲日丁卯時斷
<!-- source: L184-L225 -->

> A Jia day born at Dingmao hour:  
> Hurting Officer and Yang Blade bring real distress.  
> Though the month’s qi offers its support,  
> An unworthy disposition is still not escaped.

On a Jia day at Dingmao hour there are Hurting Officer and Yang Blade. Jia uses Xin as Officer, and Ding injures it; it uses Ji as Wealth, and Yi Wood within Mao robs it. The person is cruel and violent. Xin exposed in the pillars gives Hurting Officer Meeting Officer, with a hundred forms of punishment and harm. Luck is perilous and the end unhappy. But Seven Killings in the pillars combining with Blade, followed by Wealth, Officer, and Seal luck, gives great distinction.[^17]

**Jiazi day.** Harsh, grasping, and miserly; undertakings advance and retreat. Injury to wife and children is not escaped, or death occurs abroad. Chen, Xu, Chou, or Wei month gives distinction; Mao month is ominous, though Officer or Killings in the pillars restraining the excess also makes it auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinwei / Wuxu:** Vice Minister Li Qian. **Dingyou / Gengxu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Bingyin / Yiwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renzi / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengxu / Xinsi:** poor, ill, and short-lived; from Zhejiang.〉

**Jiayin day.** Wood and Fire balanced in year and month give the image of Mutual Illumination and distinction. Chou or Wei as monthly command gives distinction. Yihai month gives writing unsurpassed in the age and third-rank office. Metal and Water complete among stems and branches make both Officer and Seal manifest: distinguished. 〈One account says:〉 Water and Fire at war indicate ominous punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Xinsi:** Minister Tu Dashan. **Bingwu / Bingshen:** a vice minister. **Jiazi / Jiaxu:** immensely rich.〉

**Jiachen day.** Wealth fills the eye and livelihood overflows; a worthy wife and filial children. Judge it as a high fate. Spring birth makes the body too strong: without restraint, poverty, low station, and disability. Chou and You months give distinction. 〈One account says:〉 solitary and ominous. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Guichou:** first in the provincial examination. **Xinmao / Renchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingshen / Dingyou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Dingmao / Jiachen:** a county magistrate. **Wushen / Jiayin:** poor. **Jimao / Bingyin:** disabled.〉

**Jiawu day.** The body sits at Extinction, with ominous punishment. Autumn or winter birth shortens life; spring or summer gives wealth and distinction. Jiawu month gives great distinction. Luck should go northeast. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Bingshen:** a provincial graduate. **Bingwu / Guisi:** a subprefect. **Guiyou / Yiwei:** Hu Zhengmeng, metropolitan first, palace third, and Chancellor of the Imperial Academy, from Zhejiang. **Wuwu / Yimao:** Lin Chun, metropolitan first and ministry bureau director, from Taizhou. **Gengzi / Bingxu:** Minister Lin Jing, from Fujian.〉

**Jiashen day.** Military office, surveillance authority, and distinction. You month with Fire-and-Wood luck gives moderate distinction; Yin and Wu in year and month give great distinction. 〈One account says:〉 Yin and Mao months are wholly ominous. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Dingsi:** a commander-in-chief. **Wuxu / Jiazi:** Vice Minister Liu Dong. **Yihai / Gengchen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Gengwu / Xinhai:** Chief Censor Li Qiu. **Renshen / Jiachen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yimao / Yiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Renyin / Bingwu:** a minister. **Wuwu / Bingchen:** Chancellor Yuwen. **[Gengshen] / Jimao:** violent death. **Jiaxu / Bingyin:** violent death. **Jiawu / Xinwei:** Wang Yingxuan, palace third, from Zhejiang.〉[^18]

**Jiaxu day.** Hai month gives learning, talent, and manifest distinction. Yang Blade is most destructive to the configuration; only restraint or combination makes it auspicious. A weak body is not ominous. If year and month are pure Killings and Jia Wood is born in autumn, early death follows. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Gengxu:** Chief Censor Mao Peng, harm to wife and children, lived only into his forties. **Jiazi / Bingyin:** Wu Qing, palace third; not long-lived. **Gengwu / Jiashen:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈The waning moon [enclosed] by clouds:  
The person has access, but the Way is barred.  
Without rescue in the pillars,  
Wealth and Lu find little ease.〉

> The six Jia days encounter Dingmao:  
> Repeated layers fear clash and punishment.  
> Luck turns its back on Lu, without noble Officer;  
> A lifetime poring over classics wins no name.

> A Jia day approaches Dingmao hour;  
> Hurting Officer and Yang Blade accompany it.  
> Jia meeting Ding Fire turns to ash;  
> Parents and brothers offer little support.  
> Ancestral estate, fields, and wealth gather and disperse;  
> Wife and children all suffer injury.  
> Luck reaching Officer and Killings becomes exceptional;  
> The disposition alternates between anger and delight.[^19]

## 5. Jia Days, Wuchen Hour
### 六甲日戊辰時斷
<!-- source: L226-L275 -->

> A Jia day born at Wuchen hour:  
> Heavenly Wealth sits in the Storehouse and can nourish the body.  
> Rich merchants and great traders have flourishing fields and gardens;  
> Xin Metal in the month completes both name and profit.

On a Jia day at Wuchen hour, Heavenly Wealth sits in the Storehouse. Indirect Wealth upon the hour meets the Dragon guarding the Storehouse, indicating wealth gained in trade and broad fields and gardens. The eighth month carries Lu: Wealth and Officer are both present, giving wealth and distinction together. Companions and Yang Blade seizing Wealth are avoided.

**Jiazi day.** Roots are moved and leaves exchanged; surname and lineage are changed. A worthy wife and filial children: judge a high fate. Wei month with northern luck gives distinction; You month with northern luck, great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yimao / Yiyou:** Tong Chengxu, an official of the heir apparent’s Spring Office. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** a commander-in-chief. **Wuzi / Bingchen:** an imperial relative. **Gengyin / Yiyou:** a senior provincial administrator. **Guiyou / Dingsi:** Geng, a vice censor-in-chief. **Yihai / Yiyou:** also distinguished. **Jiawu / Gengwu:** a palace official.〉[^20]

**Jiayin day.** Dragon Singing and Tiger Roaring gives distinction. Pure Chen gives moderate distinction; pure Yin, health and longevity. Hai–Mao–Wei in Triple Combination, Wealth flourishing and the body strong, gives great distinction. You, Chou, and Shen in year and month are also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Dinghai:** Minister Gao Jian, banished to frontier service. **Yichou / Guiwei:** Chief Censor Lin Yuanfu; his son was likewise distinguished. **Dinghai / Guimao:** a surveillance commissioner. **Yimao / Wuzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengchen / Yichou:** an education commissioner. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renshen / Jiyou:** an assistant prefect. **Jiawu / Guiyou:** Hanlin scholar Zheng Man, suffered the extreme penalty; from Jiangnan. **Gengwu / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jichou / Bingzi:** Jin Guangxian of the Ministry of Personnel.〉

**Jiachen day.** Punishment and clash bring wealth; repeated wives, children late, and injury to both parents. You month gives third- or fourth-rank distinction; Chou month also gives distinction. Pure Chen branches give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingchen / Renchen:** Minister Pan En; three sons were metropolitan graduates. One account gives Jisi hour. **Xinhai / Wuxu:** a prefect; a distinguished son. **Bingzi / Gengzi:** a prefect; an irregularly admitted tribute student shared the chart and was sentenced to military service for fraudulent appropriation. **Yichou / Wuyin:** a bureau director; not long-lived. **Jiayin / Wuchen:** a bureau director. **Bingxu / Gengyin:** a military governor. **Bingxu / Gengxu:** civil distinction. **Bingshen / Xinchou:** military distinction. **Gengwu / Jimao:** Zhang Ruitu, palace third and admitted to the Grand Secretariat; from Fujian. **Renxu / Wushen:** Tao Wangling, metropolitan first, palace third, and mentor to the heir apparent; from Zhejiang.〉[^21]

**Jiawu day.** Much Wealth, success and failure, despondency in youth. Hai, Zi, Chou, Mao, Wu, and Wei in year and month give great distinction; You month also gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Jimao:** Zhang Chen, first in the military palace examination. **Xinwei / Jiawu:** a vice minister. **Jimao / Guiyou:** a prefect. **Renzi / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiyou / Yihai:** a provincial graduate. **Xinchou / Gengzi:** Minister Shu Yinglong. **Jiyou / Renshen:** Hanlin scholar Zou Detuan; Wuxu [event unspecified], removed from office, from Jiangxi. **Xinyou / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiyou / Gengchen:** Mao Dunyuan, palace second; died in Gengshen year, from Zhejiang.〉[^22]

**Jiashen day.** Solitude; monks and Daoists are pure and lofty. Chou month gives wealth and distinction; Yin month gives still greater distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingshen / Xinchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengyin / Jimao:** a metropolitan graduate who rose only to county magistrate. **Xinchou / Jiawu:** a chief councillor. **Gengzi / Renwu:** a provincial graduate and county magistrate.〉

**Jiaxu day.** Great wealth; year and month giving support and combination also bring distinction. Yin or Hai in year and month gives third or fourth rank. But hour and day clash and control: solitary in youth, the person develops blessing in middle life. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Dinghai:** a provincial graduate. **Bingxu / Jiawu:** a provincial graduate. **Guiyou / Dingsi:** Xu Jinfang, salt commissioner of the Two Huai regions, from Fujian. **Guisi / Renxu:** Censor Wang Zhaodui, from Jiangxi. **Yihai / Yiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Renwu / Guichou**, **Dingmao / Bingwu**, **Bingshen / Gengzi**, **Yimao / Guiwei**, and **Jiwei / Jiaxu:** all immensely rich.〉

〈The Storehouse and Money-Dragon configuration:  
Wealth’s gate opens every day.  
When luck travels Officer and Lu’s ground,  
Blessing and emolument arrive of themselves.〉

> Indirect Wealth upon the hour need not be abundant;  
> Search carefully inside and outside the stems and branches.  
> Luck reaching flourishing Wealth brings Officer’s generation;  
> With poor luck and a declining body, fear the grinding of adversity.

> A Jia day encounters Wuchen hour;  
> Wu in the pillars must offer its support.  
> Wealth-and-Officer luck unfolds a favorable plan;  
> Welcome the Money Dragon guarding the Storehouse.  
> Xin and Geng exposed give manifest distinction;  
> Ren and Gui nourish, keeping Wood from withering.  
> Only many Companion and Rob-Wealth brothers are feared:  
> Years and luck encountering them bring calamity.

## 6. Jia Days, Jisi Hour
### 六甲日己巳時斷
<!-- source: L276-L321 -->

> A Jia day born at Jisi hour:  
> Amid Illness, wealth is truly hard to bear.  
> Only a month communicating with Fire qi gives distinction;  
> If the body declines, it cannot withstand the burden.

On a Jia day at Jisi hour, Food flourishes and the body declines: Jia Wood is ill at Si. Though hidden Wu supplies Wealth and Bing supplies Food, without communication with the month’s qi their blessing is hard to sustain. Jia and Ji constitute Level-Head Sha. Spring birth makes the body strong and Wealth weak: relatives are separated like Shen and Shang, and clever undertakings turn clumsy throughout life. Jisi Metal Spirit, restrained by Fire, with Si–You–Chou combined and southern luck, gives a weighty name and high emolument. If no Fire appears, injury to transformed qi brings violence, wickedness, and sudden death.[^23]

**Jiazi day.** Poverty first, wealth afterward; little ancestral property, a diligent wife and stubborn children. Yin, Wei, Si, or Chou in year and month gives distinction, but illness must be guarded against. Shen, Zi, Chen, or Xu gives great distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 transformed qi indicates distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Yimao:** Yue, posthumously Wumu. **Wuyin / Jiwei:** an administration commissioner. **Jiazi / Guiyou:** an assistant prefect. **Guiyou / Dingsi:** a military commandant. **Xinwei / Dingyou:** Chen Xichang, chief supervising secretary. **Xinwei / Dingyou:** Military Commissioner Jie Jingbang.〉

**Jiayin day.** Hour and day punish one another, threatening wife and children. Fire in year and month gives clear judgment and an office commanding troops. Wuzi in year and month brings inherited paternal protection and wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Renwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinwei / Jiawu:** a county assistant magistrate; rich. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** Jie Jingya, a supervising secretary for Personnel.〉

**Jiachen day.** Substantial, generous bearing; lifelong peace and success with wealth. Si, You, or Chou in year and month gives distinction in Fire-and-Metal luck. Read as transformed qi, the judgment is ominous. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Wuzi:** Minister Wan Tang. **Yichou / Gengchen:** Wu Yingchen, probationary Hanlin scholar; an eye disorder. **Renzi / Dingwei:** a provincial graduate. **Renwu / Yisi:** Minister Fang Fengshi; County Assistant Magistrate Zhao Keng shared the chart. Fang came from Chu, Zhao from Ji: Metal Spirit favors Fire and dislikes Water, and this accounts for their difference.〉[^24]

**Jiawu day.** Metal Spirit enters Fire’s country: great distinction. You month with Fire-and-Wood luck gives military authority. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Bingxu:** Minister Lei Li. **Gengzi / Gengchen:** Minister Xu Wen; no sons. **Xinsi / Jiawu:** a prefect. **Renchen / Dingwei:** a military commissioner. **Bingxu / Wuxu:** a chancellor. **Renyin / Jiyou:** Chief Censor Jian Da. **Yiwei / Gengchen:** He Ding, metropolitan graduate and senior provincial administrator.〉

**Jiashen day.** Generous, intelligent, and skilled in decision; solitary, with pure distinction, but breaking and punishment remain. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Guichou:** Vice Minister Chen Lin. **Wuyin / Jimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiyou / Renwu:** distinguished. **Bingchen / Yiwei:** distinguished.〉

**Jiaxu day.** A noble Wealth-Spirit configuration, with both name and profit. Zi or Xu in year and month gives fifth-rank distinction or above. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Jisi:** Vice Minister Liu Ji; not long-lived. **Yihai / Bingxu:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Bingzi / Dingyou:** Zeng Xibing, provincial graduate; the southern circuit.〉

〈The Food-God Combining with Lu configuration:  
Ambition changes the family’s standing.  
Several times success becomes defeat;  
After ruin, splendor returns.〉

> Jia and Ji become Earth’s spirit at the center;  
> Chen or Si in the hour frees them from common dust.  
> Within the configuration, years and luck tend toward blazing Fire:  
> Far-reaching achievement, wealth, and distinction follow.

> A Jia day encounters Jisi hour;  
> Fire approaches thick Earth and loses its light.  
> Parched shoots receive rain, their branches and leaves grow strong;  
> In a Fire configuration, Metal Spirit flourishes and is supported.  
> The metropolitan graduate has a name without substance;  
> The common person changes ancestral estate and homestead.  
> The disposition is no ordinary one;  
> Luck in later years reveals its prospect.[^25]

## 7. Jia Days, Gengwu Hour
### 六甲日庚午時斷
<!-- source: L322-L363 -->

> A Jia day born at Gengwu hour:  
> At Death, a ghost again stands overhead.  
> No Ding or Bing appears, and the birth is weak besides:  
> Hurrying through poverty and hardship, one passes spring and autumn.

On a Jia day at Gengwu hour, a ghost is encountered at Death: Jia Wood dies at Wu, whose head bears Geng as ghost. Without communication with the month’s qi or rescuing assistance, there is illness and a shortened life. Bingyin month makes the body flourish and Geng become extinct: auspicious, yet still a beginning without completion. Communication with Wood qi indicates provincial authority. Communication with Water qi followed by eastern luck reaches only a bureau directorship.

**Jiazi day.** Hour and day clash, threatening wife and children: ordinary fortunes. Zi and Wu in year and month, or birth in Yin or Xu month, followed by northwest luck, brings gold and purple and surveillance office. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Renshen:** a provincial administrator; a vice censor-in-chief shared the chart. **Jiawu / Gengwu:** blind. **Gengwu / Wuyin:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Wuzi / Jiazi:** a registry officer. **Guihai / Dingsi:** a woman whose son Zhou Yanru was first in both metropolitan and palace examinations and entered the Grand Secretariat. **Dingmao / Bingwu:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Jiayin day.** Spring birth gives longevity. Summer gives Hurting Officer with Officer wholly removed, but wealth advances and retreats. Shen gives authority; You reversals. Winter threatens wife and children. Zi and Wei in year and month let Killings assist Seal’s generation: fourth rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Guisi / Renxu:** Minister Lin Ying'ang. **Dingmao / Guichou:** Vice Minister Wu Zhiqing. **Gengwu / Wuyin:** an assistant prefect. **Wuyin / Wuwu:** a provincial graduate. **Guihai / Guihai:** built immense wealth from nothing; from Shanghai. **Jiazi / Renshen:** a provincial graduate; died young. **Jiashen / Yimao:** a palace official. **Renwu / Gengxu:** an assistant in the heir apparent’s household.〉

**Jiachen day.** Enjoyment of fields and gardens. Yin month with Metal luck gives surveillance office. 〈One account says:〉 “blood arising.” 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Yiyou:** a chief administrator; ill and short-lived. **Guihai / Gengshen:** Deng Cheng, probationary Hanlin scholar.〉[^26]

**Jiawu day.** The ancestral estate is ruined, yet wealth and Lu develop. Wealth prevents a good end. Yin, Wu, or Xu in year and month gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinhai / Xinchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renwu / Gengxu:** a chancellor. **Gengyin / Renwu:** distinguished. **Bingyin / Dingyou:** an expositor-in-waiting. **Xinmao / Wuxu:** distinguished.〉

**Jiashen day.** Zi and Chen in year and month assemble Seal; Hai and Mao in year and month strengthen the body: both give distinction. Yin and Xu assemble Hurting Officer to restrain Killings, giving Jia support and distinction as well. Luck favors Metal and Water. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Dingmao:** a senior provincial administrator. **Gengyin / Gengchen:** a vice minister. **Wuwu / Gengshen:** Chen Zhize, a supervising secretary for Personnel.〉

**Jiaxu day.** Birth in Chen or Xu month gives generosity and either distinction or wealth. Chou month with Fire-and-Earth luck gives gold and purple and surveillance office. Yin month gives pure distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jichou / Bingyin:** Tang Gao, palace-examination first in Jiaxu, from Jiangnan. **Wuyin / Dingsi:** a censor. **Jiaxu / Jiaxu:** a provincial graduate. **Renwu / Gengxu:** a provincial graduate. **Gengxu / Wuyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Renyin / Jiachen:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

〈Indirect Officer in day and hour:  
A strong body and restraint raise the pattern high.  
Without breaking or harm in the pillars,  
Fragrant orchids rise above the wild grass.〉

> At Wu hour, Gengshen is Indirect Officer;  
> Fitting restraint makes no ordinary fate.  
> A weak body, strong Killings, and no Food:  
> Plans and hopes meet hardship throughout life.[^27]

> A Jia day encounters Gengwu hour;  
> Yin and Shen in the pillars are welcome.  
> A strong body and slight Killings renew the spirit;  
> Parents and the ranks of brothers lack accord.  
> Wife and children suffer injury in early years;  
> In later life, one rises above the crowd.  
> Life turns and overturns, delighting in reversal:  
> A fate of ruin first and achievement afterward.

## 8. Jia Days, Xinwei Hour
### 六甲日辛未時斷
<!-- source: L364-L405 -->

> A Jia day born at Xinwei hour:  
> Officer Star sits upon Noble, a most exceptional meeting.  
> A month encountering Metal qi must bring honor and distinction;  
> With Wealth and Lu balanced, one may venture that judgment.

On a Jia day at Xinwei hour, the hour’s Noble encounters Officer. Jia takes Xin as Officer, and Wei contains Tianyi Noble Person. Ji is Wealth, and Ji Earth within Wei possesses qi. A month communicating with Wood qi, giving support, brings wealth; a month communicating with Earth qi brings both wealth and distinction.

**Jiazi day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, Wei, Si, and You months—the ground of Earth and Metal—bring manifest civil distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Bingxu:** Tu, Minister of Personnel. **Jisi / Wuchen:** an assistant prefectural judge. **Jichou / Wuchen:** an assistant subprefectural judge. **Gengxu / Dinghai:** a Junior Guardian. **Wushen / Xinyou:** Feng Mengzheng, metropolitan first and vice minister. **Guihai / Xinyou:** a provincial graduate and ministry secretary. **Renxu / Jiyou:** Grand Secretary Fang Congzhe.〉

**Jiayin day.** Yin and Shen months give distinction; pure You and Chou in year and month give great distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Metal transforming into Wood indicates distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yimao / Wuyin:** a chief court minister. **Yiyou / Bingxu:** a prefect. **Wuyin / Dingsi:** a vice minister. **Xinsi / Bingshen:** a provincial graduate. **Yisi / Wuzi:** a provincial graduate. **Wuyin / Gengshen:** Privy Councillor Wang.〉[^28]

**Jiachen day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give wealth; Si, You, Chou, or Zi in year and month gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiwei / Jiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Wuzi / Wuwu:** a woman whose son Weng Zhengchun was palace-examination first and a minister. **Gengwu / Jimao:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jiawu day.** Spring is auspicious, summer ominous. Autumn makes the body weak and unable to sustain Officer and Lu; winter gives distinction. 〈One account says:〉 high standing. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Guiyou:** a bureau director. **Guisi / Jiwei:** a bureau director. **Jiachen / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jiashen day.** Spring is auspicious; summer brings hardship; autumn manifest distinction. In winter, a new foundation is established and distinction attained. Zi and Chou months give great distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Metal transforming into Wood indicates distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingxu / Gengzi:** Minister Wang Shizhen. **Bingshen / Jiawu:** a provincial graduate. **Bingshen / Jiawu:** Lin Minyue, serving in the two provincial commissions.〉

**Jiaxu day.** Injury first, distinction afterward. Yin, Mao, You, or Chou in year and month gives distinction; Zi and Shen in year and month bring manifest high rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Jichou / Yihai:** distinguished. **Jihai / Dingchou:** Huang Fengxiang, palace second and Minister of Rites. **Jihai / Jisi:** a metropolitan graduate who became vice minister.〉[^29]

〈Wealth and Officer Opening the Storehouse:  
Meeting danger, one escapes calamity.  
A noble person adds assistance:  
Do not doubt the wealth and distinction.〉

> Jia encountering Xinwei finds Wealth and Officer;  
> The level road into the blue clouds is not difficult.  
> It is not like a molting fowl turning into a phoenix:  
> In season, it flies to the summit of colored clouds.[^30]

> A Jia day encounters Xinwei hour;  
> Wealth and Officer guard the Storehouse and support each other.  
> Noble Person, Wealth, and Lu form a favorable design;  
> Early hardship ends in splendor and riches.  
> The gentleman advances in office and rank;  
> The common person gains substance and abundance.  
> Without punishment, clash, breaking, or harm in the pillars,  
> The road into the blue clouds is assured.

## 9. Jia Days, Renshen Hour
### 六甲日申時斷
<!-- source: L406-L443 -->

> A Jia day born at Renshen hour:  
> Visible Hurting Officer, hidden ghost, seated upon the body.  
> No Bing or Wu in the pillars, autumn or winter flourishing:  
> A life of difficulty and drifting, with no settled place.[^31]

On a Jia day at Renshen hour, Jia Wood is extinguished at Shen; Ren Water is born there and Geng Metal comes to Lu. Visible Owl and hidden ghost: a flourishing Jia transforms ghost into Officer, yet cannot escape violence. Autumn strengthens Geng, winter strengthens Ren. Without Bing and [Wu] in the pillars to restrain them, the image is drifting. Si and Wu months are greatly auspicious, with an overbearing disposition. Geng exposed is judged as Killings; northern luck gives distinction.

**Jiazi day.** Shen, Zi, Chen, and Hai months give flooding Water and floating Wood, roots moved and leaves exchanged. There is distinction in the Jade Hall and Golden Horse precincts. Water-and-Earth luck is ominous. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Wuyin:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Renyin / Wushen:** minor distinction. **Yimao / Bingxu:** a military commissioner.〉

**Jiayin day.** Loss amid flourishing. Chen, Xu, Chou, or Wei month gives Gouchen Obtaining Its Place; Yin, Wu, or Xu month gives restrained Indirect Officer. Autumn birth followed by southeast luck likewise gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingsi / Jiyou:** Minister Xie Yuanming. **Dingyou / Jiyou:** a minister. **Gengwu / Xinsi:** a chancellor. **Bingwu / Jihai:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Jiachen day.** Yin and Chen in year and month bring literary renown and distinction; Bing and [Wu] exposed are especially fine. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Bingzi:** a chancellor. **Guiwei / Guihai:** Wang Ting, Censor-in-Chief of the Left. **Jisi / Renshen:** an assistant prefect. **Guihai / Jiayin:** Sun Chengzong, palace third, commander of the armies and Grand Secretary. **Jiaxu / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jiawu day.** Shen, Zi, and Chen months indicate a change of surname and lineage and a substantial, generous fate. Wu month gives distinction. 〈One account says:〉 high standing. 〈Recorded cases—**Yimao / Renwu:** chief minister of the Court of Imperial Entertainments. **Wuxu / Jiayin:** an assistant prefectural judge. **Jiachen / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate. **Renshen / Bingwu:** a chancellor.〉

**Jiashen day.** Yin month balances body and Killings; Mao month combines Blade with Killings: both give distinction. Zi and Chen in year and month transform Killings into Seal; the Fire months Si and Wu restrain Seven Killings: all are auspicious. A strong Killings and weak body is most feared and greatly ominous. 〈One account says:〉 one leaves home and develops blessing. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Wuzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jichou / Dingchou:** a judicial reviewer. **Yisi / Wuyin:** a chief councillor. **Gengwu / Xinsi:** a chancellor. **Dingyou / Guimao:** distinguished. **Gengyin / Gengchen:** rich. **Gengshen / Jiashen:** a thief. **Dinghai / Renzi:** “killing.” **Xinsi / Gengzi:** Minister Zeng Ying.〉[^32]

**Jiaxu day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months bring brocade robes and achievement. Hai month gives the Hall of Learning, Yin month Established Lu: both give distinction. Wu and You months shorten life; otherwise poverty and low station. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Xinhai:** a chancellor. **Jisi / Wuchen:** an associate prefect, sent to frontier exile. **Jichou / Jiaxu:** great distinction.〉

〈The configuration of Lu Obtained in a Dream:  
On waking, nothing remains to reckon.  
Add punishment, Void, or control,  
And lifelong cares keep the heart occupied.〉

> A Jia day gladly encounters Shen in the hour;  
> Indirect Officer and Indirect Seal fear punishment and clash.  
> Name and profit are finally hard to secure;  
> With rescue, luck must be allowed its passage.

> A Jia day encounters Renshen hour;  
> Inverted Food and hidden ghost encroach together.  
> A strong body at birth indicates flourishing honor;  
> A weak body gives an unsettled nature.  
> The ranks of brothers and the Six Relations offer little help;  
> Plans are achieved through one’s own establishment.  
> Luck traveling auspicious ground brings a famous name;  
> Weak luck leaves an ordinary fate.

## 10. Jia Days, Guiyou Hour
### 六甲日酉時斷
<!-- source: L444-L479 -->

> A Jia day born at Guiyou hour:  
> Hidden Officer and visible Seal are not yet exceptional.  
> Fire in the pillars, no punishment or breaking,  
> And Embryo generating the original fate reveal distinction.

On a Jia day at Guiyou hour, Embryo generates the original fate. Jia Wood receives Embryo at You, supplying Jia’s generating qi. Visible Gui is Seal, hidden Xin Officer. Ji Earth damaging Seal prevents distinction. You is Metal Spirit. With Yin and Xu in the pillars communicating with Fire qi, the nature is pure and substantial and the person distinguished. Without Fire, the appearance of Water gives violence and disability.[^33]

**Jiazi day.** Spring birth strengthens Wood; You month makes Officer pure and gives distinction. If Killings mingles with it, or Killings is numerous and the pillars entirely lack Fire qi, the result is ominous. 〈One account says:〉 the ancestral estate is first lost, followed by great wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Jiazi:** a ministry secretary; died young. **Xinsi / Bingshen:** distinguished. **Jiashen / Renshen:** a thief, punished. **Xinsi / Gengzi:** a transmission commissioner.〉

**Jiayin day.** Spring birth gives longevity; summer reversals; autumn an unsettled nature and many ominous outcomes; winter ordinary fortunes. Chou and Wei months give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Xinwei:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Renshen / Guichou:** a minister. **Guihai / Xinyou:** a palace official. **Gengshen / Yiyou:** violent death; Officer and Killings both strong, without Fire restraint in the pillars. **Wuchen / Renxu:** Gong Sanyi, provincial first, metropolitan graduate, and Hanlin scholar.〉

**Jiachen day.** Zi and Xu in year and month provide both Wealth and Officer: distinguished. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Jihai:** a chancellor. **Wushen / Xinyou:** an education commissioner. **Wuwu / Xinyou:** a county magistrate.〉

**Jiawu day.** Solitude. Birth in Yin, Wu, or Xu month, followed by northeast luck, gives a bureau directorship. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Guiwei:** a provincial graduate. **Renyin / Wushen:** an assistant prefect. **Gengxu / Wuzi:** a prefect. **Xinchou / Xinmao:** a provincial graduate and ministry bureau director.〉

**Jiashen day.** Ordinary fortunes. A month communicating with Fire qi, followed by southern luck, gives wealth and distinction. Shen and You in year and month often shorten life. If Water transforms Metal’s poison, judge Officer and Seal rather than Metal Spirit; this is also auspicious, but indicates early retirement. 〈Recorded cases—**Guichou / Renxu:** an administration commissioner. **Guiyou / Renxu:** a prefect. **Wushen / Guihai:** an assistant bureau director. **Gengwu / Jimao:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Jihai / Wuchen:** a remonstrance official. **Yiwei / Bingxu:** Li Zhi, Minister of Personnel; retired in Jiwei year, from Zhejiang. Another person sharing the chart was immensely rich.〉[^34]

**Jiaxu day.** Zi and Xu in year and month give literary distinction. Zi and Wu months give either distinction or wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiwei / Jiaxu:** a chief court minister. **Xinmao / Wuxu:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈The Fowl Becoming an Azure Luan:  
Before its meeting, crows bully it.  
One morning its wings are complete,  
And all four seas lie open to its flight.〉

> A Jia day communicates with Guiyou hour;  
> Metal Spirit and a Fire configuration suit one another.  
> Luck travels south without punishment or breaking:  
> Wealth, distinction, and splendor—everything exceptional.

> A Jia day encounters Guiyou hour;  
> The person has both wealth and distinction.  
> The Three Wonders develop blessing and repeated promotion;  
> Above and below agree in manifest nobility.  
> A gentleman from humble gates becomes minister or general;  
> A common person establishes fields and gardens.  
> Uninjured and unbroken, this is an outstanding worthy;  
> The fate surely reaches the censorial offices.

## 11. Jia Days, Jiaxu Hour
### 六甲日戌時斷
<!-- source: L480-L525 -->

> A Jia day born at Jiaxu hour:  
> Wood meets a Fire configuration; its qi finds no release.  
> Good-hearted, with ordinary blessing,  
> One truly must lament injury to both parents.

On a Jia day at Jiaxu hour, Jia uses Bing as Food and Xin as Officer. At Xu, Food God enters the Fire configuration while Xin retains residual qi. The body is burned by Fire. The person loves goodness but has ordinary subsistence. Jia takes Wu as father and Gui as mother: flourishing Jia at Xu injures Wu, while hidden Wu within Xu injures Gui. Wu and Gui both controlled, the two parents are difficult to preserve.

**Jiazi day.** Spring gives longevity; summer violence; autumn distinction; winter a change of roots and leaves. Pure Hai in the pillars gives Separated Angles: though distinguished, the person ends ominously. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer and are also auspicious. 〈One account says:〉 deafness and inability to speak, head sores, or injury by dogs, wolves, and tigers. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinhai / Xinchou:** Shao Kangjie. **Dinghai / Xinhai:** Prefectural Judge Luan Shangyue; no sons, violent death implicating seventeen lives. **Gengzi / Jimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengxu / Jiashen:** Chief Censor Deng Tingzan; no sons. **Xinmao / Xinmao:** a provincial graduate. **Yisi / Yiyou:** distinguished. **Yichou / Yiyou:** distinguished. **Xinmao / Gengyin:** Chen Younian, Minister of Personnel.〉

**Jiayin day.** Companions contend for Lu, and Wood qi is burned. After forty, fortunes gradually decline. Jia and Bing, Shen and Zi, in year and month give great distinction. Pure Xu gives surveillance office. Wu month with Water-and-Fire luck gives seventh- or eighth-rank distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Yihai:** a remonstrance official. **Jiazi / Yihai:** a county magistrate. **Bingzi / Gengzi:** Gu Dazhang, bureau director in the Ministry of Justice; died in the Imperial Guard’s prison. **Guiwei / Guihai:** a marquis. **Guisi / Bingchen:** Vice Minister Shen Jiefu.〉

**Jiachen day.** Steady and substantial resources, but many ominous outcomes. Spring birth with Metal-and-Fire luck reaches sixth-rank office. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiwei / Yihai:** a senior provincial administrator. **Bingxu / Gengyin:** distinguished. **Guiyou / Jiwei:** minor distinction.〉

**Jiawu day.** Spring birth receives a noble person’s support. Summer turns its back on Lu and drives away Horse. Winter’s Seal-Ribbon is auspicious. Pure Yin in year and month gives distinction as a close attendant. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuwu / Wuwu:** a chief councillor. **Renzi / Renzi:** a county magistrate. **Xinsi / Jihai:** intelligent and versatile, but ruined the household and wandered.〉

**Jiashen day.** It flanks noble Officer at You, but the person is solitary and development does not last. Spring birth with Wood-and-Earth luck gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Guihai:** a vice minister. **Dingyou / Guimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinsi / Xinmao:** distinguished. **Jiachen / Xinwei:** a bureau director. **Jiashen / Jiaxu:** death by hanging. **Bingshen / Renchen:** Shi Kezhang, metropolitan graduate. **Gengchen / Guiwei:** a provincial graduate. **Xinsi / Xinmao:** Minister Lu Xianzu. **Xinhai / Renchen:** Minister Tu Zongjun. **Bingyin / Xinchou:** Academician Yong Rupan [name uncertain].〉[^35]

**Jiaxu day.** Turning the Back on Lu and Driving Away Horse: ordinary fortunes. Autumn birth gives Officer and Killings qi and brings distinction. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months are auspicious; Mao month ominous. If year and month are Bingyin and Jiawu, three Jia take two Bing as Food; Bing has the summer season and Birth at Yin. Jia approaches Food and encounters Lu, giving wealth and distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Jiashen:** Chief Censor Liu Wenzhuang. **Dingyou / Wushen:** Chief Censor Zhang Qin. **Renshen / Jiyou:** Minister Dai Jing’an. **Yisi / Yiyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yimao / Jiashen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuchen / Jiwei:** a provincial graduate. **Jiazi / Jiaxu:** distinguished. **Wuyin / Dingsi:** Zhuang Jichang, first in both metropolitan and palace examinations. **Jiaxu / Jiaxu:** a member of the imperial clan.〉

〈The Fire-Tomb configuration at Xu hour:  
Hearts and purposes do not agree.  
Wealth and Officer alike turn away;  
Office and Lu find passage through luck.〉

> Jiaxu in the hour encounters a Companion;  
> Heavenly Lu is in the Storehouse, stirred by Fire qi.  
> Fowl and ducks cry together; all gather and disperse;  
> In the end, their hearts and purposes are not the same.

> A Jia day communicates with Jiaxu hour;  
> A Companion carrying Lu is encountered.  
> Heavenly Solitude and the Storehouse lie hidden within;  
> You, Chou, and Chen branches supply the useful influence.  
> Without a key, clash and punishment open and break it;  
> One establishes oneself, learns much, and achieves little.  
> Metal, Wood, and Fire flourish; Water is born in the pillars:  
> A fate dark at first and bright afterward.[^36]

## 12. Jia Days, Yihai Hour
### 六甲日乙亥時斷
<!-- source: L526-L583 -->

> A Jia day born at Yihai hour:  
> Yang Blade turns to injury and brings calamity.  
> If Xin and Wu, Officer and Wealth, are not encountered,  
> One fears that achievement will find no prosperous course.

On a Jia day at Yihai hour, Jia Wood is born at Hai. Flourishing Yi is Blade, controlling and injuring the Hall of Learning. Ren is Inverted Food and comes to Lu at Hai. Jia uses Metal as Officer and Wu and Ji as Wealth; Xin Metal is at Bath, Wu and Ji declining or extinguished, and cannot produce blessing. Birth in Si, You, or Chou month, with Xin seen and Wu in the pillars, gives distinction. Otherwise, intelligence does not secure achievement: the person practices an art or craft.

**Jiazi day.** This forms Approaching Qian and gives distinction. Shen month strengthens Killings and combines it with Blade, giving authority and rank. You month gives Direct Officer; a little Earth assistance in the pillars brings great distinction. Chen, Si, Chou, Wei, and Xu months are all auspicious. Pure Mao in year and month makes Blade flourish and is ominous. 〈One account says:〉 wealth obtained through the wife, but injury to her. 〈Another:〉 frequent eye disease and ordinary resources. 〈Recorded cases—**Yimao / Yiyou:** a chancellor. **Jisi / Yihai:** a chancellor. **Wuchen / Guihai:** a marquis or earl. **Gengchen / Xinhai:** a vice minister. **Jihai / Guiyou:** Vice Minister Xu Jin. **Wushen / Renxu:** Hanlin Compiler Huang Yi. **Xinyou / Wuxu:** a prefect. **Jisi / Bingyin:** a subprefect. **Guihai / Guihai:** Hu Song, Minister of Personnel. **Wushen / Yichou:** Liu Yingqiu, provincial first, palace third, and Chancellor of the Imperial Academy. **Guichou / Yichou:** Vice Minister Bai Yu. **Xinsi / Gengzi:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jiayin day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give wealth; Shen and You distinction. Winter is ordinary, but Yin and Hai months give high distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Xinhai:** a privy councillor. **Gengxu / Yiyou:** a bureau director. **Guiyou / Dingsi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinyou / Gengyin:** a county magistrate. **Dingsi / Wushen:** Hanlin scholar Wang Tu, Vice Minister of Personnel. **Jiaxu / Yihai:** Commander-in-Chief Man Gui, killed in battle. **Gengshen / Jimao:** manifest distinction.〉

**Jiachen day.** You month gives Direct Officer and the greatest distinction. Chen, Xu, Chou, Wei, Yin, and Hai in year and month are all auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinchou / Gengyin:** a chancellor. **Dingyou / Yisi:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Xinwei / Renchen:** Minister Song Shen. **Dingyou / Jiyou:** Vice Minister Lu Shen. **Yiwei / Jimao:** a transmission commissioner. **Renyin / Bingwu:** a bureau director. **Xinhai / Gengzi:** a censor. **Gengxu / Dinghai:** a county magistrate. **Xinhai / Jiawu:** an earl. **Yisi / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuwu / Dingsi:** Envoy Hong Fusheng. **Yiwei / Yiyou:** Shen Shixing, palace first and admitted to the Grand Secretariat; from Jiangnan. Another person sharing the chart had ordinary fortunes.〉

**Jiawu day.** Mao month brings Yang Blade and punishment among relatives; with a weak body, the end is unhappy. Spring birth gives the distinction of a chief minister. Shen, Zi, Xu, or Wu in year and month, followed by Water-and-Fire luck, reaches office among the Six Ministers. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Gengzi:** Minister Wang Yue, enfeoffed Earl of Weining. **Bingyin / Wuxu:** Wang Hua, palace first and minister; another account gives Bingshen as birth year. **Yihai / Renwu:** a censor. **Jiyou / Jisi:** a prefect. **Jihai / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate. **Jiaxu / Gengwu:** a provincial graduate. **Bingxu / Jiawu:** a provincial graduate and deputy surveillance commissioner.〉

**Jiashen day.** Hai month gives accomplished learning and surveillance distinction. Shen and You months give poverty followed by wealth. Zi month with Water-and-Fire luck gives gold and purple. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer; Yin gives Established Lu: all are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinmao / Renchen:** Grand Secretary Li Shi. **Guihai / Gengshen:** Minister Feng Tianyu; no sons. **Jiayin / Xinwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guichou / Jiayin:** a chief administrator. **Renshen / Gengxu:** a county magistrate. **Jiaxu / Bingyin:** a county magistrate. **Guiyou / Renxu:** a provincial graduate. **Jiachen / Dingchou:** a chancellor. **Xinyou / Jihai:** sought a livelihood through dependence in distant places.〉

**Jiaxu day.** Spring and winter birth give wealth; in regions of substantial Earth, manifest distinction. Summer gives toil without accumulated wealth; autumn is ordinary. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Guichou:** Chief Censor Liu Yu. **Guichou / Guihai:** Remonstrance Official Zhang Da. **Yihai / Gengchen:** an erudite. **Gengzi / Wuyin:** Cai, palace-examination first. **Jiazi / Bingyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuxu / Wuwu:** Assistant Surveillance Commissioner Zou Dehan. **Xinyou / Renchen:** Sun Sheng, palace second; four sons succeeded in the examinations, from Zhejiang. **Guichou / Yichou:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈Traveling Horse at Heaven’s Court:  
Wealth and Officer both [take precedence].  
East and west must satisfy the heart;  
North and south are naturally at peace.〉[^37]

> A Jia day meeting Yihai hour is strong;  
> With Officer and Seal, it is no common fate.  
> The time arrives, and an eminent person recommends it;  
> Luck reaches Wealth’s country, and distinction spreads abroad.

> A Jia day encounters Yihai hour;  
> Within it, Ren Water gives generation.  
> The hour approaches the Imperial Seat, the Purple Tenuity Palace;  
> Adopted heirs prove useful.  
> Parents and the ranks of brothers give little support;  
> When flowers bloom and fruit forms, guard against the wind.  
> Literary distinction changes the family’s standing;  
> The course arrives, and one rises above the crowd.[^38]

## 13. Yi Days, Bingzi Hour
### 六乙日丙子時斷
<!-- source: L584-L629 -->

> A Yi day born at Bingzi hour:  
> Hurting Officer sits on Noble, yet blessing is incomplete.  
> No Officer, punishment, or breaking in the pillars:  
> Only then is lifelong noble Lu secured.

On a Yi day at Bingzi hour there is Rat Nobility of the Six Yi. Yi uses Geng as Officer, which dies at Zi; Bing is Hurting Officer. Bing secretly invites Xin, transforming Killings into authority. Without Geng or Xin in the pillars, without Chou binding or Wu clashing, the noble configuration is completed. With these prohibitions offended, or without communication with the month’s qi and without rescue, there is violence, poverty, low station, illness, and a shortened life.

**Yichou day.** A weak body and ordinary fortunes. Without Wu’s piercing clash, the person is worthy, virtuous, warm, and substantial. Shen or You in year and month gives surveillance office; Yin or Zi gives manifest distinction. Bingyin, Jiwei, Jiaxu, and Jichou months are avoided: ominous punishment and violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Bingyin:** Minister Chen Jun. **Guimao / Xinyou:** Vice Minister Yu Shouyu. **Jiyou / Bingyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiwei / Jiazi:** a transmission commissioner. **Yiyou / Wuyin:** a bureau director. **Yihai / Wuzi:** an assistant subprefectural judge. **Gengzi / Jichou:** a chief councillor. **Xinchou / Jihai:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Dingchou / Bingwu:** Minister Zhang Fengxiang.〉

**Yimao day.** High standing. Chen, Xu, Chou, Wei, or Hai in year and month, with no Officer Star, gives distinction. 〈One account says:〉 good fortune is met within misfortune. Dingsi month is avoided: ominous ruin of the ancestral estate. Wushen month gives death with the body mutilated; Jiyou month a violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Bingzi:** a vice minister. **Dingmao / Guimao:** a vice minister. **Dingyou / Guichou:** a chief censor. **Dingsi / Renyin:** a minister. **Jimao / Jichou:** a vice minister. **Yisi / Guiwei:** a vice minister. **Renyin / Guimao:** an imperial relative. **Bingxu / Wuxu:** a chancellor. **Guimao / Jiayin:** a Grand Preceptor. **Dingsi / Gengxu:** vice chancellor of the Imperial Academy. **Jiachen / Xinwei:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** a prefect. **Guisi / Jiayin:** a bureau director. **Gengwu / Bingxu:** an associate prefect. **Jiaxu / Dingmao:** Hanlin scholar Chen Yuanhui. **Jichou / Dingmao:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Yisi day.** Auspicious. Si and Wu in year and month shorten life; otherwise solitude and toil. Pure Earth makes Wealth flourish and generate Officer: western luck gives distinction. Zi and Chen with Water-and-Fire luck likewise give second or third rank. Shen month gives Direct Officer, You Indirect Officer, Mao Established Lu: all are auspicious. Jiayin month brings punishment and early death; Yiyou month low station. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiayin / Renshen:** a capital prefect. **Jiaxu / Yihai:** a capital prefect. **Jisi / Bingyin:** a vice minister. **Wushen / Xinyou:** a chancellor. **Dingsi / Guimao:** a minister. **Wuchen / Guihai:** an administration commissioner. **Jiazi / Dingmao:** a county magistrate. **Renshen / Wushen:** a court minister or superintendent. **Renshen / Jiyou:** a provincial graduate serving on a maritime circuit; violent death.〉

**Yiwei day.** Ordinary. Zi and Hai in year and month give great distinction; years and luck are judged alike. Jiwei month brings injury through punishment; Bingshen month death with the body mutilated; Jichou month ancestral ruin and violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuzi / Jiayin:** Vice Minister Zheng Yue. **Jiaxu / Gengwu:** Chief Censor Wu Jihuan. **Wuchen / Jiayin:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Jimao / Bingyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiayin / Dingmao:** a commander-in-chief. **Wuyin / Renxu:** a commander-in-chief. **Wuzi / Guihai:** a court minister.〉

**Yiyou day.** Distinguished but solitary, with repeated rises and falls. A month communicating with Water qi, without Xin or Wu, also gives distinction. Wuyin month is greatly ominous; Dingsi month ruins the ancestral estate and brings poverty; Jiyou month gives death by a metal blade. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinwei / Gengzi:** a senior provincial administrator, banished to frontier service. **Jiaxu / Bingyin:** a provincial graduate. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** a provincial graduate. **Jiwei / Jiaxu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Guihai / Jiayin:** Zhou Woqiao, whose son Yanru became Grand Secretary.〉[^39]

**Yihai day.** Transformation into Azure and Red gives blessing. Gengchen in year and month gives wealth and distinction; pure Hai success and failure. Chou month with southern luck gives a bureau directorship; Si or Wu with western luck gives extreme poverty. Renchen month brings punishment; Yiyou month ancestral ruin and a violent death amid high distinction. 〈The preferences and prohibitions of these six days are broadly alike; but sitting on You or Si does not complete Rat Nobility and must be judged under another configuration.〉 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Gengwu:** a minister. **Bingwu / Xinchou:** chief minister of the Court of Pasturage. **Bingxu / Jihai:** a commander-in-chief. **Renxu / Xinhai:** a censor. **Renwu / Renzi:** a county magistrate. **Yiwei / Wuzi:** Grand Secretary Zhang Fujing. **Xinhai / Jiawu:** poor. **Bingwu / Guisi:** a beggar.〉[^40]

〈The Hour Encountering the Six Nobles:  
Danger comes first, but no calamity follows.  
Early years make achievement difficult;  
In middle and later life, Wealth and Lu abound.〉

> The noble pattern of the six Yi at Bingzi hour  
> Is exceptional only without clash or breaking.  
> No Geng or Shen, nor Si, You, or Chou:  
> One surely rides in cap and carriage to bow at the vermilion steps.

> A Yi day approaches Bingzi hour;  
> Hurting Officer, with Officer wholly removed, brings flourishing honor.  
> Hai, Mao, and Wei months are no common thing;  
> Luck arrives at the body’s vigor and supported strength.  
> Without Xin or Geng, blessing develops;  
> Wu’s clash and Chou’s binding leave ordinary fortunes.  
> Punishment and control make everything an empty scene;  
> This fate may decline or flourish.[^41]

## 14. Yi Days, Dingchou Hour
### 六乙日丑時斷
<!-- source: L630-L677 -->
〈The unfavorable months for the six Yi days below follow the preceding rule; the hour is judged together with them.〉

> A Yi day born at Dingchou hour:  
> Food God assists, encountering Wealth and Officer.  
> A month communicating with Metal qi transforms into blessing;  
> Do not judge this as ordinary low station.

On a Yi day at Dingchou hour, Food meets Wealth and Officer. Ding is Food, Geng Officer, Ji Wealth. Chou contains Xin Metal to form the configuration, and Ji Earth obtains its position. Support gives distinction. A month communicating with Metal qi and achieving transformation gives substantial wealth and honorable standing; without communication with the month’s qi, fortunes are ordinary.

**Yichou day.** Autumn birth gives authority but also illness. Summer is auspicious; winter ordinary; spring gives strength, distinction, and longevity. 〈Recorded cases—**Guichou / Yichou:** Xiao Zhu, serving in the two drafting offices. **Gengwu / Bingxu:** Li Yunchang, provincial first and Hanlin scholar. **Wushen / Jiazi:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Yimao day.** Hai month strengthens the body. Xin appears as Indirect Officer, with Ding in the pillars restraining it: surveillance or military office. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingsi / Jiachen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiwei / Guiwei:** a deputy surveillance commissioner.〉[^42]

**Yisi day.** Hai, Mao, Wei, and Yin months give distinction. A month communicating with Metal qi, with supporting ground, brings substantial blessing. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Jiayin:** an administration commissioner. **Gengyin / Wuzi:** a mobile-corps commander, killed by Wo raiders; no sons. **Wuchen / Xinyou:** great distinction. **Jiashen / Yihai:** a provincial graduate. **Jiayin / Gengwu:** Grand Secretary Gu Bingqian. **Bingwu / Xinmao:** first in the military palace examination.〉

**Yiwei day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give wealth. Spring gives long life; autumn name and profit; summer poverty and low station; winter ordinary fortunes. Shen in year and month gives third-rank military office. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingyou / Wushen:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Yichou / Wuyin:** a ministry secretary; died young. **Xinwei / Jihai:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Yiyou day.** Communication with Wood qi and support give manifest distinction. Shen and Chou in year and month are also good; Yin and Hai are better still. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Gengyin:** a minister. **Jiashen / Yihai:** a minister. **Renzi / Wushen:** Vice Minister Wang Bing. **Wuyin / Jiwei:** a censor. **Wuchen / Yimao:** a prefect. **Jiayin / Jisi:** Hanlin scholar Shen Zibin. **Xinsi / Wuxu:** a high metropolitan-examination placing. **Dingchou / Yisi:** a censor. **Bingyin / Gengyin:** Minister Huang Guangsheng. **Bingshen / Jiawu:** a military commissioner.〉

**Yihai day.** Hai month gives an impatient but principled nature, a worthy wife, filial children, and sixth- or seventh-rank office. Wu month gives Birth; Officer and Seal exposed in year and month bring great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Renwu:** Minister Wu Peng. **Bingyin / Gengzi:** Chief Censor Chen Ruli. **Yisi / Yihai:** a prefect. **Bingzi / Xinchou:** a commander-in-chief; rich.〉

〈Wealth and Officer upon the Hour:  
Food God emerges in the heavenly stem.  
If punishment and clash are encountered early,  
The development of blessing is assured.〉

> The Storehouse opens in the hour when Yi sees Ding;  
> Food God sits in the Storehouse, near Lu and Wealth.  
> Without a key one is no guest at court,  
> Yet still a person blessed with leisure and peace.

> A Yi day encounters Dingchou hour;  
> Longevity Star develops beyond doubt.  
> The body dwells in [Capricorn]—do not resent delay;  
> Money and wealth accumulate within the Storehouse.  
> Year, hour, and month combining bring advancement;  
> Void and punishment make wife and children difficult.  
> Parents and the ranks of brothers have their gains and losses;  
> When luck arrives, treasure is secured in the golden chest.[^43]

## 15. Yi Days, Wuyin Hour
### 六乙日寅時斷
<!-- source: L678-L717 -->

> A Yi day born at Wuyin hour:  
> Defeated Wealth and turning from Lu truly injure the body.  
> There is desire but no strength, much success and failure:  
> Only an ordinary person’s food and clothing.

On a Yi day at Wuyin hour, Wealth is defeated and Lu turned away from. Yi uses Geng as Officer, but Bing within Yin injures Officer and turns from Lu. Wu and Ji are Wealth, but Jia flourishing within Yin defeats Wealth. Undertakings succeed and fail, with ordinary results. Communication with Earth qi is auspicious.

**Yichou day.** High standing. Birth in Zi year and Xu month gives wealth and distinction. Chen and Xu with Wood-and-Fire luck give commanding authority. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Guimao:** Yu Youding, palace third and Grand Secretary. **Xinyou / Guisi:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Xinchou / Dingyou:** a bureau director. **Yisi / Renwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengshen / Jiashen:** a censor. **Jiwei / Jiaxu:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Yimao day.** Blessing develops amid punishment. Autumn birth gives distinction. You year meeting Chen, Xu, Chou, or Wei month gives wealth. Mao month gives Established Lu; Wu month, Seal’s generation. Officer and Seal exposed are both auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Wushen:** Ouyang Wenzhong. **Guiwei / Yichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guimao / Yimao:** great distinction. **Wuyin / Gengshen:** a provincial graduate. **Gengshen / Renwu:** distinguished. **Guimao / Yimao:** rich. **Bingwu / Xinmao:** a commander-in-chief. **Dingsi / Renyin:** Zhu Guozhen, head of the heir apparent’s household.〉[^44]

**Yisi day.** Solitude, injury, and ordinary fortunes. Shen and Geng in year and month give Direct Officer; Chou and Xin give Seven Killings: both bring distinction. Chen month with northern luck is auspicious. 〈One account says:〉 sudden wealth in middle age. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinchou / Gengzi:** Grand Secretary Qiu Jun, an eminent official. **Yichou / Wuyin:** a provincial administrator. **Wuzi / Yichou:** distinguished. **Jiaxu / Dingmao:** an actor.〉

**Yiwei day.** Spring birth gives longevity; autumn manifest distinction; summer ordinary fortunes; winter reversals. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei are all auspicious; years and luck follow the same rule. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Jiwei:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Gengwu / Guiwei:** minor distinction. **Gengwu / Gengchen:** a provincial graduate. **Jihai / Wuchen:** an irregularly admitted tribute student. **Renxu / Bingwu:** Zeng Keqian, palace third. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Yiyou day.** Spring birth gives wealth; summer ordinary fortunes; autumn distinction and a shortened life; winter is auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Dingwei:** Song Tianxun, Marquis of Xining; violent death. **Dingmao / Xinhai:** an administration commissioner. **Guisi / Dingsi:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Dingmao / Dingwei:** Chief Censor Ji Sanquan. **Wuwu / Xinyou:** Commander Wan Biao, versed in Chan learning, a cultivated and refined gentleman. **Wuchen / Yimao:** a prince of the first rank. **Bingzi / Jihai:** Kong Zhenyun, palace second and admitted to the Grand Secretariat.〉

**Yihai day.** Spring is auspicious; summer laborious; autumn and winter distinguished. Zi and Chou in year and month give distinction up to third rank, with rises and falls and a long life. 〈One account says:〉 after thirty, solitude and the development of blessing. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Xinchou:** a senior provincial administrator. **Bingwu / Xinmao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiyou / Dingsi:** Li Changgeng, Minister of Personnel. **Yisi / Jichou:** an administration commissioner.〉

〈The Tiger Lying on the Plain:  
Action and withdrawal alike fear danger.  
Just as the bright moon rises,  
Clouds obscure the place of light again.〉

> Examine carefully a Yi day at Yin hour:  
> The person invites both right and wrong.  
> Luck declines and meets Void, punishment, and control:  
> The toil of hand and heart has no appointed end.

> A Yi day encounters Wuyin hour;  
> Within it, Wealth suffers hidden injury.  
> Hurting Officer and turning from Lu fill the pillars;  
> Even with wealth and distinction, wife and children are injured.  
> Flourishing Wealth-and-Officer luck develops blessing;  
> Luck meeting Companions and Killings raises calamity.  
> The Six Relations and blood kin find little harmony;  
> Self-established, self-accomplished, one stands alone and free.

## 16. Yi Days, Jimao Hour
### 六乙日卯時斷
<!-- source: L718-L755 -->

> A Yi day born at Jimao hour:  
> The hour holds the day’s Lu; Wealth approaches favorably.  
> Strength communicating with Wood qi gives undoubted distinction;  
> Yet heavy Xin at You can still bring distress.

On a Yi day at Jimao hour, Lu enters the ancestral hall. Yi Wood encounters its Established Lu at Mao, giving grace and beauty. Communication with Wood and Fire gives distinction. Geng and Xin injure Lu and break the fate, bringing eye disease. Si, You, and Chou months give ordinary subsistence; Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei are auspicious, as is Shen month.[^45]

**Yichou day.** High standing and great blessing in middle age. Spring makes the body too strong and solitary; summer brings poverty; autumn illness; winter warmth and generosity. Without Xin Metal in the pillars, it is auspicious.
