Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give gold-and-purple distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Wuyin:** a prefect. **Gengwu / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dinghai / Jiyou:** a metropolitan graduate and prefect. **Jiayin / Jiaxu:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Yimao day.** High standing. Spring strength gives a monk or Daoist ample means. Summer is ordinary; without Xin Metal it is auspicious. Autumn brings illness; winter warmth and generosity. Mao and Chou in year and month bring manifest distinction and long life. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Wuzi:** Minister Lin Tingxuan. **Guiwei / Wuwu:** a surveillance commissioner. **Renshen / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yimao / Wuzi:** a commander of ten thousand households. **Wuzi / Yimao:** a transport commissioner. **Xinmao / Gengzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingchou / Yisi:** a provincial graduate. **Guichou / Yimao:** immensely rich. **Renshen / Xinhai:** a minister. **Guiwei / Xinyou:** death by drowning. **Wuwu / Yichou:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Yiyou / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guimao / Jiayin:** Ding Bin, a minister in the southern capital; a clerk shared the chart.〉

**Yisi day.** Spring gives solitary poverty; summer ordinary fortunes; autumn illness; winter distinction. Wu and Chen in year and month, with the earthly branches linked along one course, are especially auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Jiachen:** a prefect. **Dinghai / Bingwu:** rich and long-lived.〉

**Yiwei day.** Without Geng or Xin Metal in year and month, there is distinction. For autumn birth, examine the depth or thinness of the supporting ground. Renxu in year and month gives third or fourth rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Xinhai:** Supervising Secretary Li Zhaolong. **Jiawu / Dingchou:** a chancellor. **Xinyou / Xinchou:** a provincial administrator. **Gengchen / Dinghai:** a provincial graduate. **Gengshen / Jichou:** a provincial graduate. **Jimao / Renshen:** a provincial graduate. **Renwu / Xinhai:** a provincial graduate. **Xinyou / Yiwei:** a pacification commissioner. **Xinhai / Gengyin:** the Ming Chongzhen emperor; lost the realm on entering Bing luck. **Bingxu / Bingshen:** Li Chengliang, enfeoffed earl; “calamity begins.” **Jiayin / Gengwu:** two sons were metropolitan graduates.〉[^46]

**Yiyou day.** Refinement. Early years ruin the ancestral estate; middle years develop wealth; later years bring solitude and injury. 〈One account says:〉 death without a burial place. Shen and Chou in year and month give gold and purple. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinmao / Xinchou:** a provincial administrator. **Dingsi / Guimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiwei / Dingsi:** a provincial graduate. **Yimao / Jichou:** an assistant prefect. **Xinwei / Wuxu:** Liu Zhifeng, Minister of Justice.〉

**Yihai day.** Birth in Yin or Si month, without Geng or Xin, gives the Day’s Lu Returning to the Hour, with manifest and pure distinction. Pure Mao in year and month gives an eminent monk or Daoist adept. Xu gives exceptional distinction, intelligence, Wealth, and Lu. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Dingmao:** a chief censor. **Jiayin / Dingchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuzi / Jiayin:** a privy councillor. **Bingshen / Renyin:** a junior provincial administrator.〉

〈The Day’s Lu Dwelling in the Hour:  
In the blue clouds, one plucks the cassia branch.  
Without Officer bringing breaking or harm,  
One’s name is known in all directions.〉

> The Day’s Lu in the Hour is a distinct configuration;  
> Food God and [Wealth-Horse] must be encountered.  
> Hurting-Officer and Seal luck are both auspicious;  
> Without Officer, Lu is naturally abundant.[^47]

> A Yi day approaches Jimao hour;  
> Indirect Wealth and the hour’s Lu return in greeting.  
> If Xin Metal and You do not punish it,  
> The tiger list surely bears one’s name.  
> Parents and the Six Relations cannot be relied upon;  
> The ranks of brothers take wing each for themselves.  
> Writing shines, and talent is present;  
> Unbroken and unclashed, this is a noble fate.

## 17. Yi Days, Gengchen Hour
### 六乙日辰時斷
<!-- source: L756-L799 -->

> A Yi day born at Gengchen hour:  
> Water white, Metal clear—the transformation image is true.  
> Ren follows Xin and You into noble Officer;  
> Yet guard against eye disease that diminishes the spirit.[^48]

On a Yi day at Gengchen hour there are a worthy wife and noble children. Yi combines with Geng and transforms into Metal. Communication with Shen, Si, You, or Chou month gives grace and distinction, though eye disease must be guarded against. If transformation does not occur, take Ren as Seal and Geng as Officer; Chen Earth and Gui Water form the configuration, giving Yi Wood support. Southeast luck brings manifest distinction and balanced peace.

**Yichou day.** Ancestral ruin and injury to father; bodily weakness makes illness a concern. Communication with the month’s qi gives distinction. Zi and Shen in year and month, with Jia and Wu exposed among the heavenly stems, assemble the Three Wonders and give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Wuchen:** Qiao Yu, Minister of Personnel. **Wushen / Jiazi:** Yang Shen, palace first. **Xinsi / Gengyin:** chief minister of State Ceremonial. **Dingchou / Guimao:** a prefect. **Dingchou / Jiachen:** Huang Shijun, palace first and admitted to the Grand Secretariat. **Gengwu / Bingxu:** Xu Xie, metropolitan first and Hanlin compiler.〉

**Yimao day.** Wealth and distinction; year and month communicating with Fire and Earth give great distinction. 〈One account says:〉 great development after punishment. 〈Another:〉 violent death; with rescue in year and month, punishment instead. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Gengwu:** Minister Yang Bo; died in Jiaxu year, his son a metropolitan graduate, from Shanxi. **Jimao / Gengwu:** a provincial graduate. **Yimao / Wuyin:** Dong Qichang, Minister of Rites, renowned for calligraphy.〉

**Yisi day.** Success and failure in undertakings. Monks and Daoists have wealth and distinction but illness; ordinary people injure wife and children. Shen, Zi, Chen, Mao, and Si in year and month give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renxu / Yisi:** vice minister of the Court of the Imperial Stud. **Yisi / Dingchou:** a prefect. **Guiyou / Renxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingxu / Guisi:** a commander of ten thousand households. **Xinhai / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jisi / Dingchou:** Liu Shenghe of Cangzhou, entered the Ministry of Revenue as bureau director. **Dingchou / Renyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengchen / Yiyou:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Yiwei day.** Hai and Mao months strengthen the body; Si and Shen strengthen Officer. Killings and Seal exposed among the heavenly stems give distinction. Pure Killings in Chou and You, with Fire in the pillars to restrain it, is also auspicious. Xu and Chou in year and month complete the Four Storehouses and give great distinction. 〈One account says:〉 ancestral ruin, punishment and calamity, and solitude. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Jimao:** a vice court minister. **Jiwei / Dingchou:** a chief censor. **Bingshen / Xinmao:** Supervising Secretary Li Guan. **Dingmao / Xinhai:** Tang Shunzhi, metropolitan first and chief censor; according to the *Star Records*, his day was Yihai. **Renshen / Xinhai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jihai / Yisi:** the Realized Master Tao. **Renchen / Jiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Guichou / Jiazi:** a woman whose son Li Chunfang was palace first and Grand Secretary. **Wushen / Yimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renshen / Wushen:** Chief Censor Yang Lian, died in the Imperial Guard’s prison.〉[^49]

**Yiyou day.** Hai and Zi in year and month, with Wu and Gui exposed in the stems, give distinction. Yin, Si, and Wu months restrain Officer and Killings and are auspicious. Pure You transforms into Metal and gives abundant blessing. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Jiaxu:** a chancellor. **Yimao / Gengchen:** a chancellor. **Yiyou / Yiyou:** a vice minister. **Jiazi / Bingzi:** a censor. **Gengwu / Xinwei:** an administration commissioner. **Bingshen / Guisi:** a judge. **Bingxu / Guisi:** magistrate of Zanhuang, executed under the law for losing the city. **Yiyou / Wuzi:** a provincial graduate. **Gengchen / Yiyou:** the Four Pillars combine in two pairs; great distinction.〉

**Yihai day.** If not distinguished, then rich. Gui and Wu in year and month make one transformation; Shen and Mao are both flourishing, and Si, Chou, and You assemble Metal. Wood-and-Earth luck reaches gold and purple. 〈One account says:〉 self-punishment and harm amid development. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Guichou:** a vice censor-in-chief. **Yihai / Wuyin:** a provincial administrator. **Bingyin / Dingyou:** an administration commissioner. **Bingyin / Jihai:** a provincial graduate. **Xinwei / Gengzi:** minor distinction. **Guiyou / Wuwu:** immense wealth. **Yisi / Jiashen:** Minister Chen Jian.〉

〈Heaven and Earth Transforming Qi:  
Autumn birth is greatly auspicious.  
Luck travels east and north:  
How could such eminence be ordinary?〉

> Yi and Geng meet: distinction is beyond doubt;  
> Yin Wood and yang Metal properly combine in the hour.  
> Auspicious luck, strong body, no clash or breaking:  
> A noble person raises one through promotion.

> A Yi day, Gengchen hour, correctly placed:  
> Heavenly Officer guards the Storehouse at Qian’s origin.  
> In youth, the tiger list carries one’s name;  
> The nature is warm, good, respectful, and frugal.  
> Scholar or commoner, the wife is worthy and children noble;  
> A talented person advances in rank and emolument.  
> Southern Li, Wu and Gui, link in Fire:  
> Amid wealth and distinction, one meets danger.[^50]

## 18. Yi Days, Xinsi Hour
### 六乙日辛巳時斷
<!-- source: L800-L847 -->

> A Yi day born at Xinsi hour:  
> Metal and Wood contend, indicating a lack of benevolence.  
> Transformation and a month strengthening the body give distinction;  
> Without communication or transformation, fear injury to the person.

On a Yi day at Xinsi hour, hidden Metal contends, and disputes arise daily. A month communicating with the body’s strength, providing support and transforming ghost into Officer, gives distinction in body-strengthening luck. A month communicating with Wood qi gives great distinction in flourishing-Metal luck; a month communicating with Metal qi also gives distinction in body-strengthening luck.

**Yichou day.** Mixture first, purity afterward. Yin and Wu, Bing and Ding, in year and month restrain Indirect Officer: judge a high fate. Si, Shen, You, and Chou months repeat Officer and Killings and often bring illness. Following Killings is also auspicious; a strong body gives military authority and renown. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingsi / Guichou:** Chief Censor Zhou [Chong]; ten sons. **Yimao / Jichou:** Chief Censor Fang Jinsha. **Dingmao / Renzi:** He Wanzuo, education commissioner in Shandong, entered the register of eminent officials for his fairness and was promoted to senior provincial administrator and surveillance commissioner in Fujian. **Wuwu / Wuwu:** Court Minister Rao Shen. **Xinsi / Wuxu:** a thief.〉[^51]

**Yimao day.** Spring gives a strong body and slight Killings: great distinction. Summer is ordinary. In autumn Officer and Killings flourish; in winter Seal-Ribbon flourishes. Both are auspicious, but autumn is inferior to winter. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Bingzi:** Grand Secretary Jia Yong. **Jiayin / Bingyin:** Chancellor Shi Miyuan. **Guiwei / Yimao:** Vice Minister Li Chi. **Wuzi / Jiwei:** Chief Censor Wang Zhuan. **Guimao / Renxu:** a court minister. **Jimao / Bingyin:** a junior provincial administrator. **Yihai / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingshen / Wuxu:** a provincial administrator. **Gengzi / Jichou:** a provincial administrator. **Bingchen / Yiyou:** a bureau director.〉[^52]

**Yisi day.** Injury to the wife; both wife and children come late. In Si, You, or Chou month, Wood is yielding and Metal heavy: illness, or otherwise a shortened life. Jia and Ji, Zi and Si, in year and month enter the Indirect-Officer configuration; western luck gives surveillance office. Spring’s strong body is still more auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiyou / Xinyou:** Hanlin scholar Lu Tai. **Guihai / Yimao:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Jiazi / Jisi:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Yisi / Xinsi:** a garrison commander. **Jiachen / Bingyin:** a chancellor.〉

**Yiwei day.** Wu month gives a strong stem and renown in military office. Hai and Zi give Seal-Ribbon and are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Bingzi:** a censor. **Yiwei / Guiwei:** a bureau director. **Gengzi / Bingxu:** a prefect; no sons. **Jihai / Bingzi:** Tian Le, Minister of War. **Jiawu / Bingyin:** Cheng Yong, serving on a southern circuit. **Bingshen / Guisi:** an education commissioner.〉

**Yiyou day.** Birth in Wei month, with the body seated upon restraint, is auspicious. Autumn gives distinction through Indirect Officer. You month with southern luck brings poverty, or otherwise disability. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinhai / Renchen:** Minister Zhang Wenxian. **Jiazi / Jiaxu:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Dingsi / Yisi:** a military surveillance official. **Jiayin / Dingchou:** a provincial graduate. **Bingwu / Jiawu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wushen / Renxu:** provincial-examination first and associate prefect. **Jiayin / Guiyou:** first in the military examination. **Renyin / Yisi:** Hanlin scholar Zhang Tuan.〉[^53]

**Yihai day.** Si and Wu months restrain Indirect Officer. Spring gives a strong stem and surveillance office; autumn gives heavy Killings and indicates disability. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingyou / Bingwu:** a commandery prince. **Bingyin / Xinmao:** a metropolitan graduate; died young. **Yiwei / Wuyin:** Minister Liu Yiru. **Xinmao / Xinchou:** Minister He Qifeng. **Jiashen / Guiyou:** Li Yutian, governor of Liao; met calamity. **Guihai / Bingchen:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈Indirect Officer upon the Hour:  
At the edge of danger, passage still opens.  
A strong body meets flourishing luck;  
Only after leaving the ancestral home is distinction achieved.〉

> Yi and Si injure one another, meeting Metal and Wood;  
> The search for name and profit turns repeatedly.  
> The Six Relations and blood kin are as though absent;  
> In Seal-Ribbon’s luck country, blessing can develop.

> A Yi day encounters Xinsi hour;  
> Ghost flourishes in the pillars and the body declines.  
> The Six Relations offer no support or harmony;  
> Plans and hopes meet success and failure.  
> Several times, misfortune turns to good;  
> Know truly that bitterness ends and sweetness comes.  
> Luck strengthens the body, held in Seal-Ribbon’s embrace;  
> Wealth and distinction win the acclaim of the age.

## 19. Yi Days, Renwu Hour
### 六乙日午時斷
<!-- source: L848-L891 -->

> A Yi day born at Renwu hour:  
> Seal-Ribbon generates the body; Wealth and Food gather.  
> A month communicating with Water and Wood makes Lu abundant;  
> Without communication with the month’s qi, the allotment is ordinary.

On a Yi day at Renwu hour there are Seal-Ribbon and the Hall of Learning. Yi Wood is born at Wu; Ren is Seal, Ding Food, and Ji Wealth. Ding and Ji come to flourishing at Wu. Communication with a Water month’s qi gives graceful writing. Without communication with the month’s qi, food and clothing are ordinary, though suitable luck is also favorable.[^54]

**Yichou day.** Spring and summer often give wealth and distinction. In autumn or winter, Officer and Seal, or pure Killings exposed in the stems, are particularly auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengxu / Jichou:** a provincial administrator. **Xinchou / Dingyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengshen / Gengchen:** a provincial graduate. **Xinyou / Bingshen:** a provincial graduate. **Wuyin / Jiwei:** a vice general. **Yichou / Jiashen:** Sun Shenxing, palace third and minister.〉

**Yimao day.** High standing. Chou month enters Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer; Shen and You months balance body and Killings: all give manifest distinction. Pure Wu and You in year and month give third or fourth rank; Chen and Xu are ordinary. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Jichou:** the old villain Qin Hui. **Wuzi / Guihai:** a vice court minister. **Dingyou / Renzi:** a chief censor. **Jiaxu / Dingchou:** the upright Hai Rui, provincial graduate and chief censor, a forthright minister. **Bingchen / Renwu:** a provincial graduate. **Gengwu / Gengchen:** a provincial graduate.〉[^55]

**Yisi day.** Auspicious. Spring and summer give wealth and distinction; autumn and winter ordinary fortunes. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Renchen:** a chief censor, removed from office in Jiwei and died in Xinwei. **Guisi / Dingsi:** Governor-General Sun Chuanting. **Dingchou / Renyin:** Governor-General Zhang Zongheng; “in Renwu year, was…” [the event is unfinished]. **Jiawu / Yihai:** a provincial graduate. **Wuxu / Xinyou:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^56]

**Yiwei day.** Yin and Mao strengthen the body; Hai and Zi strengthen Seal. Chou month gives the Three Wonders of Wealth, Officer, and Seal. All bring manifest distinction. Shen month’s Direct Officer is particularly auspicious. Gengwu year and Dinghai month give Food God in the same nest: approaching Food and encountering Lu bring wealth and distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingxu / Bingshen:** a bureau director. **Jihai / Jiaxu:** a censor. **Jiawu / Dingchou:** an assistant prefect. **Yichou / Jiashen:** a provincial graduate. **Guihai / Yimao:** a chancellor. **Yihai / Jichou:** a prefect. **Gengchen / Renwu:** distinguished. **Yihai / Jichou:** rich. **Gengyin / Wuyin:** a subprefect. **Jiawu / Dingmao:** an earl.〉

**Yiyou day.** Spring is auspicious; autumn and summer ordinary. Pillars of pure Yiyou, with Geng exposed to combine and transform, or Seal appearing to assist, give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengxu / Yiyou:** a vice minister. **Guichou / Bingchen:** a prefect. **Bingchen / Xinchou:** Tang Dazhang, an attendant in the heir apparent’s household. **Yihai / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Yihai day.** Spring strengthens the body; summer gives abundant blessing; autumn reversals; winter auspicious celebrations. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Guimao:** Minister Lin Jun, an eminent official; a Dingsi-year case was likewise distinguished. **Wushen / Renxu:** a provincial administrator. **Xinsi / Guisi:** Minister Xi Shu. **Gengshen / Renwu:** Minister Qian Bangyan. **Yiyou / Renwu:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Guimao / Guihai:** a provincial graduate. **Guiwei / Jiayin:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Xinyou / Jiawu:** a ministry bureau director.〉[^57]

〈Seal-Ribbon Approaching the Hour:  
Food God is encountered within.  
The time arrives, and the person becomes eminent;  
Poor luck obstructs achievement and name.〉

> A Yi day born at Renwu hour:  
> A month communicating with Water and Wood wins noble esteem.  
> Luck travels flourishing Officer without clash or breaking;  
> The household prospers and affairs satisfy the heart.

> A Yi day encounters Renwu hour;  
> Food God and Seal-Ribbon share one palace.  
> Without clash, breaking, or mutual punishment,  
> Renown truly finds its answering echo.  
> A refined scholar of the Literary Hall,  
> Whose writing rises above the crowd:  
> Noble people welcome him, petty people resent him;  
> This fate stands high in middle and later life.

## 20. Yi Days, Guiwei Hour
### 六乙日未時斷
<!-- source: L892-L927 -->

> A Yi day born at Guiwei hour:  
> Within the Tomb, inverted injury is encountered.  
> Horse is feeble, Wealth slight, and Food controlled:  
> Lifelong food and clothing are ordinary.

On a Yi day at Guiwei hour, Yi takes Gui as Inverted Food. Within Wei are Ding Fire as Food God and Ji Earth as Indirect Wealth. Ji breaks Gui, while Gui overturns Ding Fire’s Food within Wei. Subsistence is ordinary; communication with an Earth month is auspicious.[^58]

**Yichou day.** Ominous punishment and solitude. Year and month communicating with Earth qi are auspicious. 〈One account says:〉 mixture first, purity afterward. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Dingmao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingwei / Xinhai:** the Ming Tianqi emperor’s empress.〉

**Yimao day.** One changes the ancestral establishment, leaves the parents, and makes a home through the wife. Wu and Wei in year and month give distinction, especially with spring birth. 〈Recorded cases—**Yimao / Wuyin:** Minister Xu Lun. **Bingshen / Xinmao:** a Grand Preceptor.〉

**Yisi day.** If not distinguished, then rich; difficulty first, ease afterward. Pure Wu gives third-rank distinction. Chen, Xu, and Chou months are all auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuchen / Wuwu:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Gengzi / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guichou / Renxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingchou / Gengxu:** Grand Secretary Gao Yi; a company commander shared the chart.〉

**Yiwei day.** Spring makes the body strong and brings injury. Autumn strengthens Officer and Killings and gives a share in examination success. Winter is stable; summer ordinary. 〈Recorded case—**Gengchen / Jihai:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Yiyou day.** The body sits upon Killings. Spring strengthens it and is auspicious. Summer gives weak body and declining Killings: poverty. In autumn Killings flourishes and the body can follow transformation: distinction. Winter is ordinary. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months, with Geng or Xin exposed, give distinction in Metal luck. Once Blade luck arrives, the person withdraws or is removed from office. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Gengyin:** Chief Censor Zhang Laixi. **Gengyin / Bingxu:** a surveillance commissioner. **Wuxu / Gengshen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yimao / Guiwei:** Governor Qian Shiwan.〉

**Yihai day.** Spring’s flourishing Wood injures wife and children. Shen month strengthens Officer and gives distinction. You strengthens Killings; Fire in year and month makes it auspicious. Wu, Wei, and Xu in year and month give first or second rank. Winter birth is steady and substantial. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Wuxu:** a minister. **Jiashen / Dingmao:** a chief censor. **Gengwu / Guiwei:** a court minister. **Jihai / Jiaxu:** a supervising secretary.〉

〈The Tomb Configuration of the Six Yi:  
A strong body employs Wealth and Officer.  
Without injury or control in the Four Pillars,  
Achievement and name are no ordinary thing.〉

> A Yi day encounters Guiwei hour:  
> Reckoning it, one leaves the ancestors and fails to establish a home.  
> Punishment, control, and harm bring repeated success and failure;  
> Auspicious luck adds flowers to brocade.

> A Yi day encounters Guiwei;  
> Born at Wood’s Tomb, one is short-lived and solitary.  
> Brothers in their ranks are as though absent;  
> The disposition is inconstant in joy and anger.  
> One establishes and achieves a livelihood alone;  
> The Six Relations and blood kin are near or distant.  
> Noble Person obtaining combination gives mutual support:  
> This fate is poor at first and rich afterward.

## 21. Yi Days, Jiashen Hour
### 六乙日甲申時斷
<!-- source: L928-L969 -->

> A Yi day born at Jiashen hour:  
> Officer Star obtains Seal and comes to life in its position.  
> The month communicates with qi, without clash or breaking:  
> Surely a person of splendor on the official road.

On a Yi day at Jiashen hour, Officer and Seal generate the body. Yi takes Geng as Officer and Ren as Seal; at Shen, Geng flourishes and Ren is born. The body has support. Communication with Metal-and-Water months and luck gives distinction. Without such communication, the body is weak and Officer heavy: even distinction does not endure.

**Yichou day.** High standing. Pure Zi and Chen in year and month give great distinction in southeast luck. Si–You–Chou gives distinction with danger to be guarded against. Pure Wu and Wei are auspicious, as are Hai and Mao. Other months are ordinary. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Dingsi:** a prefect; violent death. **Jiawu / Dingchou:** distinguished. **Yiyou / Yihai:** rich. **Bingshen / Gengyin:** Li Zihua, palace second and vice chancellor of the Imperial Academy. **Wushen / Xinyou:** He Luoshu, Hanlin examiner.〉

**Yimao day.** Transformation gives distinction. A month communicating with Water qi, without injury or breaking, gives distinction; otherwise wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Xinwei:** a vice minister. **Jiawu / Bingyin:** an education commissioner. **Dingmao / Wushen:** Academician Zheng Yiwei. **Gengzi / Jimao:** a supervising secretary.〉

**Yisi day.** Body strong, Officer flourishing. Spring gives intelligence and manifest distinction up to fourth rank; summer bodily and mental labor; autumn and winter eye disease. Wu in year and month, followed by Wealth luck, gives distinction. 〈One account says:〉 transformation into distinction amid punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Guiwei:** an associate prefect. **Guiyou / Wuwu:** a judicial reviewer. **Bingyin / Jiawu:** Zhou Daodeng, Vice Minister of Rites.〉

**Yiwei day.** Birth in Wei, You, or Hai month gives intelligence, refinement, exceptional achievement, and second- or third-rank office. Bing, Ding, Yin, Wu, Mao, or You in year and month let Hurting Officer and Food restrain Killings: authority and distinction. 〈One account says:〉 loss amid flourishing, but flourishing in the end. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Guiyou:** Mao Zan, palace first and vice minister; another person owed his appointment to inherited favor. **Jiazi / Xinwei:** a vice minister. **Yiwei / Jimao:** Chief Censor Huang Yingpeng. **Jiawu / Guiwei:** a county magistrate. **Jiachen / Renshen:** a provincial graduate. **Jiyou / Jiaxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guichou / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate. **Gengshen / Renwu:** a commander-in-chief. **Renshen / Jiachen:** first in the provincial examination.〉[^59]

**Yiyou day.** Officer and Killings are mixed. Ding Fire in the pillars restraining Killings and retaining Officer is auspicious. Hai, Mao, Wei, or You in year and month gives the highest military rank, but not for long. Southeast luck gives great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Yiyou:** a minister. **Yisi / Gengchen:** a prefect. **Xinchou / Renchen:** a provincial graduate. **Renshen / Guichou:** Yu Fu, palace first. **Gengshen / Wuzi:** distinguished. **Gengzi / Yiyou:** distinguished.〉 〈For this day and hour, pure You in year and month follows Killings; Si and Chou make a Killings configuration; Zi and Chen transform into Seal. All are auspicious.〉[^60]

**Yihai day.** The hour falls in Void, indicating few children. Autumn birth gives office among the Six Ministers. Hai, Mao, Wu, and Wei months are all auspicious. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 communication with Yi–Geng transformation gives abundant blessing. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuxu / Gengshen:** Minister Wang Yaofeng; no sons. **Renxu / Jiyou:** a minister. **Jiashen / Bingzi:** a vice minister. **Yihai / Gengchen:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Dingyou / Bingwu:** a junior provincial administrator. **Gengchen / Jichou:** Wu Shouli, a great merchant. **Guichou / Guihai:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

〈Birth and Traveling Horse:  
Heavenly blessing governs literary accomplishment.  
Metal-and-Earth luck is auspicious;  
Achievement and name cannot be measured.〉

> A Yi day encounters Shen in the hour;  
> Birth and Traveling Horse are intimate within.  
> Tianyi Noble Person comes to assist;  
> One puts aside rough clothes and enters the Purple Palace.

> A Yi day meets nobility at Shen hour;  
> Eminent people look on with delight.  
> Petty people praise its rare excellence;  
> Control, breaking, clash, and punishment diminish its strength.  
> The body strong, luck meets auspicious ground:  
> Know that Wealth and Officer both flourish.  
> Saddle and horse, cap and robes are present;  
> The family’s standing surely changes.

## 22. Yi Days, Yiyou Hour
### 六乙日乙酉時斷
<!-- source: L970-L1009 -->

> A Yi day born at Yiyou hour:  
> Meeting a Metal configuration, Fire makes it exceptional.  
> The useful influence encounters Wood, repeatedly appearing;  
> Ghost extinguished, longevity injured—support is lost instead.[^61]

On a Yi day at Yiyou hour, the body is extinguished and ghost flourishes. Yi takes Xin as ghost; at You, Xin flourishes and Yi is extinguished. Communication with Si, You, or Chou month, transforming into a Metal configuration, gives distinction. If the useful influence sits upon Wood, the body is strong and does not transform, yet You appears again, the result is either early death or poverty.

**Yichou day.** High standing. Si, You, and Chou months assemble Metal; western luck gives great distinction. Yin, Wu, and Xu months bring poverty and low station; Hai, Mao, and Wei are auspicious. Pure Zi in year and month, followed by southern luck, gives first or second rank. Yin month with Fire-and-Metal luck gives seventh rank; Shen month with Water-and-Wood luck gives gold-and-purple distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Yiyou:** Minister Mao Cheng. **Renzi / Renzi:** a minister. **Yimao / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yichou / Xinsi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinchou / Dingyou:** a prefect. **Jiawu / Wuchen:** Wu Runan, a supervising secretary for Rites.〉

**Yimao day.** A month communicating with the Metal configuration gives distinction. Wei and Yin in year and month give first- or second-rank office. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Dingyou:** Han Xin. **Yiyou / Jiyou:** a military commander. **Gengwu / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate; died young. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate. **Xinyou / Jihai:** Zhao Yan, Minister of War, from Shaanxi.〉

**Yisi day.** Spring is auspicious. Summer’s Hurting Officer with restraint is good. In autumn Wood is weak and Metal heavy: early death, or otherwise illness. Winter brings abundant blessing but also early death.

**Yiwei day.** Arching Nobility: without punishment or breaking, distinction. Shen filling the position prevents this. Hai and Mao months with western luck give distinction. 〈One account says:〉 punishment amid flourishing. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Bingchen:** a censor. **Renshen / Yisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yihai / Gengchen:** Liu Kangzhi, a high metropolitan-examination placing and ministry bureau director. **Gengzi / Dinghai:** a provincial graduate and ministry bureau director.〉

**Yiyou day.** Self-punishment at flourishing ground. Heavy Fire and Earth in year and month indicate calamity. Communication with the month’s qi, Seal and Food exposed, and Fire-and-Wood luck give great distinction. Pure You in the earthly branches transforms into a Metal image; with Seal-Ribbon, its distinction is beyond telling. Officer encountered in years and luck is most feared. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Yiyou:** the Yuan Shizu emperor. **Yiyou / Yiyou:** Imperial Consort Zhang; a pure and lofty person and a county magistrate shared this chart. **Gengxu / Yiyou:** a chancellor. **Jiayin / Guiyou:** a Grand Preceptor. **Dinghai / Renzi:** a minister. **Renxu / Gengxu:** a transmission commissioner. **Jichou / Yihai:** a county magistrate. **Wuwu / Dingsi:** a provincial graduate. **Yiyou / Jiashen:** distinguished. **Renzi / Renzi:** rich. **Renshen / Guichou:** violent death.〉[^62]

**Yihai day.** Spring birth gives the Benevolence-and-Longevity configuration and distinction. Yin month with Metal-and-Fire luck gives great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Jiashen:** Lun Wenxu, palace first; his sons Yixun, Yiliang, and Yishen were all distinguished. **Renshen / Gengxu:** a ministry secretary. **Bingyin / Dingyou:** a prefect. **Wuchen / Jiayin:** a provincial administrator. **Jiawu / Gengwu:** a county magistrate. **Jiayin / Guiyou:** an assistant prefect. **Xinmao / Xinchou:** Vice Minister He Qiming. **Gengyin / Dinghai:** granted the title of minister. **Bingyin / Xinchou:** a court minister. **Gengxu / Yiyou:** a deputy commissioner.〉

〈A Boat Sailing with the Current:  
The great river flows day after day.  
Killings subdued and body strong are auspicious;  
Seek Wealth and Lu to the heart’s content.〉

> The day stem is Yi, the hour approaches You;  
> Borrowing Killings for authority, a strong body is exceptional.  
> A weak body meeting Officer labors in vain;  
> Achievement must await the passage of favorable luck.

> A Yi day approaches Yiyou hour;  
> The birth-day Yi Wood has no sorrow.  
> Authority and distinction may be freely sought;  
> Without breaking, achievement is assured.  
> Wife and children suffer injury in early years;  
> Sources of wealth scatter like rain and gather like clouds.  
> Changing clan and [ancestral establishment] avoids grief;  
> In middle and later years, the household’s resources are secured.[^63]

## 23. Yi Days, Bingxu Hour
### 六乙日丙戌時斷
<!-- source: L1010-L1057 -->

> A Yi day born at Bingxu hour:  
> Defeated ghost approaches the body and brings injury.  
> Unless qi communicates with a body-strengthening month,  
> Solitary poverty and toil bring suffering hard to bear.

On a Yi day at Bingxu hour, defeated ghost approaches the body. Yi takes Geng as Officer; Bing turns away from Lu. Xu contains Xin’s residual qi and the Storehouse of Bing and Ding: Food God restrains Killings. Geng exposed in the pillars gives Hurting Officer Meeting Officer and a hundred forms of calamity. Yin and Wu in year and month assemble Bing Fire’s configuration: one Wood repeatedly encounters Fire positions. The person is proud and scornful, with ordinary subsistence and disability, or otherwise a short life. Communication with a body-strengthening month is auspicious.

**Yichou day.** Spring strengthens the body and is auspicious; summer gives excessive Hurting Officer; autumn labor and hardship. Winter’s Hai and Zi give Seal-Ribbon carrying Hurting Officer: the highest distinction. Xu month with Wood-and-Fire luck gives seventh rank. Pure Xu in year and month, with Geng and Bing exposed in the stems, gives great distinction. Yin and Wu completing the combination shorten life. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Yisi:** Li Bin, in the Personnel administration. **Guimao / Renxu:** Vice Minister Lin Ruchu. **Jimao / Jiaxu:** Minister Ling Yunyi; a clerk shared the chart. **Dingyou / Wushen:** Chief Censor Chai Jing. **Yihai / Dinghai:** Chief Censor Meng Zhong.〉

〈Liu Dashi and Qin Liang shared the chart. Liu succeeded in the Wuxu examination, Qin in Dingwei, and Meng in Guichou. Liu reached only vice-ministerial office; Qin only provincial administration commissioner; Meng’s outcome is not yet settled. Liu belonged to Yuzhou, Qin to Yangzhou, Meng to Yongzhou: their regions differed, and that accounts for it.〉[^64]

**Yimao day.** Yin and Mao months with western luck give sixth or seventh rank. Zi month gives Seal-Ribbon; Chou month gives Mixed Qi, with punishment bringing forth Wealth and Officer: both give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Jiazi:** a minister. **Yichou / Jichou:** distinguished. **Guihai / Dingsi:** a metropolitan graduate serving on a southern circuit.〉

**Yisi day.** Auspicious. Chou, Xu, and Wei in year and month give surveillance office or one of the Six Ministries. Hai month with eastern luck gives pure distinction in the Hanlin Academy. 〈Recorded cases—**Guihai / Dingsi:** a vice minister. **Dinghai / Renyin:** an academician. **Yimao / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiwei / Yihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuchen / Bingchen:** distinguished. **Renyin / Gengxu:** immensely rich. **Yichou / Wuyin:** immensely rich.〉

**Yiwei day.** Misfortune at flourishing ground. Mao, Wu, Wei, and Xu in year and month give manifest distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Yihai:** Minister Wang Hongru. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** an education commissioner. **Jiawu / Yihai:** a county magistrate. **[Jiaxu] / Gengwu:** a provincial graduate.〉[^65]

**Yiyou day.** Spring strengthens the body and winter strengthens Seal: great distinction. Summer’s Si and Wu and autumn’s You and Xu also give distinction, but the heavenly stems must be examined. Dingwei and Jiachen give a hard livelihood and lifelong encounters with noble people. Chou month punishing Xu is auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Renzi:** Minister Yang Wuhua. **Gengzi / Bingxu:** Minister Tu Zhizhai. **Bingzi / Gengyin:** Chief Censor Xu Shen, sentenced to death but spared. **Bingchen / Dingyou:** a commander-in-chief. **Yiwei / Wuyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yimao / Xinsi:** Lin Huan, palace first. **Wuxu / Wuwu:** a provincial graduate. **Wushen / Dingsi:** distinguished; no sons. **Yiwei / Bingxu:** ordinary fortunes, violent death. **Jisi / Bingzi:** a remonstrance official. **Guiyou / Gengshen:** a metropolitan graduate serving in a southern ministry. **Gengchen / Bingxu:** a censor.〉

**Yihai day.** A blood disorder. Hai, Zi, Mao, Wei, and Yin months bring noble encounters and developing blessing. Wealth exposed in the stems lets Hurting Officer generate Wealth and is particularly auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiwei / Wuzi:** Grand Secretary Zhang Fujing; another account gives Bingzi hour. **Guiwei / Wuwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengwu / Jimao:** an imperial relative.〉[^66]

〈The Encounter of Withered Wood:  
Spring arrives, and leaves are born again.  
Only in later years does it gain its ground;  
Flowers open into renewed splendor.〉

> A Yi day at Bingxu hour: the Fire Storehouse  
> Conceals Xin; meeting Chou brings auspicious flourishing.  
> If luck instead encounters ominous control and harm,  
> Reckon this fate as ordinary for the time.

> A Yi day encounters Bingxu;  
> Hurting Officer’s Storehouse—the Wood’s branches wither.  
> Without Xinchou there is no key;  
> Parents and the Six Relations offer little support.  
> The ranks of brothers fly apart in discord;  
> One’s heart grieves and grows distant from others.  
> Know that blessing develops and the family’s standing changes:  
> This fate is bitter first and sweet afterward.

## 24. Yi Days, Dinghai Hour
### 六乙日丁亥時斷
<!-- source: L1058-L1105 -->

> A Yi day born at Dinghai hour:  
> Food God and Seal-Ribbon are indeed exceptional.  
> The month’s qi is Water and Earth; without Wealth, distinction:  
> Above all, beware injury to the wife and calamity for the children.[^67]

On a Yi day at Dinghai hour, Birth is encountered at Death. Yi Wood dies at Hai, but Ren Water provides generating qi as Seal-Ribbon. Yi takes Ding as Food; Ding sits without qi at Hai, so Jia Wood generating and assisting Ding Food is welcome and gives blessing. A Metal configuration followed by Water luck requires guarding against eye disease. Wealth seen in the Four Pillars, or Wealth luck, gives Coveting Wealth and Damaging Seal and indicates loss of wealth. Wu is Wealth and wife; Geng is Officer and child. At Hai, Geng is extinguished and Earth ill: the wife declines and children are few.

**Yichou day.** Refinement. Birth in Renzi, Shen, Wei, or Mao month, with Wealth and Seal exposed in the stems, gives talent and virtue together and surveillance office. Pure Metal in year and month’s stems and branches makes the body decline and Killings flourish: violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Renzi:** Grand Secretary Peng Hua. **Yiwei / Jichou:** a vice minister. **Gengxu / Wuzi:** rich; four sons. **Xinsi / Gengzi:** died in a fall. **Jichou / Dingchou:** died from poison. **Renshen / Wushen:** a metropolitan graduate; a county magistrate shared the chart. **Jiayin / Bingzi:** Vice Minister Zhang Pan, from Shanxi.〉

**Yimao day.** Si, You, and Chou months give Indirect Officer; Shen gives Direct Officer: all bring distinction. Hai month with southeast luck gives surveillance office. Wei month completes the triple Wood configuration and gives great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Xinwei:** a chancellor. **Wushen / Wuwu:** a school instructor. **Renwu / Jiachen:** a provincial graduate. **Xinmao / Wuxu:** a vice court minister. **Gengzi / Bingxu:** Xing Jie, Minister of War in the southern capital, from Shandong. **Xinmao / Gengzi:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Yisi day.** Auspicious. Mao month with northwest luck gives fifth rank. Communication with the month’s qi and southern luck give moderate distinction. Ding and Ren exposed in year and month, with Mao, You, Yin, and Chen in the branches, give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingyou / Renyin:** Grand Secretary Zhai Luan. **Dingsi / Jiachen:** a chief councillor. **Yiwei / Jimao:** a subprefect. **Guiyou / Xinyou:** Hai Rui, provincial graduate and chief censor in the southern capital, famed for integrity and forthrightness; no sons. **Jimao / Wuchen:** a military commissioner. **Guiyou / Jiazi:** a provincial graduate.〉[^68]

**Yiwei day.** Distinguished. Zi and Hai in year and month give a duke or marquis. Spring birth with western luck gives a bureau directorship. You gives solitary distinction. Wood and Fire in year and month give high examination success. Water, Earth, and Metal combining and transforming usefully with the day stem are all auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengxu / Jichou:** Hanlin scholar Huang Zuo. **Renyin / Bingwu:** Wang Wenzhuo, palace first. **Guiwei / Yichou:** Li Jie of the Ministry of Personnel. **Wuwu / Guihai:** Supervising Secretary Yin Xiang. **Gengzi / Wuyin:** Supervising Secretary Zhang Shu. **Yihai / Dinghai:** a marquis or earl. **Gengzi / Wuyin:** a provincial graduate. **Renshen / Guimao:** a provincial graduate. **Renshen / Renzi:** a palace official.〉

**Yiyou day.** A month communicating with the Metal configuration gives great distinction in Water luck. Communication with Wood qi brings advancement; with Earth qi, satisfaction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingshen / Gengyin:** a minister. **Bingchen / Dingyou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Guiwei / Jiwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiazi / Renshen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiwei / Yimao:** a provincial graduate. **Xinchou / Xinmao:** distinction first, punishment afterward. **Renshen / Jiachen:** a supervising secretary for Justice. **Gengshen / Bingxu:** Yang Shuzhong, governor of Yun. **Guisi / Guihai:** entered through selected tribute, served in a southern ministry and as prefect.〉

**Yihai day.** Wealth with self-punishment. Yin and Mao strengthen the body; Wealth exposed in the heavenly stems gives riches. Chen and Chou with Metal-and-Fire luck give distinction. Hai, Zi, and Shen make Officer and Seal doubly clear; Wealth added as assistance gives great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Jihai:** a minister. **Wuzi / Renxu:** a minister. **Gengshen / Bingxu:** Minister Zhang Shiche. **Xinhai / Gengzi:** a vice court minister. **Yichou / Dinghai:** a prefect. **Bingyin / Dingyou:** a provincial graduate. **Yimao / Jimao:** rich. **Yichou / Dinghai:** Wu Yuanyou, prefect of Dongchang, promoted to deputy commissioner, forthright and trustworthy. **Gengxu / Wuzi:** rich, many sons, lost five wives. **Jihai / Bingyin:** rich.〉[^69]

〈Seal and Food Encountered in the Hour:  
Achievement and name cannot be measured.  
Noble people gather in assembly;  
Wealth and distinction sit in the court hall.〉

> Born in an hour of Hai and Ding,  
> Food God and Yi Wood encounter Birth.  
> The month’s qi offering support gives the greatest distinction;  
> A declining body without support makes an ordinary person.

> A Yi day encounters Dinghai hour;  
> Food God and Seal-Ribbon support each other.  
> Birth attains its purpose—how good to be uninjured;  
> Splendor, a pure name, and encounters with the noble.  
> Welcome Ding and Ren transforming qi;  
> Luck approaches Cap and Belt, bringing appointment and promotion.  
> The profound mechanism and subtle method are truly hard to discern;  
> Bing, Si, Yin, and Shen diminish distinction.[^70]

## 25. Bing Days, Wuzi Hour
### 六丙日戊子時斷
<!-- source: L1106-L1153 -->

> A Bing day born at Wuzi hour:  
> Wealth and Officer at Birth and flourishing encounter Food God.  
> The month’s qi offering support gives the greatest distinction;  
> A declining body without support makes an ordinary person.

On a Bing day at Wuzi hour, Officer flourishes and Wealth is born. Bing takes Xin as Wealth and Gui as Officer; Bing combines with Xin, Wu with Gui. At Zi, Gui flourishes and Xin is born, while Bing Fire lacks qi. Communication with a Fire month and supporting ground gives distinction; without it, poverty and low station. Communication with Wood qi is also auspicious.

**Bingzi day.** Yin, Si, Mao, and Wei months let Wood generate Fire and give great distinction. Winter leaves Bing Fire without qi: poverty and early death. Xu month with Fire-and-Earth luck gives fifth or sixth rank. Dingsi month is avoided for early death; Jiyou month for the loss of household and land and low station. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Xinyou:** Dong Qi, metropolitan first and vice minister. **Dingwei / Bingwu:** Chief Censor Ying Dayou. **Jiwei / Bingzi:** a censor. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** great distinction. **Renshen / Jiyou:** moderate distinction. **Jimao / Guiyou:** the Duke for Perpetuating the Sage. **Xinsi / Wuxu:** Minister Wang Tingzhan. **Dingyou / Renyin:** Chen Yubi, Minister of Revenue in the southern capital; five sons.〉[^71]

**Bingyin day.** Mao and Chou months give pure distinction; Yin and Xu are ordinary. Summer strengthens the body; Water and Metal in the pillars then make it auspicious. Zi month gives Direct Officer and great distinction. Guisi month brings punishment; Guihai month violent death; Jiyou month great defeat. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Yisi:** Supervising Secretary Zou Yingchen. **Renshen / Wushen:** a censor. **Guimao / Jiazi:** a provincial graduate. **Dingyou / Jiachen:** Mao Bozhi, palace first. **Bingshen / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinyou / Yiwei:** a surveillance commissioner. **Jiyou / Yihai:** Censor Cao Xuecheng, pardoned from a capital sentence. **Jimao / Bingzi:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Bingchen day.** Bingchen is the Day-Seal configuration and welcomes Officer Star. Xu month strengthens the body and especially favors military distinction. Yin month with Metal-and-Water luck gives moderate distinction. [Shen] month assembles Killings through Triple Combination; Seal present gives distinction. Jisi month brings violent death; Jihai month death through self-punishment; Guichou month ancestral ruin and violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuchen / Guihai:** a senior provincial administrator. **Yiyou / Jiashen:** a county magistrate. **Guiyou / Xinyou:** an education commissioner.〉[^72]

**Bingwu day.** Bingwu is Day Blade and requires Officer and Killings to restrain or combine with it. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give great wealth; Hai, Mao, Wei, and Yin in year and month give great distinction. Shen and Si give third-rank civil distinction; military distinction does not endure. Pure Zi gives the noble Zi and Wu Doubly Enclosing configuration. Dingsi month brings violent death; Dinghai month self-punishment and violent death; Xinchou month solitude. 〈Recorded cases—**Jichou / Xinwei:** Master Yan, the Second Sage. **Xinwei / Gengyin:** a duke. **Wuxu / Jiwei:** a minister. **Xinyou / Dingyou:** an official’s son who became prefect. **Guihai / Yimao:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Bingshen day.** Si and Wu in year and month give surveillance office in northeast luck. Zi month with Wood-and-Fire luck gives third rank; Chou gives seventh. You and Hai encounter nobility but turn instead to low station. Guisi month brings punishment in middle age; Yiyou month ruin and defeat. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuwu / Jiazi:** Vice Minister Zheng Yue. **Wuchen / Xinyou:** first in the provincial examination. **Xinsi / Jihai:** a provincial graduate. **Xinsi / Bingshen:** a provincial graduate. **Wuzi / Guihai:** a provincial graduate. **Dingwei / Gengxu:** a censor. **Dingyou / Renzi:** Fang Jiugong of the Central Region, succeeded in the Jiazi and Yichou examinations, became vice minister, and died in Wuzi; and Zhong Huamin of Zhejiang, succeeded in the Jimao and Gengchen examinations, became deputy surveillance commissioner, and died in Jiawu.〉[^73]

**Bingxu day.** Spring’s Seal-Ribbon is most auspicious. Summer makes the body excessively strong and gives ordinary fortunes. Autumn strengthens Wealth and weakens the body; with support, distinction. Pure You in year and month gives distinction through literary advancement. Jihai month gives death with the body mutilated; Guichou month poverty and early death. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Yimao:** Minister Liu Baichuan, retired in Dingchou year. **Bingchen / Jihai:** a censor. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jichou / Bingyin:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Dingyou / Renzi:** death by fire.〉

〈Food God Welcoming Horse:  
Wealth qi flourishes in complete measure.  
No Void, clash, or control is offended:  
Talent and name spread far and near.〉

> Livelihood and livelihood’s resources flourish through all four seasons;  
> Bing meeting Wuzi has Food and Officer together.  
> Without injury, everything is accomplished in later years;  
> Misfortune is met in auspicious places, passage found amid danger.

> Bingzi encounters Wuzi hour;  
> Officer Star and Food’s blessing are arrayed together.  
> Until Wu or Ding appears, they remain submerged;  
> Connection in middle age brings great delight.  
> Parents, wife, and children welcome union;  
> Literary talent lies hidden in the breast.  
> Should good luck arrive all at once,  
> Wealth, distinction, and untroubled leisure follow.[^74]

## 26. Bing Days, Jichou Hour
### 六丙日己丑時斷
<!-- source: L1154-L1195 -->
〈The unfavorable months for the six Bing days below follow the preceding rule; the hour is judged with them.〉

> A Bing day born at Jichou hour:  
> Officer-ghost is injured, and Lu is not established.  
> If Shen and Geng appear, with Yi flourishing,  
> One passes through life without needing to seek Wealth and Lu.

On a Bing day at Jichou hour, Hurting Officer turns away from Lu: pride toward others and lofty ambition. Bing takes Gui as Officer; Gui’s residual qi in Chou is injured by visible and hidden Earth. Gui exposed in the pillars brings calamity. Geng or Xin seen lets Hurting Officer generate Wealth and instead brings blessing and celebration. 〈One account says:〉 the configuration of Brightness Emerging above the Earth indicates distinction.[^75]

**Bingzi day.** Yin, Hai, Shen, and Chen in year and month, with Wealth, Seal, and Food exposed in the stems, give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinhai / Gengyin:** Luo Lun, palace first, an eminent official. **Wuyin / Guihai:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Wuchen / Yiwei:** a prefect.〉

**Bingyin day.** Ordinary. Yiyou month gives Direct Wealth; vigorous Yi and Geng bring distinction. Jichou in year and month, with Officer and Seal exposed, gives distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 the Fire-and-Earth image gives distinction, with a blood disorder. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Bingxu:** Chief Censor Xu Jin. **Renchen / Jiyou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Jihai / Wuchen:** a [censor]. **Yiyou / Jichou:** Chen Jin, palace first; died amid mutinous troops in Jiazi. **Dingmao / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingzi / Dingyou:** a commander-in-chief. **Bingxu / Guisi:** a metropolitan graduate, whose father and elder brother were both distinguished. **Wushen / Bingchen:** an imperial son-in-law. **Gengchen / Renwu:** a judicial commissioner. **Bingzi / Jihai:** Lin Yuji, Minister of Rites, and metropolitan graduate Wang Jiayan shared this chart: Lin’s examinations were Guimao and Dingwei, Wang’s Jiawu and Wuxu. Lin was from Fujian, Wang from Jiangyou. **Bingxu / Guisi:** a senior provincial administrator.〉[^76]

**Bingchen day, [Jichou] hour.** Shen and Hai in year and month are auspicious if transformation into Water occurs; without transformation, life is shortened. Xu month clashes open the Storehouse: no one fails to develop. Yin and Wu strengthen the body, completing Blazing Upward and giving great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Bingxu:** a vice minister. **Jiaxu / Guiyou:** a vice minister. **Gengyin / Renwu:** Vice Minister Dong Chuance; violent death. **Wushen / Wuwu:** Yao Lai, palace first and minister. **Jisi / Jiaxu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Dingyou / Dingwei:** Bureau Director Fang Fuyu. **Renyin / Wushen:** Minister Wang Honghui.〉[^77]

**Bingwu day.** Spring birth with Fire-and-Metal luck reaches the highest rank. Summer is ordinary; autumn wealthy; winter distinguished. Wife and children are difficult. Wu and You in year and month give fifth or sixth rank: this demonstrates the month’s Lu generating Wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiachen / Jiaxu:** an administration commissioner. **Gengwu / Jichou:** a censor. **Dingchou / Wushen:** a provincial graduate. **Renshen / Guimao:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Bingshen day.** A blood disorder. Shen month gives literary learning and a scholarly post. [Xu] and Mao give distinction; Zi and Chen assemble Officer, Yin and Mao assemble Seal: all are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Dingchou:** first in the provincial examination. **Gengzi / Yiyou:** a censor. **Renyin / Renyin:** Zhao Huan, Minister of Personnel. **Wuyin / Bingchen:** a provincial graduate. **Bingzi / Gengyin:** received an honorific patent.〉[^78]

**Bingxu day.** High military standing; advancement follows punishment. Hai or Mao month with Fire-and-Metal luck gives great distinction. Chen and Wei complete the Four Storehouses; Fire and Earth form a configuration of great wealth. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 the six Bing days seeing Jichou hour often indicate blood disorders. 〈Recorded cases—**Yimao / Bingxu:** an administration commissioner. **Dingmao / Guichou:** Yu, Minister of Personnel. **Xinwei / Xinchou:** a county magistrate. **Wuchen / Jiwei:** rich.〉

〈Hurting Officer upon the Hour:  
Splendor does not endure.  
For the common person, leaving the ancestral home is auspicious;  
For the gentleman, honor flourishes abroad.〉

> On a Bing day, Wealth and Officer lie hidden in the Storehouse;  
> Xu, Chen, and Wei reveal literary accomplishment.  
> If the body declines and there is no key,  
> Seeking name and profit yields only ordinary results.

> A Bing day encounters Jichou hour;  
> Hurting Officer and Wealth Storehouse are hidden within.  
> Luck reaching Wei and Xu is no ordinary thing;  
> Wealth and Officer broken forth must flourish.  
> Near the noble, schemes to seize and Rob Wealth arise;  
> Reckoning it, some injury must follow.  
> True or false kinship brings little harmony;  
> Do not blame a frank judgment made according to the hour.

## 27. Bing Days, Gengyin Hour
### 六丙日庚寅時斷
<!-- source: L1196-L1243 -->

> A Bing day born at Gengyin hour:  
> The Hall of Learning’s generating qi assists the body.  
> Combination in luck communicates with the Metal configuration:  
> Surely a person of splendor, wealth, and distinction.

On a Bing day at Gengyin hour, generating qi occupies the Hall of Learning. Bing is born at Yin, giving refined qi for literature. Bing takes Geng and Xin as Wealth; at Yin, Geng is extinguished and Bing flourishes. If the month’s qi communicates with a Metal configuration, Wealth flourishes and both wealth and distinction follow. Western luck is welcome. Without communication with the configuration, Wealth is slight.

**Bingzi day.** Zi-month birth brings proximity to the noble. Guiyou month with Water-and-Wood luck gives high distinction; Fire-and-Wood luck gives fifth rank or above. Wei, Shen, Gui, or Wu in year and month gives great distinction in military office, but a short span. 〈Recorded cases—**Yimao / Wuzi:** a chancellor. **Dingsi / Renyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiyou / Guiyou:** a provincial administrator. **Wuyin / Jiwei:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Wuzi / Jiazi:** Supervising Secretary Wu Shilai; two remonstrance memorials brought a beating that nearly killed him. After several years of frontier exile he was recalled by edict and rose to chief censor. **Guichou / Xinmao:** a subprefect. **Dingsi / Bingwu:** Education Commissioner Yang Tingyun.〉[^79]

**Bingyin day.** Distinction does not last. You and Shen in year and month give a hereditary descendant an obscure post. Zi, Chou, Yin, and Wei give manifest distinction; pure Yin is especially auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Yichou:** a minister. **Guiwei / Jiayin:** a vice minister. **Gengwu / Yiyou:** first in the provincial examination; died. **Guisi / Jiazi:** provincial first and metropolitan graduate. **Dingwei / Renzi:** great distinction. **Jiaxu / Yihai:** minor distinction. **Renchen / Renzi:** inherited protection and wealth. **Gengchen / Wuzi:** a commander of a thousand households; violent death.〉

**Bingchen day.** Yin, Wu, Xu, or Wei in year and month gives a worthy wife, filial children, and wealth and distinction together. Shen and Zi with northern luck give great distinction; You and Chou give wealth. 〈One account says:〉 high standing throughout. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Renzi:** Minister Gan Weilin. **Xinmao / Gengzi:** a prefect. **Guimao / Wuwu:** a provincial graduate. **Xinchou / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinchou / Gengzi:** a bureau director. **Renyin / Renyin:** an assistant surveillance commissioner.〉

**Bingwu day.** Without Ren, Gui, Zi, Wei, or Si in year and month, Flying Heavenly Lu and Horse gives distinction. Si, You, Chou, and Shen indicate literary learning and either rank or wealth. Wei month gives Hurting Officer; Chen month poverty followed by riches; Hai month manifest distinction in western luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Bingchen:** Minister Fei Cai. **Jimao / Bingzi:** Chief Censor Liu Siwen. **Xinchou / Guisi:** a bureau director. **Dinghai / Wushen:** a minister. **Jiaxu / Bingyin:** a chancellor. **Dingyou / Guichou:** an expositor-in-waiting. **Guisi / Yichou:** Minister Fan Qian. **Jiyou / Dingchou:** a commander-in-chief.〉

**Bingshen day.** The two configurations Hai–Mao–Wei and Shen–Zi–Chen make Officer and Seal both flourish: great distinction. Si–You–Chou is an auspicious Wealth configuration. Yin–Wu–Xu, the body’s own configuration, is ordinary. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiwei / Jiashen:** Zeng Cong, palace first. **Gengshen / Wuzi:** a minister. **Dinghai / Xinhai:** a vice minister. **Jimao / Dingmao:** a privy councillor. **Jiwei / Renshen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Wuwu / Jichou:** a senior provincial administrator. **Renxu / Renyin:** a censor. **Renchen / Guimao:** Wang Duo, Minister of Rites. **Jiayin / Xinwei:** a provincial graduate.〉[^80]

**Bingxu day.** Hai and Zi months give manifest distinction. Shen and You in year and month with northern luck, or Yin–Wu–Xu with Officer-ghost luck, give great distinction. If luck approaches Death or Extinction, entry to the Yellow Springs is beyond doubt. 〈Recorded cases—**Wushen / Wuwu:** a minister. **Xinhai / Jiawu:** Vice Minister Gu Sui. **Guiyou / Renxu:** an administration commissioner. **Jiayin / Jiaxu:** Chief Censor Zhu Wan; died by hanging. **Guihai / Wuwu:** a court minister. **Guihai / Gengyin:** a court minister. **Guichou / Dingsi:** “Jia ministry” [title uncertain].〉[^81]

〈Wealth Spirit upon the Hour:  
Metal born, blessing is beyond measure.  
Ten years hidden beneath the study window,  
Then purpose fulfilled and a fragrant name.〉

> Bing and Geng join at Yin hour;  
> Danger and difficulty vanish, blessing follows naturally.  
> Luck arrives: a humble house produces a famed minister or general;  
> The time comes: one walks level-footed up the cloud ladder.

> Judge a Bing day at Gengyin hour thus:  
> Parents decline or flourish; the person leaves home.  
> Wife and children are injured early, honored late;  
> The White Tiger returns to the mountain in full strength.  
> Wood forms a forest of pine and cypress;  
> Livelihood gathers wealth and grain in abundance.  
> Gold and jade pile high in the great hall;  
> All admire what people call an exemplary life.[^82]

## 28. Bing Days, Xinmao Hour
### 六丙日卯時斷
<!-- source: L1244-L1285 -->

> A Bing day born at Xinmao hour:  
> Flourishing Wood, two wives, and a clever person.  
> Without strength, transformation into Water enters Death’s country;  
> Sexual desire follows the body, with many fond pursuits.

On a Bing day at Xinmao hour, defeated Wealth encounters combination. Bing is at Bath at Mao and sees Xin as the combining spirit. If the body is very strong and cannot transform, the person is merely discourteous and covetous of sexual pleasure; the outcome is nevertheless good. A weak body transforming into Water finds Water dead at Mao: refinement without substance, a person [practiced in] cunning and deceit. Only Bingwu or Bingyin born in a spring month, with a strong body that does not transform, gives manifest literary distinction.[^83]

**Bingzi day.** Zi and Mao punish one another, injuring wife and children. Identical year and month give first-place examination success and distinction as a close attendant. Yin, Wu, Chou, and Xu, with heavenly stems and earthly branches both combining, give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Guichou / Renxu:** Minister Zheng Ji. **Bingzi / Xinmao:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Bingshen / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinyou / Bingshen:** a court minister.〉

**Bingyin day.** No ancestral support; self-establishment and bodily illness. Yin, Mao, Wei, and Zi months give distinction; the other months are ordinary. Years and luck follow the same rule. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiwei / Jiaxu:** a censor. **Jiwei / Yihai:** great distinction; violent death. **Yiwei / Dinghai:** a prefect. **Gengxu / Bingxu:** a provincial graduate. **Jisi / Bingyin:** a provincial graduate. **Bingchen / Renchen:** a metropolitan graduate and county magistrate, removed from office in Yiwei. **Bingshen / Xinchou:** an official in the Personnel administration. **Bingshen / Xinmao:** Hanlin scholar Xi Kongjiao.〉 〈For this day, Wu and Xu in year and month forming the configuration are judged as Blazing Upward.〉

**Bingchen day.** Yin and Xu months give Heavenly and Monthly Virtue and high standing. Si month with northern luck gives distinction, as do You and Chou. Hai–Mao–Wei gives great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Guihai / Guihai:** Hanlin Compiler Ji Shichen. **Xinsi / Xinchou:** Vice Censor-in-Chief Chang Daoli. **Guiyou / Yichou:** an official in the judicial administration.〉

**Bingwu day.** Gui Water Officer Star in year and month removing Blade is auspicious. Zi month injures wife and children. Yin and You give an unyielding nature that will not endure affront, with third- or fourth-rank distinction. Wu and Xu with southeast luck, or Mao month with northwest luck, give distinction. 〈One account says:〉 loss amid flourishing. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Bingwu:** Vice Minister Wang Yu; Yang Blade unrestrained, violent death. **Dingyou / Yisi:** Zhang, palace first; the Five Phases Returning to Lu. **Dingwei / Jiyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** a chancellor. **Jiayin / Wuchen:** a Grand Preceptor. **Jiayin / Guiyou:** chancellor of the Imperial Academy. **Dingyou / Yisi:** Chen Dong, metropolitan first and palace third.〉[^84]

**Bingshen day.** Obstruction. Intelligence and a fondness for wine and sex. A strong body that does not transform gives distinction. Spring is auspicious; winter with northern luck gives both wealth and distinction. Ji and Chou in year and month, with eastern luck, give second rank; Wu and Wei give third. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Dingwei:** Zhang Gun, chief minister of the Court of Imperial Sacrifices. **Wuxu / Xinyou:** a junior provincial administrator. **Bingwu / Xinmao:** an academy assistant. **Guiyou / Jiazi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiwei / Guihai:** a junior provincial administrator. **Gengzi / Wuyin:** Xu Sanwei, supreme commander and minister. **Jiaxu / Jiaxu:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Bingxu day.** Injury to wife and children. A strong body that does not transform gives distinction. Spring gives intelligence and a taste for wine and sex. Winter with western luck gives wealth and distinction; summer surveillance office. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Fire-and-Wood transformation gives abundant blessing. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Dinghai:** Yao [Ye], palace first. **Guichou / Renxu:** a vice minister. **Guiyou / Jiazi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuchen / Xinyou:** a provincial graduate. **Renchen / Renyin:** rich and long-lived. **Guisi / Yimao:** Minister Chu Tie. **Bingyin / Jihai:** Grand Secretary Han Guang.〉[^85]

〈Bing and Xin Transforming into Water:  
A weak body finds blessing difficult.  
Official disputes obstruct and entangle;  
The common person meets many reversals.〉

> Bing and Xin transforming into Water do not balance well;  
> With assistance and a strong body, great auspiciousness comes.  
> Clash, control, and breaking in the Four Pillars:  
> Toiling in mind and strength, one passes the years.

> A Bing day encounters Xinmao hour;  
> Coveting Wealth and Damaging Seal makes achievement difficult.  
> Wealth-and-Officer luck reveals a famous name;  
> A weak body leaves the disposition unsettled.  
> Parents and the Six Relations cannot be relied upon;  
> Only standing forth and changing the ancestral establishment brings success.  
> The ranks of brothers each look to their own future;  
> With breaking, this remains an ordinary fate.

## 29. Bing Days, Renchen Hour
### 六丙日壬辰時斷
<!-- source: L1286-L1333 -->

> A Bing day born at Renchen hour:  
> Killings Star sits in the Storehouse; Fire cannot draw near.  
> A strong body instead gives official distinction;  
> A weak one surely makes a person poor and short-lived.

On a Bing day at Renchen hour, Water and Fire have not achieved completion. Bing sees Ren as Indirect Officer. Ren Water forms a configuration at Chen; Fire dies and loses its light. Spring or summer birth makes the body strong, transforming ghost into Officer; body-strengthening luck then gives distinction. Autumn and winter make the body decline and ghost flourish. Without further support, there are poverty, low station, and disability.

**Bingzi day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months restrain Indirect Officer and are auspicious. Hai and Mao in year and month give wealth and distinction. Yin and Wu with Zi luck, or Zi with Yin-and-Wu luck, give distinction; otherwise, a monk or Daoist. 〈One account says:〉 wealth attracts disputes. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingchou / Renyin:** a chancellor. **Guichou / Renxu:** a prefect. **Bingshen / Gengzi:** a provincial graduate. **Gengwu / Bingxu:** minor distinction. **Dingchou / Dinghai:** blind in both eyes. **Xinmao / Bingshen:** Ruan Xuqing, a supervising secretary for Personnel, banished for bribery. **Gengshen / Wuyin:** a censor. **Xinsi / Gengzi:** Grand Secretary Yang Jingchen.〉

**Bingyin day.** Body and Killings both flourish. Yin, Mao, Chen, Chou, and Wei in year and month give great distinction. Si, Wu, and Xu in year and month also give distinction. 〈One account says:〉 “[enmity and injustice], greatly ominous.” 〈Recorded cases—**Renyin / Dingwei:** Grand Secretary Xia Yan; violent death, no sons. **Yimao / Jichou:** Wu Kuan, palace first. **Wuyin / Jimao:** first in the provincial examination. **Renwu / Jiyou:** a vice minister. **Yiwei / Wuyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengchen / Bingxu:** a prefectural judge. **Jichou / Dingmao:** a provincial graduate. **Yimao / Guiwei:** immensely rich. **Dingwei / Jiyou:** a bureau official appointed through inherited favor.〉[^86]

**Bingchen day.** Solitary, with wealth, and a violent death indicated. Spring birth with northern luck or summer birth with eastern luck gives distinction. Autumn with southern luck reaches third rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinsi / Gengyin:** Remonstrance Official Xu Tianxi. **Yiwei / Jichou:** Minister Yang Yikui. **Bingchen / Bingshen:** Minister Pan Huang. **Jiayin / Jiaxu:** vice minister of the Court of Imperial Sacrifices. **Jisi / Yihai:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Guiwei / Xinyou:** a prefect.〉

**Bingwu day.** Distinguished: the body flourishes and Killings [flourishes]. Chen, Xu, Chou, or Wei month restraining Indirect Officer gives distinction; without restraint, ordinary fortunes. 〈Recorded cases—**Renxu / Guimao:** chief minister of the Court of Imperial Entertainments. **Renwu / Dingwei:** a ministry secretary. **Bingwu / Xinchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renyin / Renyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiayin / Guiyou:** a vice minister. **Jisi / Dingmao:** a court assistant. **Jiayin / Bingzi:** Minister Xu Honggang. **Xinmao / Xinchou:** a vice general.〉[^87]

**Bingshen day.** Calamity amid flourishing. Spring is ordinary; summer fortunate; autumn wealthy; winter shortens life. Shen–Zi–Chen forms a [Water] configuration; Seal and Companions exposed in the stems to assist give great distinction. Food restraining Killings also gives distinction. Exposed Killings without restraint is unwelcome; Wealth siding with and strengthening Killings gives premature, unnatural death. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Renshen:** Shi Chong; although his wealth rivaled a kingdom, he died an unnatural death. **Yiyou / Jiashen:** County Magistrate Qian Zhong, died in the Wo-raider calamity. **Guisi / Jichou:** Ni Yuanlu, Hanlin scholar and minister, died in the Chongzhen calamity. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** a vice court minister. **Wuzi / Bingchen:** a ministry secretary. **Bingzi / Bingshen:** a censor. **Guihai / Jiazi:** the Prince of Zhou in Henan, a worthy man. **Dingwei / Renzi:** wandered and died away from home.〉[^88]

**Bingxu day.** Ominous. Mao and Wei in year and month, with Fire-and-Earth luck, give third-rank office, a worthy wife, and filial children. Chen, Xu, and Chou months give stability; Yin, Wu, Zi, and Si in year and month give surveillance office. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingxu / Wuxu:** Minister Wang Hong. **Guimao / Dingsi:** a chief censor. **Gengwu / Wuzi:** a censor. **Jisi / Gengwu:** a county magistrate. **Jiazi / Renshen:** chief minister of the Court of Pasturage. **Jiashen / Jiaxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuxu / Jiazi:** a provincial graduate. **Bingxu / Yiwei:** a censor. **Jimao / Dingchou:** a censor.〉

〈Chen as Officer’s Storehouse:  
Wife and children are not preserved in completeness.  
Even though Wealth and Lu flourish,  
Leaving the ancestral home avoids entanglement in calamity.〉

> A Bing day at Renchen fears seeing Shen;  
> Yang Water encountered again ensures trouble.  
> If Yin, Wu, and Xu appear in the pillars,  
> Misfortune turns to good and distinction is beyond compare.

> A Bing day, Renchen hour, at the Tomb:  
> The body declines and is depleted; ghost holds the road.  
> The ranks of brothers offer no support;  
> Why should wife and children suffer a bond gone wrong?  
> For the gentleman, literary talent receives blessing’s help;  
> For the common person, kindness turns instead to distance.  
> Luck through Officer and Lu permits the pursuit of plans;  
> Without breaking, there is either distinction or wealth.

## 30. Bing Days, Guisi Hour
### 六丙日癸巳時斷
<!-- source: L1334-L1377 -->

> A Bing day born at Guisi hour:  
> The day’s Lu returns to the hour and encounters Officer as well.  
> No Ji, Yin, Ren, or Gui in the month:  
> How could achievement, ready to hand, be difficult to obtain?[^89]

On a Bing day at Guisi hour, the day’s Lu returns to the hour. Bing Fire at Si sees Gui as Direct Officer seated upon Noble. Without Ren or Ji in the pillars, and without Yin and Hai to punish or clash, there is distinction; with them, there is not. Officer communicating with flourishing Water and Bing communicating with flourishing Wood invariably give distinction.

**Bingzi day.** Bing’s Lu is at Si, Gui’s at Zi: Lu and Horse exchange places. Without Ren, Ji, Yin, and Hai in year and month to clash or break, the person becomes a close attendant or surveillance official, reaching duke or marquis. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 transformation into Fire gives blessing; wine is unsuitable. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengyin / Yiyou:** Minister Liang Cai, an eminent official. **Gengzi / Bingxu:** Chief Censor Wu Zhang. **Jiayin / Renshen:** a Grand Secretary. **Wuzi / Guihai:** a censor. **Renchen / Gengxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuwu / Renxu:** transmission commissioner in the southern capital. **Guiyou / Guihai:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Bingyin day.** Spring birth, with no Water in stems and branches, gives literary advancement and embroidered robes, honoring the wife and sheltering the children. Mao, Xu, Shen, and You in year and month give second or third rank. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Fire transformation gives distinction but no long life; wine is unsuitable. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Gengxu:** Minister Ni Yue, an eminent official. **Guisi / Yichou:** a censor. **Renyin / Gengxu:** a vice censor-in-chief. **Xinmao / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Bingchen day.** Unfavorable to the ancestors. You, Xu, Yin, and Chou in year and month give the Kuigang configuration; communication with bodily strength gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Guiwei:** Chief Censor Zhu Ji. **Wuchen / Gengshen:** Chief Censor Kang Lang. **Gengzi / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinwei / Jiawu:** a provincial graduate. **Dingwei / Wushen:** a provincial graduate. **Renchen / Bingwu:** a Grand Preceptor. **Gengyin / Wuzi:** a metropolitan graduate who died in that same year.〉[^90]

**Bingwu day.** Chou and Chen months give Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer and manifest distinction. Yin month gives Bing’s Birth; Si month its Established Lu. Wealth and Seal exposed in the heavenly stems bring great distinction. Wine should be avoided. Zi strengthens Officer, You strengthens Wealth: both are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Renxu / Jiyou:** Vice Minister Lin Tingji. **Gengwu / Jichou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Bingwu / Gengyin:** a prefect. **Guichou / Dingsi:** a duke. **Jiachen / Jisi:** a commander-in-chief. **Xinwei / Guisi:** a provincial graduate; died young.〉

**Bingshen day.** The body sits on Indirect Officer and Indirect Wealth: if not distinguished, then rich. 〈One account says:〉 obscurity while still in the world. Si, Wu, and Wei strengthen the body and are most auspicious. Yin, Mao, and Chen strengthen Seal; Hai, Zi, and Chou strengthen Officer: all are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Yiwei:** Chief Censor Zhang Gao. **Yisi / Dinghai:** Chief Censor Shen Jiao. **Bingyin / Gengyin:** Supervising Secretary Huang Zonggai. **Bingzi / Renchen:** a provincial graduate. **Yichou / Bingxu:** a provincial graduate. **Dingwei / Bingwu:** great distinction. **Bingyin / Renchen:** minor distinction; died. **Dingyou / Wushen:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Bingxu day.** Mao, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give distinction that does not last. Yin and Hai in year and month give surveillance office. Clash and punishment are unwelcome, and wine should be avoided. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Jiaxu:** Minister Wang Qiong. **Dinghai / Xinhai:** a censor. **Yimao / Guiwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiawu / Dingmao:** first in the provincial examination. **Xinmao / Gengzi:** a provincial graduate. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈The Day’s Lu Returning to the Hour:  
Even meeting Officer, Lu spreads abundantly.  
The time arrives without obstruction;  
Wealth and distinction need no laborious pursuit.〉

> A Bing day truly meets Guisi hour;  
> Called Upright Nobility, it welcomes intimacy.  
> No clash or breaking in the year and month:  
> Surely a person of splendor, wealth, and distinction.

> A Bing day encounters Guisi hour;  
> Direct Officer, Lu, and Horse are rare and exceptional.  
> Reckoning it, wife and children are difficult in early years;  
> Clash and control of Officer and Lu are most forbidden.  
> A gentleman’s literary name rises above the crowd;  
> A common person has Wealth and Lu to spare.  
> Gold and white jade emerge from sunken mud;  
> Luck and the appointed time arrive, and abundance gathers.[^91]

## 31. Bing Days, Jiawu Hour
### 六丙日甲午時斷
<!-- source: L1378-L1411 -->

> A Bing day born at Jiawu hour:  
> The configuration enters Concealed Brilliance and needs Earth.  
> Wu and Ji encountered gladly are most auspicious;  
> Fire blazing to excess brings much hardship.

On a Bing day at Jiawu hour, Bing Fire at Wu is excessively strong. Ji must appear to combine with Jia and transform into Earth: Fire’s qi conceals its brilliance, shining clearly in all directions. Without Earth, blessing is difficult to enjoy. Birth in Chen, Xu, Chou, or Wei month lets [Earth] conceal Fire’s qi and brings the creative process to balance. Wu or Ji exposed in year and month, followed by Metal-and-Water luck, gives distinction. Even without exposure, luck communicating with Earth qi gives distinction. In every case, punishment and breaking are avoided.[^92]

**Bingzi day.** Spring is auspicious; summer lacks support; autumn strengthens Wealth; winter gives noble Officer. Children are few and achievement late. Fire-and-Earth luck brings advancement. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Renshen:** Mo, palace first. **Renzi / Wushen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiawu / Gengwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuwu / Wuwu:** a commander of a thousand households. **Xinmao / Bingshen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuchen / Jiwei:** Wen Yinglu, palace third.〉

**Bingyin day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months let [Earth] conceal Fire’s qi and bring the creative process to balance: distinction. Wu month makes Fire excessively strong and is ominous. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiyou / Jiazi:** Grand Secretary Ma Ziqiang. **Jiachen / Gengwu:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Bingchen day.** Yin and Mao assist through Seal; Shen and You strengthen Wealth; Si and Wu strengthen the body; Hai and Zi strengthen Officer. All are auspicious. Nevertheless Fire and Earth are essential; without them, blessing is slight. Wei, Xu, Chen, and Chou are auspicious when seen. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Bingwu:** Minister Lin Ting'ang. **Jimao / Bingyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiaxu / Guiyou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Renshen / Gengxu:** a censor. **Yiyou / Guiwei:** a chief censor. **Jiaxu / Jisi:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Bingwu day.** Yin, Wu, and Xu months are judged as Reversed Clash and give second or third rank. Zi month with southern luck gives eighth or ninth rank. Yin month with southern luck gives gold and purple; Wu month with eastern luck gives distinction as a close attendant. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuxu / Jiayin:** a chancellor. **Jiayin / Gengwu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Yichou / Renwu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiaxu / Guiyou:** a censor. **Guiwei / Xinyou:** a provincial graduate. **Guiwei / Jiayin:** an associate prefect. **Renyin / Gengxu:** a bureau director. **Wuxu / Wuwu:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Bingshen day.** Without Fire-and-Earth qi, blessing is slight. Metal-and-Water luck gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Jiaxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiyou / Yihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yihai / Bingxu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Yihai / Jiashen:** first in the provincial examination.〉

**Bingxu day.** Spring birth is auspicious; summer brings solitude and injury; autumn is auspicious but children are few.
