Yin, Wu, and Xu months give wealth and distinction surpassing the crowd: an immortal-like chancellor. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Guiyou:** Wang Xijue, palace second and Grand Secretary. **Renzi / Jiyou:** Wu Mo, metropolitan first and transmission commissioner. **Jichou / Dingchou:** Jin Lijian, in the Personnel administration. **Jiawu / Dingmao:** “Jia ministry” [office uncertain].〉

〈Adding Fuel to Blazing Fire:  
Amid anxiety, Lu develops.  
Keeping the old livelihood is unsuitable;  
Leaving the ancestral home instead creates blessing.〉

> A Bing day at Wu hour has little Water calamity;  
> It is like adding fuel to blazing Fire.  
> Fire appears in the pillars without punishment or breaking:  
> Do not doubt splendor in middle and later years.

> A Bing day encounters Jiawu hour;  
> Rob Wealth, Blade, and Hurting Officer fill the pillars.  
> Wood declining and Fire flourishing turns it to ash;  
> Only a season-ending monthly framework gives distinction.  
> The gentleman passes in and out with success;  
> The common person preserving ancestral property meets many calamities.  
> The Six Relations and blood kin have little harmony;  
> Undertakings meet success and failure.[^93]

## 32. Bing Days, Yiwei Hour
### 六丙日乙未時斷
<!-- source: L1412-L1455 -->

> A Bing day born at Yiwei hour:  
> Birth in a Fire month often brings wealth and distinction.  
> Yi as Direct Seal is encountered in the configuration;

On a Bing day at Yiwei hour there is generating qi and Seal-Ribbon. Bing regards Wei as Seal’s Storehouse. Communication with a Fire month gives distinction. Even without such communication, a chart without Wealth and without Wealth luck is judged a high fate. Wealth encountered gives Coveting Wealth and Damaging Seal and indicates ordinary fortunes. **Bingchen day.** Spring is auspicious; summer stable; autumn laborious; winter gives manifest distinction. Shen–Zi–Chen supplies Officer Star to restrain Blade; Hai–Mao–Wei supplies Seal-Ribbon to transform Blade: both are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Jiaxu:** fifth-rank office. **Yimao / Wuzi:** blind. **Bingyin / Gengyin:** blind.〉

**Bingshen day.** Even without communication with Fire qi, absence of Wealth Star permits judgment as a good fate. Zi and Chen in year and month assemble Officer; Hai and Mao assemble Seal: both give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Jiyou:** the Ming Guangzong emperor, whose reign title was Taichang. **Renzi / Renyin:** a prefect. **Jiazi / Bingzi:** chief minister of the Court of Pasturage. **Dingmao / Bingwu:** a provincial graduate. **Guisi / Xinyou:** a provincial graduate. **Jiachen / Bingzi:** Minister Wang Yikui. **Jiayin / Yihai:** a bureau director in the Ministry of Rites. **Wuwu / Yichou:** Feng Qi, Minister of Rites.〉[^94]

**Bingxu day.** Shen, Zi, and Chen in year and month give distinction as a close attendant; Yin, Mao, and Si in year and month give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Guisi / Yimao:** Gu Dingchen, palace first and Grand Secretary. **Dingyou / Gengxu:** a person subjected to punishment.〉

〈Direct Seal at Wei Hour:  
Those encountering it are noble beyond doubt.

> The ranks of brothers each take flight as circumstances lead.  
> If luck travels east and north,  
> Lifelong food and emolument naturally lack nothing.

> Luck traveling northeast brings splendor.

## 33. Bing Days, Bingshen Hour
### 六丙日丙申時斷
<!-- source: L1456-L1499 -->

> A Bing day born at Bingshen hour:  
> The body declines, Wealth is broken, Companions divide it.  
> Hidden ghost flourishes in Seven Killings’ ground;  
> Without rescue, how could the household prosper?

On a Bing day at Bingshen hour, Wealth flourishes and the body declines. Bing encounters Geng as Wealth and Ren as ghost. At Shen, Geng flourishes and Ren is born; Bing Fire lacks qi, and Wealth must be divided and seized by Companions. Without communication with a body-strengthening month, there is poverty and low station. Support and rescue, communication with a body-strengthening month, and corresponding luck give distinction.

**Bingzi day.** Communication with Fire qi, or Yin and Mao months, followed by body-strengthening luck, is auspicious. Pure Metal in year and month gives Abandoning the Fate and Following Wealth, also judged auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiyou / Gengshen:** Jia Sidao, a treacherous minister. **Gengzi / Jimao:** a chief councillor. **Jichou / Dingchou:** rich. **Jimao / Renshen:** an imperial relative.〉

**Bingyin day.** Hour and day stand opposite and clash, threatening wife and children. A month communicating with Fire qi and body-strengthening luck are auspicious. Hai month gives an artisan. 〈One account says:〉 loss amid flourishing, defeat and death. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinmao / Jiawu:** an assistant surveillance commissioner, died young; another assistant commissioner shared the chart. **Guihai / Xinyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengzi / Xinsi:** Tian Yijun, metropolitan first and vice minister. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** Dai Shiheng, supervising secretary for Personnel, banished to frontier service. **Yiyou / Guiwei:** military distinction.〉

**Bingchen day.** Yin month with southern luck gives distinction. Zi completes the triple Officer configuration; Seal exposed in the heavenly stems gives great distinction. Hai birth with Ren exposed often shortens life. Xu and Wei are ordinary. 〈Recorded case—**Renzi / Gengxu:** Zhang Jingxiu, son of Zhang Juzheng, a metropolitan graduate, dismissed.〉

**Bingwu day.** Bloodshed and Fire calamity, followed by great development. Si-month birth with Geng and Xin exposed gives the Wealth-Star configuration and military distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Gengchen:** a subprefect. **Yisi / Bingxu:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Bingshen day.** High standing and fondness for sex. Zi and Chen in year and month give distinction in eastern luck; otherwise disability and a shortened life. Hai–Mao–Wei is auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Yichou:** a county magistrate. **Jiachen / Wuchen:** wealth and distinction. **Xinyou / Wuxu:** Wang Heng, palace second. **Yiyou / Bingxu:** an official in the Personnel administration.〉

**Bingxu day.** Without Ren, Gui, Hai, Zi, or You filling the positions in the pillars, judge an arching configuration and distinction. Yin, Xu, Si, and Wu give a strong body without support: a monk or Daoist. 〈One account says:〉 ancestral ruin, eventual development, and calamity through Fire and blood. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Gengwu:** Chief Censor Zhang Shizai. **Guiwei / Jiayin:** a bureau director. **Wuyin / Yichou:** a provincial graduate. **Wuxu / Gengshen:** distinguished. **Jichou / Dingchou:** poor. **Yihai / Jimao:** “Jia ministry” [office uncertain].〉[^96]

〈Officer Flourishing at Birth:  
Wealth and distinction belong to it by nature.  
Turning from Lu, with Void and clash,  
Gives restless travel and insufficient wealth.〉

> Two Bing encounter Shen in the hour;  
> Without punishment or breaking, the family’s standing changes.  
> Fire and Metal melt and refine through much success and failure;  
> Only Seal permits escape from the common crowd.

> A Bing day encounters Shen in the hour;  
> Companion and yang Fire hesitate.  
> Prospering Indirect Officer brings right and wrong;  
> Within, wife and Wealth find their fulfillment.  
> Ancestral resources wax and wane, are gained and lost;  
> Parents and the ranks of brothers offer little support.  
> The time arrives: saddle, horse, and household are complete;  
> Wealth and resources, yet an empty name and scant profit.[^97]

## 34. Bing Days, Dingyou Hour
### 六丙日丁酉時斷
<!-- source: L1500-L1539 -->

> A Bing day born at Dingyou hour:  
> Blade is born and the body dies, bringing calamity.  
> No rescue in the pillars makes the outcome surely ominous;  
> Following Wealth and abandoning the Fate makes long life difficult.

On a Bing day at Dingyou hour, Blade is born and the body dies. Ding is Blade and Xin Wealth. At You, Xin flourishes, Bing dies, and Ding Fire is born: the Fate is abandoned to follow Wealth. Without rescuing assistance or communication with a body-strengthening month, and without punishment or clash affecting Blade Spirit, the person is cruel, ignorant of propriety and righteousness, and begins things without completing them. Communication with the month’s qi or rescuing assistance gives skill in crafts and techniques. With birth in Guimao month, Gui can break Ding Blade and serve as Officer. Gui Water is born at Mao, whose flourishing Yi supplies Seal. If the useful influence has strength, Water-and-Wood luck gives manifest distinction.[^98]

**Bingzi day.** Spring is stable; summer distinguished; autumn ordinary; winter auspicious. Wei, Shen, You, and Hai in year and month give great distinction. Chen combining with Zi or Chou combining with You is also judged distinguished. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Yiyou:** Vice Minister Shao Bao, an eminent official. **Guichou / Guihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengchen / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingshen / Gengyin:** a commander-in-chief. **Wuxu / Bingchen:** a bureau director. **Renchen / Guimao:** a commander-in-chief. **Dingchou / Jiyou:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Bingyin day.** Mao, Hai, and Wei in year and month, with Officer and Killings exposed in the stems, give distinction. Other months give ordinary fortunes and a craft. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingxu / Xinchou:** Supervising Secretary Li Chongkui. **Guimao / Gengshen:** a chief councillor.〉

**Bingchen day.** Yin, Wu, Xu, and Si in year and month, with Killings and Seal exposed, give distinction. Shen and Zi assemble Officer; Seal and Companions exposed give distinction. If year and month are Fire and Earth, Wealth is welcome and auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Renyin / Yisi:** a Grand Secretary. **Gengchen / Bingxu:** a Hanlin compiler. **Dingsi / Wushen:** a censor. **Gengwu / Jimao:** Zhao Bingzhong, palace first.〉

**Bingwu day.** Si, Wu, and Xu months make a good fate for a monk or Daoist. You month gives distinction. Hai, Zi, Chou, Yin, Mao, and Chen as Officer-and-Seal year/month positions give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiayin / Wuchen:** a chancellor. **Dingmao / Guichou:** a chief court minister. **Bingzi / Bingwu:** Wu Yuwen, in the Personnel administration. **Renxu / Xinhai:** Li, palace first.〉

**Bingshen day.** A month communicating with Wood and Water qi gives distinction in Water-and-Wood luck. Si and Wu strengthen the body, Hai and Zi strengthen Officer; both conditions complete in year and month give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Wuzi:** Wang Yikui, palace first. **Gengxu / Jimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingchou / Dingwei:** a commander-in-chief. **Jisi / Yihai:** Minister Bi Ziyan.〉

**Bingxu day.** A month communicating with Wood qi gives distinction in Water luck. Communication with Metal qi, followed by Fire luck, brings the Golden Horse and Jade Hall, with talent and name supreme in the age. Exposed Geng and hidden Wu give poverty and early death. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Gengyin:** a provincial graduate. **Jiayin / Bingzi:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈Blazing Fire Melting Metal:  
A declining body is most pitiable.  
The common person finds eminence difficult;  
The gentleman too remains ordinary.〉

> Bing Fire meeting You is ill matched;  
> The sun has set and little brilliance remains.  
> Add clash, control, and breaking in the Four Pillars:  
> The Six Relations suffer injury, and one wanders in distress.

> A Bing day encounters Dingyou hour;  
> Heavenly Origin’s blazing Fire melts Metal.  
> The Six Relations cannot remain together in peace;  
> The road ahead is obstructed and difficult to advance.  
> Day and night, anxious thought never finds enough;  
> Even holding office, one still fears poverty.  
> Si month encountered changes the family’s standing;  
> Zi and Wu provoke resentment from others.[^99]

## 35. Bing Days, Wuxu Hour
### 六丙日戊戌時斷
<!-- source: L1540-L1577 -->

> A Bing day born at Wuxu hour:  
> Within the Fire configuration, Food God is encountered.  
> A month communicating with Fire gives blessing and longevity;  
> Without communication, even good fortune leaves an ordinary person.

On a Bing day at Wuxu hour, Food God occupies the temple hall. Bing takes Wu as Food God and Longevity Star. At Xu, Bing Fire enters the Storehouse and Wu Earth occupies its own position. Communication with a Fire month and southeast luck gives abundant blessing and long life. Without communication, ordinary fortunes.

**Bingzi day.** Long life; name and profit are not lost. Yin, Hai, Mao, and Wei supplying Seal, and Shen, You, Xu, and Si in year and month, indicate distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Gengyin:** Minister Zhang Xian. **Bingshen / Wuxu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Guihai / Renxu:** a bureau director. **Jiachen / Dingmao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yimao / Guiwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingxu / Gengzi:** a county magistrate. **Bingwu / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiwei / Jiazi:** a bureau director; not long-lived. **Bingwu / Wuxu:** a prefect. **Bingchen / Bingshen:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Bingyin day.** Summer gives blessing and longevity together. Spring’s Wood controls Earth: Food God meets Owl. Yin month gives Birth to both Bing and Wu and is most auspicious; Shen and Geng restraining Jia give great distinction. Autumn and winter’s declining Fire give poverty and low station. Si, Shen, and Hai in year and month give the highest surveillance office. 〈Recorded cases—**Wushen / Renxu:** Minister Zhang Xian. **Jisi / Renshen:** a minister. **Guiyou / Jiayin:** a prefect. **Renshen / Renyin:** Minister Zhu Heng and Li Tinglong shared the chart. Li reached only senior provincial administrator and died in Bingzi; the regions of Jing and Wu differed. **Gengwu / Jiashen:** a censor. **Dingsi / Gengxu:** a censor. **Jisi / Wuchen:** an assistant surveillance commissioner.〉[^100]

**Bingchen day.** [Wu] month gives Bing Fire qi; a region of substantial Earth brings fifth- or sixth-rank distinction. Hai month with Metal-and-Fire luck gives surveillance office. 〈One account says:〉 ancestral ruin and solitude; a monk or Daoist’s life is suitable. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Guichou:** violent death. **Guisi / Yichou:** a surveillance commissioner. **Wushen / Gengshen:** a prefect. **Jihai / Dingchou:** a censor.〉

**Bingwu day.** Yang Blade and Food God are vigorous; name and profit rise suddenly. Hai, Wei, Chen, or [Xu] in year and month, followed by western luck, gives surveillance office. 〈One account says:〉 calamity amid joy. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiayin / Wuchen:** a vice minister. **Wuxu / Wuwu:** a vice minister. **Jiazi / Xinwei:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Dingsi / Bingwu:** a prefect. **Renxu / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renshen / Xinhai:** a runner-up in the provincial examination. **Gengzi / Wuzi:** a censor.〉[^101]

**Bingshen day.** Hai month with eastern luck gives distinction. Yin and Wu strengthen the body; Wealth exposed in the stems is auspicious. Chou and Chen punish or clash with Xu’s Storehouse, bringing development in later years. 〈One account says:〉 early death and poverty. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinchou / Jiawu:** Minister Li Tingxiang. **Renshen / Xinhai:** a remonstrance official.〉

**Bingxu day.** Poverty. Yet communication with a Yin–Si–Wu–Xu Fire configuration gives blessing and longevity together and great distinction. Wealth appearing is also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Guisi:** Minister Han Bangyan. **Dingyou / Yisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuyin / Dingsi:** a ministry secretary. **Yiyou / Wuyin:** a provincial graduate. **Wuxu / Renxu:** rich. **Renxu / Wushen:** an ominous outcome. **Bingxu / Xinchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renxu / Bingwu:** vice minister of the Court of the Imperial Stud.〉

〈Food God upon the Hour:  
Among towers, terraces, shops, and lodgings,  
Tea rooms and wine houses,  
Several times one establishes a household’s way.〉

> A Bing day at Xu hour opens Wealth’s Storehouse;  
> Unrecognized in youth, one remains submerged for a time.  
> Luck finds passage: sooner or later, title and office are conferred;  
> Even without office, wealth develops.

> A Bing day encounters Wuxu hour;  
> Within it, granaries and Storehouses are complete.  
> Layer upon layer of blessing and Lu comes by nature;  
> Wealth and distinction, worthy wife and admirable children.  
> The gentleman establishes literary accomplishment early;  
> The common person’s possessions continue to grow.  
> Early death, solitude, and parents caught early in adversity;  
> Chen and Xu, the keys, open and reveal it.[^102]

## 36. Bing Days, Jihai Hour
### 六丙日己亥時斷
<!-- source: L1578-L1625 -->

> A Bing day born at Jihai hour:  
> Ren flourishing within Hai is injured by Ji.  
> Only communication with the month’s qi gives distinction;  
> Without Yin or Mao, ordinary fortunes are indicated.

On a Bing day at Jihai hour, ghost flourishes and the body is extinguished. Bing regards Ji as the influence injuring Killings, Ren as the proper ghost, and Jia as Inverted Food. At Hai, visible Ji stands with flourishing Ren and newly born Jia, while Bing Fire reaches Extinction. A body-strengthening month with support transforms ghost into Officer and gives distinction, though the person remains cruel. Body-strengthening luck also gives distinction. A weak body without support or communication with the month’s qi, with Ren exposed in the pillars, brings a hundred calamities, pride toward others, lofty airs, and ordinary fortunes. Bing belongs to the small intestine, heart, and navel, and often indicates a heart-and-blood disorder.[^103]

**Bingzi day.** Autumn and winter are ordinary. Wei month with Water-and-Wood luck and Wood and Fire exposed gives distinction. Zi month with eastern luck gives great distinction. Shen, Hai, Chou, and Xu in year and month also give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinwei / Gengzi:** Minister Zhang Han, lived beyond ninety. **Jiazi / Dingchou:** a duke. **Xinhai / Xinchou:** Fang, palace first. **Bingshen / Jihai:** an administration commissioner. **Dingsi / Dingwei:** an academician. **Dingchou / Guimao:** rich, many sons.〉

**Bingyin day.** Summer transforms ghost into Officer, giving a worthy wife and filial children, and either distinction or wealth. Yin, Mao, Chen, Si, Shen, and Chou in year and month, or Wu and Xu assembling the configuration, with Bing and [Wu] exposed among the heavenly stems, indicate great authority and rank. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 a Fire-and-Wood nayin image gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Gengchen:** highest office and a princely title; a chancellor shared the chart. **Gengyin / Gengchen:** a Grand Preceptor. **Bingwu / Wuxu:** Minister and Supreme Commander Wang Yiqi. **Bingzi / Dingyou:** a commander-in-chief. **Dingsi / Wushen:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Guimao / Yimao:** Chief Censor Guo Chiping. **Guichou / Jiazi:** Luo Cheng, palace second. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** chancellor of the Imperial Academy; an associate prefect shared the chart. **Guisi / Bingchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guisi / Yichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinhai / Jihai:** a subprefect. **Xinwei / Gengyin:** a provincial graduate. **Jimao / Dingmao:** a provincial graduate. **Yiyou / Jichou:** a county magistrate. **Yichou / Yiyou:** Vice Minister Zang Erquan. **Jiyou / Guiyou:** a provincial graduate and subprefect. **Jihai / Guiyou:** a duke’s younger brother, immensely rich.〉[^104]

**Bingchen day.** Day Virtue. Yin-month birth is auspicious. Chen month gives wealth to monks and Daoists, solitude and injury to ordinary people. Xu month clashes with Chen; Si month clashes with Hai; Bing’s flourishing and Storehouse are both auspicious. Mao and Wei combining, with Metal-and-Water luck, or Shen and Zi combining, with Wood-and-Fire luck, give distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Fire-and-Earth transformation must give blessing, but the span is short. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Bingyin:** a prefect. **Renwu / Gengxu:** a subprefect. **Jisi / Renshen:** a provincial graduate. **Jiawu / Dingchou:** a registrar. **Dingyou / Wushen:** a chancellor.〉

**Bingwu day.** Military distinction. Birth in Yin, Si, or Wu month gives repeated wives and children late; either distinction or wealth. Autumn and winter bring advances and retreats in name and profit. You, Wu, and Xu give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Guiyou:** Vice Minister Li Sui. **Wuxu / Yimao:** Li Jing, palace first. **Gengzi / Jimao:** a provincial administrator. **Dingmao / Renyin:** a remonstrance official. **Xinwei / Dingyou:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Dingmao / Jiachen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yihai / Guiwei:** a provincial graduate. **Renwu / Dingwei:** a ministry secretary. **Gengshen / Dinghai:** a surveillance commissioner.〉

**Bingshen day.** Spring and summer birth move roots and exchange leaves; fortune changes through establishing oneself with the wife. Autumn and winter are ordinary. You month with southeast luck gives surveillance office. 〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Bingzi:** a chief censor. **Wuwu / Yimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiashen / Yihai:** a prefect. **Jiawu / Renshen:** a censor. **Dingchou / Renyin:** a ministry bureau director. **Gengzi / Bingxu:** a supervising secretary in the southern capital. **Bingyin / Jihai:** a surveillance commissioner.〉

**Bingxu day.** Yin, Mao, Si, and Wu give generating Wood and blazing Fire: injury to wife, few children, intelligence, wealth, and distinction. You, Hai, Zi, and Chou are ordinary; eastern luck nevertheless gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Gengyin:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Bingzi / Jiawu:** a vice censor-in-chief. **Guimao / Xinyou:** a censor. **Xinsi / Xinchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinyou / Jihai:** a provincial graduate. **Renwu / Guimao:** a provincial graduate. **Jiwei / Renshen:** a transmission commissioner. **Dingwei / Bingwu:** an education commissioner. **Wuzi / Bingchen:** Wang Yongji, Minister of Justice in the southern capital.〉

〈Tianyi Supporting Officer:  
A pure name spreads wherever one goes.  
The common person must develop blessing;  
The gentleman becomes marquis or prince.〉

> A Bing day at Hai hour makes the highest fate;  
> Like orchids rising above tangled grass.  
> Add clash, control, and breaking in the Four Pillars,  
> And seeking name and profit becomes empty toil.

> A Bing day approaches Jihai hour;  
> Without Ren, it is exceptional.  
> The fate has few children and two or three wives;  
> Within, Tianyi is gladly encountered.  
> Parents and the ranks of brothers go far away;  
> Punishment and Void make literary blessing difficult to complete.  
> All because the Eight Characters have their high and low:  
> Above all, avoid coveting Wealth and losing office.

## 37. Ding Days, Gengzi Hour
### 六丁日庚子時斷
<!-- source: L1626-L1659 -->

> A Ding day born at Gengzi hour:  
> The body declines and ghost flourishes in concealment.  
> No rescue from the month gives much poverty and early death;  
> Obtaining a strong bodily seat restores auspicious flourishing.

On a Ding day at Gengzi hour, the body is extinguished and ghost flourishes. Ding takes Geng as Wealth and Gui as ghost. Zi contains Gui; Ding Fire lacks qi and cannot be supported. A month communicating with bodily strength and offering support gives distinction; further body-strengthening luck gives great distinction. The reverse gives poverty, early death, and low station. Ding belongs to the heart, the repository of blood, and also indicates blood disease.

**Dingchou day.** Ordinary. Communication with Wood-and-Fire qi, or body-strengthening luck, gives distinction. Some born in Hai or Zi in year and month are also distinguished, because yielding yin Ding Fire does not fear Water’s country. 〈One account says:〉 poverty first, good fortune afterward. Wuyin month shortens life; Xinchou month ruins the ancestral estate and brings obstruction in middle age. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiyou / Bingyin:** a senior provincial administrator; a metropolitan graduate shared the chart. **Xinhai / Gengzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renzi / Guimao:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Gengchen / Guiwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiwei / Jiazi:** a provincial graduate. **Wuxu / Yichou:** a woman whose son Han Jing was first in both metropolitan and palace examinations. **Guisi / Gengshen:** a woman whose husband Wang Xijue was Grand Secretary and whose son Wang Heng was palace second. **Gengshen / Wuzi:** a vice censor-in-chief. **Dingsi / Wushen:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Dingmao day.** Poverty. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei restrain Indirect Officer; Wu month makes the stem strong; spring strengthens the body: all are auspicious. Autumn and winter are ordinary. Guisi month brings ancestral ruin and misfortune; Jiwei month injury through punishment; Jiashen month facial tattooing, bloodshed, and death. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Gengzi:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Bingwu / Yiwei:** a censor. **Guiyou / Renxu:** a provincial graduate. **Xinhai / Dingyou:** a chancellor. **Dinghai / Guimao:** Chief Censor Gao Wenjian and Tao Dalin, palace second, shared the chart; the note records death in Guiyou and different regions, Wu and Liang. **Renchen / Renyin:** vice minister of the Court of the Imperial Stud.〉[^105]

**Dingsi day.** Spring flourishing and summer strength both give distinction. Autumn and winter are ordinary. The season-ending months give appropriate restraint and are auspicious. Zi and Chen with western luck give fourth or fifth rank. 〈One account says:〉 sudden wealth and great flourishing. Gengyin month brings punishment; Gengshen great ruin; Xinyou ruin and punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuyin / Renxu:** Chief Censor Li Shangzhi. **Renshen / Xinhai:** a prefect. **Dingchou / Dingwei:** a censor. **Jiazi / Dingchou:** palace first. **Guihai / Xinyou:** “Jia ministry” [office uncertain].〉

**Dingwei day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months restrain Indirect Officer. Wu month makes the stem strong and gives distinction. Other months with restraint are auspicious. Wuzi gives manifest literary refinement. Wushen month is avoided for early death, Bingxu for punishment, Xinchou for punishment and misfortune. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Renwu:** a junior provincial administrator. **Jiwei / Guiyou:** a senior provincial administrator.〉

**Dingyou day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give firmness, clarity, outstanding achievement, and distinction. Chen and Si in year and month give honored rank in the chancellor’s office. Jiayin month brings ruin and violent death; Guisi departure from home and violent death; Yiyou death through punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingsi / Dingwei:** a senior provincial administrator. **Bingzi / Jiawu:** a prefect. **Yimao / Wuyin:** a ministry secretary. **Wushen / Guihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiyou / Jimao:** a bureau director. **Guihai / Gengshen:** a provincial graduate. **Renyin / Guichou:** an imperial relative.〉

**Dinghai day.** Great flourishing after fifty. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months are auspicious; Yin and Wu in year and month give distinction. Gengyin month brings violent death; Gengshen death with the body mutilated; Xinyou ruin. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Guichou:** Zhang, palace first, and metropolitan graduate Zheng Cun shared the chart. **Jiyou / Bingyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Gengzi / Jimao:** a senior provincial administrator.〉

〈Panning Gold from Sand:  
Scholar and commoner have ordinary fortunes.  
Punishment and injury bring many dangers;  
The gentleman escapes calamity.〉

> Gengzi hour encounters Ding as the day;  
> Fire falls among rivers and lakes, darkens, then shines again.  
> Add clash, control, and breaking in the Four Pillars:  
> Study into old age yields only an empty name.

> A Ding day encounters Gengzi hour;  
> The day stem’s Ding Fire shines brightly.  
> Fire at Embryo, Metal at Extinction: gains and losses;  
> Wife and concubines cannot be wholly satisfactory.  
> Control, punishment, clash, and breaking appear;  
> Literary blessing within is difficult to complete.  
> Generation and rescue bring promotion by a noble person;  
> This fate is difficult first and easy afterward.[^106]

## 38. Ding Days, Xinchou Hour
### 六丁日辛丑時斷
<!-- source: L1660-L1703 -->
〈The unfavorable months for the six Ding days below follow the preceding rule; the hour’s prohibitions [are judged together with them].〉[^107]

> A Ding day born at Xinchou hour:  
> Within the Storehouse, wealth and grain age in abundance.  
> A body without support is not thereby ominous;  
> With support, the wife is worthy and wealth substantial.

On a Ding day at Xinchou hour, Chou is Wealth’s Storehouse. Xin is Wealth and wife; Ji is Food God. Chou strengthens the Metal configuration and hidden Ji obtains its position, while Ding Fire lacks qi. If the season is missed and there is no support, advancement comes through the wife. With support and rescue, Wealth and Food are plentiful.

**Dingchou day.** Shen and You months give a Wealth configuration; flourishing Wealth generates Officer and brings distinction. Wu month injures Wealth while strengthening the body and indicates office and Lu. Pure Zi and Yin give third-rank military distinction. Water months bring hardship and toil; Chen month an imperial relative. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Wuwu:** a minister. **Yimao / Jichou:** a vice minister. **Wuchen / Jiwei:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Jiyou / Bingyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingyin / Xinchou:** blind in both eyes. **Wuxu / Renxu:** a bureau director in the Ministry of Rites.〉

**Dingmao day.** Chen, Si, and Wei in year and month give wealth and distinction; wife and children come late. Yin and Mao assist Seal; Xu’s Storehouse strengthens the body and punishes Chou. Ding obtains support and great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Guichou:** Minister Li Sancai. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** Vice Minister Zhang Han. **Jiashen / Bingzi:** a prefect. **Bingchen / Guisi:** a provincial graduate. **Bingyin / Yiwei:** a military commandant.〉

**Dingsi day.** Distinction through a woman. Si, You, Chou, and Shen in year and month make Wealth flourish and generate Officer, giving both wealth and distinction. Mao month is ordinary, but northern luck also gives distinction. Chou month with southwest luck gives great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuwu / Yichou:** a chancellor. **Yimao / Jichou:** an academician. **Renxu / Dingwei:** an associate prefect; violent death. **Dingchou / Jiyou:** a county magistrate. **Dinghai / Jiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Guiyou / Wuwu:** a provincial graduate; Wu month establishes Lu and exposed Officer is auspicious. **Renshen / Jiyou:** “Jia ministry” [office uncertain]. **Jiawu / Bingyin:** a palace official. **Yisi / Wuzi:** a senior provincial administrator.〉

**Dingwei day.** Hour and day clash, threatening wife and children. You month communicating with Water qi is auspicious. Years and luck follow the same rule. 〈Recorded case—**Jimao / Jiaxu:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Dingyou day.** Yin, Mao, Si, and Wu strengthen the body and provide support, giving literary renown, high distinction, and extraordinary favor. Shen, You, and Xu strengthen Wealth: following it is most auspicious. Hai and Zi strengthen Officer and are also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Yimao / Xinsi:** Minister Hu Ying, an eminent official. **Xinchou / Dingyou:** Minister Huang Gao. **Wuchen / Wuwu:** a bureau director. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** a ministry secretary. **Bingchen / Wuxu:** a provincial graduate. **Bingchen / Gengzi:** a provincial graduate. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** Wang, Duke of Jing. **Guihai / Renxu:** Huang Zhiqing, probationary Hanlin scholar. **Jiayin / Jisi:** Xiong Shangwen, governor of Chu. **Jiayin / Xinwei:** Hanlin scholar Chen Yidian; blind, no sons.〉

**Dinghai day.** Spring’s Seal-Ribbon is auspicious. Autumn birth with Fire qi is also auspicious. Summer’s excessive strength is ominous; winter’s flourishing Officer and Killings are auspicious. Wei and Xu months clash open Chou’s Storehouse and give wealth. 〈Recorded case—**Gengzi / Wuzi:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

〈Stored Wealth upon the Hour:  
People encounter it through their allotted bond.  
Without injury, wealth and distinction come in the end;  
This saying is not transmitted falsely.〉

> Xinchou meeting Ding is a treasury;  
> No key in the pillars makes wealth hard to predict.  
> Punishing and clashing luck arrives, bringing an eminent person;  
> One rises abroad and establishes a name.

> A Ding day encounters Xinchou hour;  
> Indirect Wealth’s Storehouse welcomes punishment and clash.  
> If it does not become manifest, reputation is empty;  
> Wife and children share fitting celebration.  
> Parents and the ranks of brothers find little accord;  
> The household changes and is made anew.  
> Wealth and blessing develop in a middle resurgence;  
> Surely this is a fate of splendor.

## 39. Ding Days, Renyin Hour
### 六丁日壬寅時斷
<!-- source: L1704-L1753 -->

> A Ding day born at Renyin hour:  
> The body departs to follow Officer and transforms into Wood.  
> A Water-or-Wood month communicates and completes the image;  
> Honor, peace, and wealth bring incomparable distinction.

On a Ding day at Renyin hour, the body departs to follow Officer. Ding and Ren transform into Wood, vigorous at Yin. A Water-configuration month gives great distinction; a month communicating with Wood also gives distinction. Dingwei month, for example, is favorable in eastern luck.

**Dingchou day.** Transformation gives distinction. Winter birth strengthens Officer and gives rank; spring’s Seal-Ribbon stability; summer good fortune; autumn ordinary results. Eastern luck is favorable. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengzi / Wuzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiyou / Renwu:** an academician. **Bingwu / Gengyin:** Zheng Rubi, governor of Huai. **Yihai / Bingxu:** Vice Minister Wan Gong. **Yimao / Xinsi:** rich.〉

**Dingmao day.** Distinguished; transformation is auspicious. Yin and Mao in year and month give great distinction in Metal-and-Water luck. Hai and Zi months give distinction in western luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Renyin / Gengxu:** Chief Censor Wang Deming. **Guichou / Yimao:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Gengshen / Renwu:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Bingchen / Xinchou:** Fang Zhengliang, a high examination placing. **Wuchen / Gengshen:** a ministry secretary. **Guichou / Wuwu:** an associate prefect. **Renshen / Renzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingyin / Gengyin:** an imperial relative.〉

**Dingsi day.** Ding dies at Yin and already has no impulse of generation; Yin also punishes it. There is a beginning but no completion. Metal-and-Water luck gives honor and distinction. Yin and Wu in year and month strengthen the body; Shen–Zi–Chen strengthens Officer; Hai–Mao–Wei strengthens Seal: all permit a judgment of distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Renyin:** Hanlin Compiler Li Renjie. **Bingwu / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingxu / Xinchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiwei / Bingyin:** a ministry secretary. **Jiashen / Xinwei:** a provincial graduate. **Dingyou / Bingwu:** a prefect. **Xinhai / Xinmao:** a bureau director. **Renchen / Renyin:** a commander-in-chief. **Guisi / Jiwei:** likewise a commander-in-chief. **Jiashen / Dingmao:** Fu Chaoyou, chief supervising secretary. **Guichou / Yichou:** an honorifically titled Mr. Li, whose son Chunfang was palace first and Grand Secretary. **Gengchen / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate and bureau director; died. **Jiawu / Guiyou:** a censor.〉

**Dingwei day.** Violent death. Spring’s Seal is auspicious; summer’s Wood-and-Fire luck develops blessing; autumn gives wealth; winter distinction. You and Xu in year and month give third rank; Hai, Mao, Yin, and Xu literary distinction. Pure Mao with Metal-and-Water luck gives gold and purple. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 transformation into Wood gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Renshen:** Chief Censor Peng Ze. **Bingwu / Gengzi:** a commander-in-chief. **Guisi / Jiwei:** a commander-in-chief. **Bingwu / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guimao / Jiayin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renyin / [Renzi]:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Jimao / Jiaxu:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Yihai / Wuzi:** a chancellor. **Wuchen / Guihai:** He, palace first. **Yimao / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^108]

**Dingyou day.** Hai, Wei, Yin, Mao, Shen, and Zi in year and month give intelligence, wealth, distinction, and the highest surveillance office. Si and Wu give a chancellor. Chen month with Metal-and-Water luck, or Xu month with eastern luck, gives distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Wood transformation gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Xinhai:** Minister Hu Zongxian. **Yisi / Jiashen:** Chief Censor Chen Zeqing. **Wushen / Guihai:** Chief Censor Lin Ershan. **Yisi / Dinghai:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Jiachen / Gengwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinwei / Jihai:** a commander-in-chief. **Bingzi / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guichou / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate. **Jimao / Bingzi:** the Duke for Perpetuating the Sage. **Gengxu / Gengchen:** Zhao Nanxing, Minister of Personnel. **Xinwei / Bingshen:** Education Commissioner Tan Changyan. **Dingsi / Jiyou:** “Jia ministry” [office uncertain].〉

**Dinghai day.** Day Nobility paired with Renyin supplies both Officer and Seal, giving manifest literary achievement. Zi month gives great distinction. Transformed qi is ominous. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 transformation into Wood gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Gengyin:** a vice minister. **Jimao / Dingchou:** Commander-in-Chief Zu Dashou, a loyal minister. **Jisi / Guiyou:** a prefect. **Guiyou / Renxu:** a provincial graduate.〉[^109]

〈Touching Iron into Gold:  
The hour encounters great good fortune.  
The common person often develops blessing;  
The gentleman’s name and profit shine abroad.〉

> Ding and Ren combine and transform, then enter Metal’s country:  
> A cur’s stipend and a flyspeck of fame keep one vainly busy.  
> Integrity withers, leaving nothing worth esteem;  
> Even kin before one’s eyes are divided like Shen and Shang.[^110]

> A Ding day combines with Renyin hour;  
> The transformed configuration is flourishing Wood’s country.  
> Let Shen and You not appear in the monthly branch;  
> Purpose fulfilled, an eminent person recommends one for service.  
> Parents and the ranks of brothers have little strength;  
> Outsiders greet one with spring-wind smiles.  
> Luck meets Water and Wood, with no trace of Metal:  
> A fate of manifest distinction and flourishing honor.

## 40. Ding Days, Guimao Hour
### 六丁日癸卯時斷
<!-- source: L1754-L1793 -->

> A Ding day born at Guimao hour:  
> Ghost flourishes, the body declines, and hardship is unbearable.  
> Only support and communication with the month permit blessing;  
> Otherwise poverty and low station grieve the heart.

On a Ding day at Guimao hour, the body declines and ghost flourishes. Ding takes Gui as ghost and Yi as Inverted Food. At Mao, Gui is born and Yi flourishes. Supporting rescue and a body-strengthening month transform ghost into Officer and are auspicious. Even if absent from the pillars, communication through luck is also auspicious. The reverse gives poverty, low station, blindness, blood disease, calamity for the wife, and few children.

**Dingchou day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give balanced restraint; Wu month gives a strong stem and distinction. Shen and Chou give surveillance office. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Gengwu:** a bureau director. **Renzi / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinchou / Yiwei:** Zhao Kehuai, governor of Chu, beaten to death by members of the imperial clan. **Guichou / Jiwei:** an earl. **Xinyou / Bingshen:** Fang Yingxiang, metropolitan graduate and bureau director in the Ministry of War.〉

**Dingmao day.** Yin and Mao months give Seal-Ribbon carrying Killings, not judged ominous. Officer luck brings manifest distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinchou / Guisi:** Minister Yang Baiquan. **Gengxu / Dinghai:** Xiao Liangyou, metropolitan first, palace second, and chancellor of the Imperial Academy. **Jisi / Guiyou:** a senior provincial administrator. **Yiwei / Wuyin:** a prefect. **Xinsi / Gengyin:** a provincial graduate. **Jiyou / Xinwei:** one man of She succeeded in the Gengwu examination and became a prefectural judge; another from Fujian succeeded in Bingzi and became a school instructor. **Wushen / Bingchen:** Chief Censor Jiang Duo.〉[^111]

**Dingsi day.** Chou-month birth with northern luck is auspicious in regions of substantial Earth. In places of refined Water it gives second or third rank. Shen and You employing Wealth are also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**[Yisi] / Jiashen:** a bureau director. **Bingxu / Jiawu:** an imperial relative. **Wuxu / Guihai:** a chancellor. **Yiyou / Xinsi:** an imperial physician. **Dingyou / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingwu / Bingshen:** a provincial graduate, dismissed, immensely rich.〉[^112]

**Dingwei day.** Indirect Officer generates Seal. Spring is auspicious; summer ordinary; autumn wealthy; winter poor. 〈Another says:〉 Ding Fire does not fear Water. Winter’s Hai and Zi make Killings heavy and the body yielding, giving great distinction. Chou and Wei with eastern luck give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Renzi:** a chancellor. **Bingxu / Xinmao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingshen / Yiwei:** Provincial Administration Commissioner Han Shao, whose son Jing was first in both metropolitan and palace examinations. **Dingyou / Dingwei:** Minister Tang Long, whose son Ruji was palace first. **Gengyin / Wuyin:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Dingyou day.** Day and hour both sit upon Noble: most auspicious. Spring and summer strengthen the body and transform ghost into Officer. Autumn and winter weaken the body and give toil and hardship. Si and Xu months give distinction. Pure Wu and Chou in year and month, with Geng and Ji exposed, give pure distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Jiawu:** Yang Jisheng, honored as Lord [Zhongmin]. **Yimao / Dinghai:** Jin, palace first. **Gengwu / Jichou:** Li Chunfang, palace first and Grand Secretary. **Guichou / Bingchen:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Jisi / Yihai:** a judicial reviewer.〉[^113]

**Dinghai day.** Mao month gives Seal-Ribbon and distinction as a close attendant. Yin and Wu give fifth- or sixth-rank office in the capital. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Wuzi:** a ministry secretary. **Jiwei / Gengwu:** a bureau director in the Ministry of Rites.〉

〈Becoming a Butterfly in a Dream:  
Once awake, do not dwell upon it.  
Luck and body communicating with flourishing ground  
Alone permit the promise of name and profit.〉

> A Dingchou day encounters Guimao at birth;  
> For now, seeking name and profit yields middling results.  
> A declining body turns cleverness into clumsiness;  
> Purpose fulfilled must come through meeting a noble person.

> A Ding day approaches Guimao hour;  
> A weak body without support is ordinary.  
> Ghosts and thieves within the gate consume wealth and grain;  
> Preserving ancestral property, guard against ruin.  
> Parents and the ranks of brothers offer little help;  
> Wife and children cannot avoid leaving home.  
> Only a strong body in luck subdues Killings;  
> Then name and profit may flourish freely.[^114]

## 41. Ding Days, Jiachen Hour
### 六丁日甲辰時斷
<!-- source: L1794-L1835 -->

> A Ding day born at Jiachen hour:  
> Officer Star obtains its place and Seal generates the body.  
> No communication with the month’s qi gives ordinary blessing;  
> Supporting assistance brings Lu and Noble Person together.

On a Ding day at Jiachen hour there are Seal-Ribbon and Officer’s Storehouse. Jia is Seal and Ren Officer. Jia Wood’s Seal-Ribbon within Chen generates the body, while Officer Star joins the configuration. Communication with the month’s qi and support give distinction; otherwise ordinary fortunes. Spring birth favors northern luck, winter birth southern luck: auspicious.[^115]

**Dingchou day.** Auspicious. Hai and Zi months give wealth and distinction. Shen month with eastern luck and Wu month with Metal-and-Water luck give distinction. Pure Yin gives the highest surveillance office. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingshen / Gengzi:** a privy councillor. **Jiachen / Renshen:** a Minister of Personnel. **Guichou / Xinyou:** a school instructor. **Jiayin / Dingchou:** a provincial graduate. **Dinghai / Xinhai:** a provincial graduate. **Guichou / Guihai:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Dingmao day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give solitude and injury; when luck finds passage, wealth develops. Chou month with southern luck gives distinction; Mao month with Metal-and-Water luck gives authority and distinction as a close attendant. Presenting remonstrances or proposals is unfavorable. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiayin / Xinwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengchen / Renwu:** a provincial graduate. **Jiayin / Dingchou:** a provincial graduate. **Guiwei / Yimao:** a remonstrance official. **Wushen / Yichou:** an associate prefect. **Renxu / Wushen:** rich.〉

**Dingsi day.** Ominous punishment, solitude, and injury. Spring’s Seal is auspicious; summer and autumn ordinary; winter strengthens Officer. Pure Xu with Wood-and-Fire luck gives an obscure official post. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Renchen:** an imperial relative. **Yiwei / Renshen:** a county magistrate. **Bingshen / Jihai:** Chen Zizhuang, palace third and vice minister. **Bingxu / Guisi:** Chen Guanyang, in the Personnel administration. **Guiwei / Yichou:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Dingwei day.** Chou, Hai, Mao, and Wei months give distinction through Seal-Ribbon and Officer. Summer is ordinary; Chen and [Xu] give substantial distinction. You and Wu with Metal-and-Water luck give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Bingzi:** Minister Wang Tingxiang. **Renwu / Gengxu:** an administration commissioner. **Jiwei / Jiaxu:** a prefect. **Xinmao / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renxu / Bingwu:** a Hanlin reviser. **Jimao / Bingyin:** a Central Secretariat officer, executed under the law. **Guihai / Jiazi:** Governor Yu Tianxing. **Jisi / Renshen:** distinguished; untimely death.〉[^116]

**Dingyou day.** Ordinary. Without Xu in year and month, the Day Master enters the Glorious-Nobility configuration. Spring birth with southern luck gives distinction, with northern luck great distinction. The [year stem] and monthly branch combining into the configuration give Horse Transformation and still greater distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Xinhai:** Provincial Administration Commissioner Li Weizhen of Jingshan, whose collected writings circulated in the world. **Yihai / Gengchen:** a vice minister. **Dingchou / Renyin:** a prefect. **Jiashen / Jisi:** a surveillance commissioner. **Bingchen / Renchen:** a prefect. **Dingchou / Renzi:** an associate prefect. **Bingchen / Renchen:** received an official title after initial poverty. **Bingzi / Wuxu:** a county assistant magistrate, immensely rich. **Yichou / Renwu:** a metropolitan graduate; died early.〉

**Dinghai day.** Day Nobility, with Officer and Lu obtaining their positions, must bring manifest advancement. Shen and Chen in year and month give great distinction. Wu month with northeast luck or Chen month with northern luck gives distinction. 〈One account says:〉 blood disease and ominous punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Jiachen:** palace first. **Renxu / Jiyou:** a chief censor. **Yiwei / Wuyin:** a commander-in-chief. **Gengshen / Wuzi:** a palace official.〉

〈Officer’s Storehouse within the Hour:  
Developing blessing requires punishment and clash.  
If luck travels a prosperous course,  
Wealth is generated and Officer follows naturally.〉

> A Ding day’s hour encounters Officer and Seal together;  
> A key opens Wealth’s Storehouse and reveals the Money Dragon.  
> No punishment or breaking in the Four Pillars’ stems and branches:  
> Only when luck arrives is one called rich and noble.

> A Ding day, Chen hour, the Storehouse flourishing;  
> Within, Seal-Ribbon generates in accord.  
> The Xu key opens it, welcoming Ren and Ding;  
> Literary refinement rises above the crowd.  
> Blood kin and the Six Relations suffer injury;  
> Fruit forms, flowers fade, and bloom returns.  
> Only the lateness of luck delays the Money Dragon:  
> A fate dark at first and bright afterward.[^117]

## 42. Ding Days, Yisi Hour
### 六丁日乙巳時斷
<!-- source: L1836-L1875 -->

> A Ding day born at Yisi hour:  
> Hurting Officer secretly meets Owl Spirit.  
> Eastern luck produces refinement without substance;  
> Metal-and-Water country gives Lu and Noble Person.

On a Ding day at Yisi hour, Ding takes Ren as Officer and Yi as Inverted Food. Ren is extinguished at Si. Visible Yi as Inverted Food and hidden Wu as Hurting Officer are vigorous, giving pride toward others, lofty ambition, and ordinary fortunes. Communication with a Metal-and-Water month gives distinction; communication through luck does so as well.

**Dingchou day.** Spring gives wealth; summer solitude; autumn good fortune; winter distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Guihai:** Vice Minister Xu Chengming. **Jiaxu / Bingyin:** an administration commissioner. **Xinwei / Jiawu:** a provincial graduate. **Renchen / Guimao:** Mr. Xu, granted the title of vice censor-in-chief through his son Minshi. **Yisi / Bingxu:** Li Changchun, Minister of Rites. **Jimao / Dingmao:** punishment and misfortune. **Renxu / Renyin:** a bandit.〉

**Dingmao day.** Spring gives Seal, summer bodily strength, autumn Wealth, winter Officer. Examine what is employed; all can be judged auspicious. Wu and Wei in year and month bring literary renown and manifest distinction. Officer-and-Lu luck is auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinwei / Bingshen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiyou / Yiyou:** “Jia ministry” [office uncertain]. **Xinwei / Wuxu:** Grand Secretary Chen Yiqin.〉

**Dingsi day.** Birth also in Si month clashes backward to Hai’s Ren as Officer. If no Water fills the position and breaks the configuration, fourth- or fifth-rank distinction follows. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Dingsi:** a Hanlin compiler. **Bingzi / Guisi:** Ren, palace first. **Jiawu / Bingzi:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiayin / Jiaxu:** a prefect. **Wuchen / Dingsi:** a bureau director. **Xinyou / Guisi:** an institutional judge. **Xinwei / Bingshen:** a transport commissioner. **Jisi / Guiyou:** literary distinction. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** military distinction.〉

**Dingwei day.** Entering Reversed Clash without clash or breaking gives distinction. Jihai in year or month gives third or fourth rank. You and Chou assemble a Wealth configuration and give riches. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiyou / Yichou:** a prefect. **Dingsi / Yisi:** distinguished. **Guiwei / Yimao:** a woman, honored and long-lived. **Dingwei / Yisi:** the woman of the Tianqi emperor’s Western Palace.〉[^118]

**Dingyou day.** Wealth damaged and Food inverted. Year and month communicating with Metal and Water, followed by Metal-and-Water luck, are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinmao / Dingyou:** a censor. **Guimao / Bingchen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Guimao / Jiwei:** great distinction. **Dingsi / Yisi:** a councillor of the left. **Renxu / Xinhai:** a prefect. **Wuyin / Bingchen:** a provincial graduate. **Xinchou / Wuxu:** Huang Hongxian, deputy head of the heir apparent’s household. **Jiwei / Xinwei:** Chief Censor Wang Keshou.〉

**Dinghai day.** Hour and day clash, threatening wife and children. Si–You–Chou and Shen–Zi–Chen, the Metal and Water configurations, make Wealth and Officer useful: judge wealth and distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Jiachen:** Vice Minister Wang Kuang. **Dinghai / Jiachen:** a beggar.〉

〈Indirect Seal Damaging Wealth:  
In early years, remain hidden for a time.  
Luck travels Officer and Lu’s ground;  
Wealth and distinction descend from Heaven.〉

> A Ding day at Si hour fears the Tiger’s punishment;  
> Only Wealth-and-Officer luck opens the way.  
> Goodwill and human kindness turn to ill will;  
> Difficulty first, ease afterward—joy at leisure.

> A Ding day approaches Yisi hour;  
> Damaged Wealth and Inverted Food impede passage.  
> Parents and the ranks of brothers are reasonably at peace;  
> Wife and children have no anger or distress.  
> The gentleman has refined qi for learning and letters;  
> The common person understands wealth and skilled pursuits.  
> If Ren, Geng, Xin, and Gui appear repeatedly,  
> Middle and later years have wealth and a name sufficient for use.[^119]

## 43. Ding Days, Bingwu Hour
### 六丁日丙午時斷
<!-- source: L1876-L1911 -->

> A Ding day born at Bingwu hour:  
> The day’s Lu rejoices to occupy the hour.  
> Let no Rat, Rabbit, or Gui inflict injury in the pillars;  
> In youth, rise swiftly on the road to azure clouds.

On a Ding day at Bingwu hour, the road to azure clouds opens. Ding Fire has Established Lu at Wu. If no Owl in the branches breaks it and no Gui in the stems punishes it, the Lu Origin is pure and gives distinction. With Zi or Mao in the branches, or Gui in the stems to break it, Lu is encountered as though not encountered.[^120]

**Dingchou day.** Ordinary. Yin, Mao, Xu, or Wei in year and month gives distinction. You or Chou, employing Wealth, is most auspicious. Hai and Zi as Officer and Killings are avoided. 〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Bingyin:** a chancellor. **Wuyin / Xinyou:** a provincial graduate. **Gengyin / Jichou:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Dingmao day.** Misfortune amid flourishing. Mao’s punishment and breaking leave one a commoner. In Si month, northwest luck gives distinction; southeast luck brings the highest rank and authority. Yin or Hai gives second-rank military office. With Zi and You both present, great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuzi / Xinyou:** a vice minister; a bureau director has the same fate. **Jiawu / Bingyin:** a senior provincial administrator; a chancellor has the same fate. **Xinwei / Dingyou:** a court registrar. **Dingmao / Renzi:** Grand Secretary Liu Yijing.〉

**Dingsi day.** Ding’s Lu is Wu, Bing’s Lu Si: the Exchanging Lu configuration. With no Yin, Hai, or Zi in the pillars, literary attainment brings manifest distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Guichou / Dingsi:** Minister Lu Houping. **Gengshen / Renwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingchen / Wuxu:** a provincial graduate. **Dinghai / Yisi:** a provincial graduate. **Yimao / Renwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinyou / Yiwei:** Jin Da, first in the metropolitan examination and third in the palace examination. **Bingzi / Dingyou:** a woman without children, granted an honorific title through an adopted son. **Guisi / Yichou:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Dingwei day.** Distinguished; the ancestral establishment is broken before one succeeds. Ren and Gui in year and month are avoided. Jiashen month gives the Three Wonders of Wealth, Officer, and Seal; Chen month gives Officer’s Storehouse; Hai month, Officer and Seal. All give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Jiashen:** Minister Tan Lun, died in Dingchou year. **Jihai / Jisi:** Lü Nan, palace first. **Jichou / Wuchen:** a commander of ten thousand. **Xinyou / Renchen:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Yisi / Yiyou:** great distinction. **Jihai / Bingzi:** first in the provincial examination. **Dinghai / Gengxu:** Wang Sunfan, provincial graduate and assistant censor-in-chief; formerly magistrate of Jining, whose clarity and mildness the scholars and people still praise. **Gengchen / Xinsi:** a duke. **Xinchou / Renchen:** “Jia ministry” [office uncertain].〉[^121]

**Dingyou day.** Ordinary and unfavorable to descendants. Hai, Mao, or Wei in year and month gives distinction; Si, You, or Chou is ordinary; Yin, Wu, or Xu gives wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingchou / Renzi:** a prefect. **Jisi / Gengwu:** a deputy court director. **Guiyou / Renxu:** Chen Suozhi, a supervising secretary for justice.〉

**Dinghai day.** Ordinary. In Zi month, Metal-and-Water luck gives a bureau office. Wei, Shen, You, or Chou in year and month is auspicious. 〈One account says:〉 Ruin first, wealth afterward. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Dingwei:** Zhang, palace first. **Xinyou / Xinchou:** a councillor of the left. **Guiwei / Renxu:** Governor-General Chen Qiyu.〉

〈The Horse Transforming into a Qilin configuration:  
Those who meet it must have abundance.  
Without punishment, clash, or breaking,  
Their renown approaches the imperial court.〉

> Wu hour with a Ding day forms Lu Origin;  
> Without Officer Star, it surpasses its fellows.  
> No punishment in the pillars, auspicious luck ahead:  
> A road through azure clouds leads up the crimson sky.

> A Ding day meets Bingwu hour;  
> Exchanged Lu and Horse shine brightly.  
> Such illustrious achievement is rare in the world;  
> Learning and letters promise distinction.  
> No Gui, Zi, or Mao in year and month:  
> When the time comes, literary blessings stand arrayed.  
> Plain cloth is exchanged for brocade on the journey home;  
> The wind bears the cloudward course ten thousand li.

## 44. Ding Days, Dingwei Hour
### 六丁日丁未時斷
<!-- source: L1912-L1961 -->

> A Ding day born at Dingwei hour:  
> Fire rests upon a Wood configuration, receiving living qi.  
> Food and emolument are secure, though ordinary;  
> Only when luck meets Water does it gain its ground.

On a Ding day at Dingwei hour, Fire rests upon a Wood configuration. Ding takes Jia as Seal-Ribbon; Wei is Wood’s Storehouse, the country of Seal. If Wealth Star is absent from the pillars, Wealth luck is not traveled, and year and month communicate with the Hai–Mao–Wei configuration, this support gives secure and settled blessing.

**Dingchou day.** Chou and Wei punish and clash, preventing a good end. Chen and Xu in year and month complete the Four Storehouses and give the highest distinction. Shen or Wei gives third-rank judicial office, modest and untroubled. 〈One account says:〉 Loss of the ancestral establishment and punishment. 〈Recorded case—**Wuchen / Renxu:** Ming Taizu. On examining the dynastic history, Wuchen year, ninth month, eighteenth day is given as Bingzi day, Jichou hour, differing from the transmitted account. The dynastic history should be taken as correct. With Bingzi and Jichou, “brightness comes forth above the earth”: fittingly, the ruler of Great Ming.〉[^122]

〈Further recorded cases—**Bingshen / Bingshen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Wuchen / Gengchen:** immense wealth. **Jimao / Gengwu:** Grand Secretary Cai Guoyong.〉

**Dingmao day.** Development amid punishment. In Yin or Mao month, Seal-Ribbon brings wealth and substance. Chen, Xu, Shen, and Wu are all auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Renshen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiaxu / Wuchen:** Vice Minister Yu Lin. **Renshen / Bingwu:** a bureau secretary. **Yiyou / Renwu:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Guiwei / Dingsi:** a gentleman appointed by wealth qualification, who died violently. **Dinghai / Guimao:** Tao Dalin, palace second and Vice Minister of Personnel.〉[^123]

**Dingsi day.** Arching Lu gives distinction. With Zi in the year, Wu becomes the palace gate being arched, giving great distinction. Void and filling the position are avoided. Wealth, Officer, and Seal in year and month are all auspicious: Wealth brings riches; Officer and Seal distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuzi / Yimao:** Fei Hong, palace first and grand secretary. **Renwu / Wushen:** a censor. **Renzi / Jiyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingmao / Renyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinchou / Dingyou:** immense wealth. **Guihai / Jiwei:** a great merchant, poor in later life. **Dingwei / Dingwei:** a gentleman appointed through family privilege.〉

**Dingwei day.** The Eight Specialists are excessively strong. Parents, wife, and children are injured early; subsistence is ordinary. A monk’s or Daoist’s life is auspicious. The pillars communicating with Metal, Water, and Wood, together with Metal-and-Water luck, give secure subsistence and great distinction. 〈One account says:〉 Late development amid misfortune, and great wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Jichou:** Grand Secretary Yang Yiqing, a renowned minister, without sons. **Xinhai / Xinmao:** Zou Shouyi, metropolitan first, minister, and eminent scholar of the Learning of the Way. **Jiyou / Dingmao:** Tang Wenxian, palace first and vice minister. **Dingwei / Xinhai:** Luo Dahong, a supervising secretary for rites. **Yimao / Jiashen:** Minister Feng Yue. **Dingwei / Dingwei:** one metropolitan graduate and one licentiate. **Renwu / Jiyou:** “Jia ministry” [office uncertain], died early. **Jihai / Dingchou:** a chief minister of a court.〉[^124]

**Dingyou day.** Day Nobility. Birth in Hai, Mao, Wei, or Yin month gives ample subsistence. Yin month with Metal-and-Water luck gives gold and purple and censorial office. 〈One account says:〉 Misfortune and early death. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Jiawu:** a county magistrate. **Renyin / Bingwu:** a county magistrate. **Renxu / Xinhai:** a student admitted through official privilege. **Gengshen / Yiyou:** immense wealth. **Dingwei / Dingwei:** a provincial graduate who died in prison.〉

**Dinghai day.** Mao month forms the triple Seal configuration: distinction and longevity. Shen strengthens Wealth and Officer; Hai strengthens Officer. Both are auspicious. Summer birth gives Ding Fire qi and a scholar’s office, though solitude and poverty are not escaped. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengxu / Jimao:** Minister Geng Yu. **Jimao / Yihai:** a remonstrance official. **Dingchou / Wushen:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈The hour meets the Wood Storehouse configuration;  
Seal-Ribbon welcomes the key that opens it.  
Luck travels Officer’s flourishing ground;  
Blessing and emolument descend from Heaven.〉

> Two Ding meet, arrayed at Wei hour;  
> Through a perilous road, blessing develops in middle life.  
> Auspicious luck brings Noble Persons into combination;  
> Secure subsistence need not be doubted.

> A Ding day meets Dingwei hour;  
> Within it, the storehouse lies deeply buried.  
> Development is difficult in youth: await the time;  
> Chou and Wei clashing bring free passage.  
> Rely neither on parents nor the ranks of geese;  
> Flowers open and fruit is gathered: wife and wealth.  
> When the time comes, life is full and blessing returns;  
> Splendor in later years is cause for delight.[^125]

## 45. Ding Days, Wushen Hour
### 六丁日戊申時斷
<!-- source: L1962-L1999 -->

> A Ding day born at Wushen hour:  
> The Heavenly Origin turns from Lu, and the body is defeated.  
> Without rescue from the month, wealth develops with difficulty;  
> It is merely a life of ordinary subsistence.

On a Ding day at Wushen hour, the body is defeated and turns its back on Lu. Ding takes Ren as Officer, but Wu Earth upon Shen injures and controls it. Ren emerging in the year or month stem makes encountering Officer a calamity. Although flourishing Geng provides Wealth, the body’s own Defeat leaves it unable to control that Wealth. Without communication with a month that strengthens the body, the result is ordinary. Jia and Yin in year and month are welcome; Ren and Zi are avoided.

**Dingchou day.** Refined distinction. Wei or Shen in year and month gives distinction; southeast luck gives great distinction. Si month with northwest luck gives a post among the Six Ministers. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Jimao:** chief minister of Imperial Entertainments. **Gengxu / Wuyin:** a bureau director. **Guisi / Dingsi:** a provincial graduate. **Renchen / Xinhai:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Dingmao day.** In spring, Hurting Officer employs Seal; in autumn, Hurting Officer employs Wealth. Both are auspicious. Summer gives Companions, winter Hurting Officer Meeting Officer: ordinary. The four season-ending months are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinhai / Xinchou:** Minister Ouyang Bijin. **Gengwu / Wuyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Guiwei / Bingchen:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Dingsi day.** If not distinguished, then rich, though punishment and injury are not escaped. Summer birth with northwest luck gives distinction; autumn and winter bring toil and hardship. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Jihai:** Chief Censor Xie Sanzhou. **Jiyou / Wuchen:** a judicial commissioner. **Jisi / Dingchou:** an assistant prefect. **Yiwei / Wuyin:** a provincial graduate. **Yimao / Gengchen:** rich.〉

**Dingwei day.** Birth in Si, Wu, Wei, or Xu makes the body strong and brings manifest distinction. Hai and Mao assembling with Wei, or Zi and Chen assembling with Shen, are also judged distinguished. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Bingxu:** a Hanlin scholar. **Renzi / Renzi:** distinguished. **Guisi / Renchen:** distinguished. **Bingshen / Xinmao:** Hanlin scholar Huang Wenhuan. **Wuwu / Yichou:** Huang Ruliang, Minister of Rites.〉

**Dingyou day.** Birth in Yin, Wu, Xu, Chou, Chen, or Wei month gives the excellence of Hurting Officer with Officer Completely Removed. It is also Day Nobility, indicating success in the examinations. Metal-and-Water luck brings gold at the waist and purple robes. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinhai / Wuxu:** Ming Wuzong. The branches Hai, Xu, You, and Shen are linked without a gap, called the Glory Reaching the Crown configuration. Xinhai and Wushen are also called Heaven’s Gate and Earth’s Axis: the utmost distinction. **Renzi / Bingwu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Bingwu / Yiwei:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Guihai / [Wuwu]:** a provincial graduate. **Xinmao / Jiawu:** a bureau secretary.〉[^126]

**Dinghai day.** Day and hour harm one another, threatening wife and children. Communication with the month’s qi and a strong body give manifest distinction. Wuxu and Dingsi in year and month make Fire and Earth too heavy, indicating eye disease. 〈Recorded cases—**Guihai / Bingchen:** a deputy surveillance commissioner with eye disease in later life. **Jiwei / Xinwei:** a senior provincial administrator. **Renshen / Gengxu:** a censor. **Jiazi / Dingmao:** a county magistrate.〉

〈The configuration of Blessing and Lu Following Skill:  
Misfortune turns instead to good.  
Only when luck travels Metal-and-Water country  
Does the day of developing wealth arrive.〉

> A Ding day at Wushen hour is the proper form;  
> The Heavenly Origin’s strong qi displays literary brilliance.  
> Though serving in office, content with austere simplicity,  
> In auspicious luck the household must at last prosper.[^127]

## 46. Ding Days, Jiyou Hour
### 六丁日己酉時斷
<!-- source: L2000-L2043 -->

> A Ding day born at Jiyou hour:  
> Study Hall meets Noble—a configuration truly rare.  
> Wife and children possess qi, and Food God flourishes;  
> Only without breaking or punishment is its excellence complete.

On a Ding day at Jiyou hour, Ding Fire reaches Birth at You, obtaining both Study Hall and Tianyi Noble Person. Ding takes Ji as Food and Xin as Wealth. At You, visible Ji and hidden Xin have Birth and flourishing, giving graceful literary accomplishment. Mao or Yi bringing clash and breaking prevents distinction.

**Dingchou day.** Chen, Si, Wu, Wei, Shen, or Xu in year and month gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Wuchen:** a Hanlin scholar. **Gengxu / Gengchen:** a junior provincial administrator. **Dingyou / Guimao:** Vice Minister Cai Yichen. **Gengzi / Jichou:** Mr. Yue, honored through his sons; three sons were metropolitan graduates.〉

**Dingmao day.** Hour and day clash, threatening wife and children. Communication with Fire qi is auspicious; Yi and Mao are avoided. If year or month contains Hai, Wei, Si, or Chou, even one such character makes two combinations, so this is not judged as a clash. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Dingwei:** Lord Yang. Three Tianyi Noble Persons and three sha converge upon the hour, giving military strategy beyond others and victory in a hundred battles. **Dingsi / Yisi:** an academician. **Guisi / Xinyou:** a county magistrate.〉[^128]

**Dingsi day.** Si, You, or Chou in year and month gives flourishing Wealth generating Officer: lifelong wealth and distinction. Hai or Zi is also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinsi / Wuxu:** Vice Minister Shi Zonglu. **Guiwei / Guihai:** a junior provincial administrator. **Xinhai / Xinchou:** a provincial graduate. **Yichou / Xinsi:** rich. **Bingyin / Gengzi:** Hanlin scholar Liu Shengzhong.〉

**Dingwei day.** Communication with Fire qi gives distinction. Mao, Yi, or Gui prevents it. 〈Recorded case—**Yiyou / Yiyou:** a chancellor.〉

**Dingyou day.** Punishment, harm, solitude, and misfortune. A month communicating with Wood and Fire is auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Xinwei:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Bingxu / Gengyin:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Yisi / Dinghai:** a provincial graduate. **Xinwei / Bingxu:** deputy chief minister of Imperial Entertainments.〉

**Dinghai day.** Obstruction and delay. Wu, Ji, Bing, or Ding in year and month gives an influential post close to the ruler. Mao, Jia, Yi, or Yin, with northwest luck, gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Renwu:** an official in the Ministry of Rites. **Yichou / Bingxu:** a metropolitan prefect. **Wuzi / Jiazi:** Lü Min, Hanlin compiler. **Wuyin / Dingsi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wushen / Renxu:** Grand Secretary Sun Ruyou. **Gengwu / Wuyin:** Educational Commissioner Ge Yinliang.〉

〈The configuration of Birth in the Autumn Moon:  
Wealth Star shines bright and clear.  
Without punishment, control, or breaking,  
Wealth and distinction do not leave the person.〉

> A Ding day at You hour finally meets distinction;  
> Indirect Wealth and Food meet, and Lu Origin returns.  
> Stems and branches at Birth and flourishing bring good amid ill;  
> Clashing with and breaking Wealth Star conceals a seed of calamity.

> A Ding day approaches Jiyou hour;  
> Food God flourishes and generates Wealth.  
> Untroubled blessing and emolument come of themselves;  
> Throughout life, frankness and good cheer.  
> The gentleman is broad-minded, generous as the sea;  
> The common person’s sympathies embrace the four seas.  
> Wealth and Officer in Double Beauty are arrayed within the image;  
> The whole course flows onward without hindrance.[^129]

## 47. Ding Days, Gengxu Hour
### 六丁日庚戌時斷
<!-- source: L2044-L2089 -->

> A Ding day born at Gengxu hour:  
> Defeat encountered in the Tomb makes blessing hard to form.  
> Without rescue, ghost comes to injure;  
> Wealth will not gather, and the eyes suffer harm.

On a Ding day at Gengxu hour, Defeat is encountered in the Tomb. Ding takes Geng and Xin as Wealth, but Bing within Xu brings Defeat, preventing blessing. Without rescue, repeated Gui Water indicates injury to the eyes.[^130]

**Dingchou day.** Hour and day punish one another, threatening wife and children. Yin, Hai, Shen, or You in year and month gives office to the third rank. Wu, Wei, Zi, or Chen, with Metal-and-Wood luck, also gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Gengyin:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Renshen / Jiachen:** a censor. **Wuyin / Xinyou:** a bureau director. **Dingwei / Renzi:** a provincial graduate. **Jiawu / Jiaxu:** two cases recorded together—Cheng Zhengyi, Wuwu and Xinwei examination years, a county magistrate, died in Bingxu; and Yu Huaizhong, Dingmao and Xinwei examination years, by Guisi year had risen to provincial administration commissioner. **Jichou / Dingchou:** a palace eunuch.〉[^131]

**Dingmao day.** Hai or Wei in year and month completes the triple Seal combination and gives distinction. Zi month gives auspicious Killings and Seal. Established Lu in year and month gives distinction as a monk or Daoist. With no rescue in the pillars, flourishing Gui often brings eye disease. You month gives no distinction. Pure You and Xu in year and month, with Ji and Jia exposed in the stems, gives distinction mixed with misfortune. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiwei / Gengchen:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Yichou / Dinghai:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Xinyou / Gengzi:** a bureau director. **Wushen / Jiazi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingchou / Dingwei:** Chief Censor Gu Zhongxu.〉

**Dingsi day.** Chen or Si month with Metal-and-Water luck gives censorial office. Gui–Zi or Ren–Hai with southern luck gives the highest rank. Pure Wei with northwest luck gives third or fourth rank. A strong body without Wealth-and-Officer luck is ordinary, or pure and lofty as a monk or Daoist. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuxu / Yichou:** a bureau secretary. **Jiashen / Xinwei:** first in the provincial examination. **Wuchen / Renxu:** a bureau secretary. **Wuzi / Xinyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jichou / Dingmao:** Lao Kan, governor of Fujian, banished to frontier service. **Wuchen / Gengshen:** a marquis, drowned.〉

**Dingwei day.** Punishment is indicated. Hai and Mao assembling Seal, or Shen–Zi–Chen assembling Officer, give literary distinction. Wu month gives Established Lu; Zi clashing with it is ominous. If year and month employ Earth to generate Wealth, riches follow; employing Wealth to generate Officer gives both wealth and distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Wushen / Xinyou:** Zhang Zhi, metropolitan first and grand secretary. **Wuxu / Jiazi:** a chancellor. **Jimao / Wuchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinhai / Xinmao:** Chief Censor Chu Shu. **Guihai / Guihai:** Hanlin scholar Li Xueshi. **Dingchou / Jiachen:** a provincial graduate. **Renchen / Renyin:** a provincial graduate. **Jiazi / Yihai:** a commander with seven sons. **Wuchen / Bingchen:** Zhang Dian, who entered the Secretariat from commoner status, rose to vice minister, and secured family privilege for two sons. **Jisi / Wuchen:** rich.〉

**Dingyou day.** Day Nobility: association with the distinguished, knowledge of skilled pursuits, and resourceful planning. You–Xu constitutes one of the Six Harms, leaving blood kin without affection. An autumn month gives fifth- or sixth-rank distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingsi / Renyin:** a vice minister. **Bingchen / Wuxu:** a prefect. **Guimao / Xinyou:** Wang Yinglin, governor of Suzhou. **Dinghai / Gengxu:** Minister Yin Wuqing, as transmitted in the astrological case records.〉[^132]

**Dinghai day.** Day Nobility. Si, You, or Chou in year and month gives fourth- or fifth-rank distinction. Yin, Mao, or Hai with Fire-and-Metal luck gives a post among the Six Ministers. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiyou / Yihai:** a minister. **Dingsi / Guimao:** a vice minister. **Jimao / Gengwu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Guiyou / Jiayin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jichou / Gengwu:** Chief Censor Chen Sheng. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** a prefect. **Renyin / Jiyou:** a bureau secretary. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** a bureau secretary.〉

〈The Fading Flower Meeting Rain configuration:  
Those who meet it cannot find passage.  
Only when luck reaches Wealth-and-Officer ground  
Do they know release from distress and poverty.〉

> A Ding day truly meets the time of Xu;  
> No key to the lock: the storehouse door is closed.  
> Parents and siblings offer little to rely upon;  
> Establishing the household requires one’s own diligence.

> A Ding day meets Gengxu hour;  
> Fire and Metal punish and harm: early death and solitude.  
> Without Chou or Chen there is no key;  
> The storehouse closed, wealth can be gathered and kept.  
> Form a union, depend on the wife, and dwell with her;  
> The Six Relations and blood kin grow sparse.  
> In later years, blessing develops and the household is transformed;  
> This fate is sweet afterward, bitter first.[^133]

## 48. Ding Days, Xinhai Hour
### 六丁日辛亥時斷
<!-- source: L2090-L2141 -->

> A Ding day born at Xinhai hour:  
> Wealth and Officer in Double Beauty, Seal at Birth.  
> Communication with the month’s qi gives the highest distinction;  
> Without it, name and profit are slight.

On a Ding day at Xinhai hour, Wealth and Officer possess Double Beauty. Ding employs Ren as Officer, Xin as Wealth, and Jia as Seal. At Hai, visible Xin is Wealth, hidden Ren Officer, and generated Jia Seal. Communication with Fire qi and support gives great distinction, called Complete Beauty. Without communication, name and profit are adverse and inferior.

**Dingchou day.** Autumn strengthens Wealth, summer the body, spring Seal: manifest distinction. In winter, Officer and Killings are too heavy; a weak body may be unable to sustain their blessing. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Gengxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renxu / Guichou:** an earl. **Gengzi / Wuzi:** Minister Wang Gao. **Bingzi / Dingyou:** Vice Minister Hong Chaoxuan, died in prison, as transmitted in the astrological case records. **Wuzi / Guihai:** He Rujian, metropolitan graduate; his son and grandson also attained the metropolitan degree. **Dingyou / Bingwu:** Pei Yingzhang, Minister of Personnel at the southern capital.〉

**Dingmao day.** The hour has the Three Wonders of Wealth, Officer, and Seal. Seal’s assistance and Wealth’s generation in year and month give great distinction. Pure Zi with Wood-and-Fire luck permits sixth-rank office. Si, You, or Chou month gives Xin Wealth its configuration and strong support: wealth and distinction together. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinwei / Wuxu:** Grand Secretary Chen Yiqin. **Dingmao / Guichou:** Minister Zhao [Bingran]. **Jimao / Guiyou:** Minister Xie Dongzhi. **Yichou / Xinsi:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Gengchen / Renzi:** a vice minister. **Dingchou / Dingwei:** Minister Wang Haowen. **Bingwu / Jiawu:** no name or office is supplied.〉[^134]

**Dingsi day.** Hour and day clash, threatening wife and children. Communication with Fire qi, or autumn birth followed by eastern luck, gives distinction. A Xin month with [Si] and Hai gives loss of the ancestral establishment and manifest refinement. 〈One account says:〉 Turbid. 〈Recorded cases—**Jichou / Bingyin:** Minister Hong Fuzhong, Horse’s Head Bearing a Sword; wealth and emolument beyond measure in middle life, all lost in later years. **Xinhai / Jiawu:** Hu Zhouzi, a supervising secretary for justice.〉[^135]

**Dingwei day.** Hai, Mao, Wei, Yin, Chen, or Wu in year and month, with Direct Seal and Direct Officer exposed in the stems, gives intelligence and manifest distinction. Western luck brings the highest rank. Jichou month gives censorial office. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Jiachen:** Grand Secretary Yin Tangchuan. **Dingyou / Jiyou:** Hanlin scholar Zhang Cheng. **Guisi / Yichou:** Grand Secretary Zhang Wei. **Gengzi / Gengchen:** Vice Minister Lu Xiangsheng, died following military defeat.〉

**Dingyou day.** Noble Person Bearing the Seal gives distinction. Zi month with eastern luck gives censorial office. Si and Chou in the pillars assemble Wealth; Mao and Wei assemble Seal. Wealth, Officer, and Seal exposed in the stems give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinwei / Yiwei:** Grand Secretary Guo Pu; another account gives Guisi month; nine sons. **Jiashen / Dingchou:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Dingchou / Renzi:** Prefect Rao Cai, with five sons; Vice Minister Wan Lai had the same fate and was banished to frontier service. Rao was a provincial graduate, Wan a metropolitan graduate; Rao came from Guizhou, Wan from Jiangxi. **Dinghai / Gengxu:** Chief Censor Yin Wuqing, as transmitted in the original text. **Bingchen / Yiwei:** Minister Liu Yuanlin. **Bingzi / Wuxu:** Minister Cao Sanyang.〉[^136]
